Built on DINOv3 dense features and SAM-style proposals, ZODS-RS chains: PP (prototype purification via Tyler covariance), R-SEM (rotation-scale equivariant matching with separable kernels and global Hungarian assignment), and UAM (uncertainty-aware pixelwise merging with adaptive priors and optional negative prototypes). A lightweight CWLA fuses multiple DINOv3 layers. On FAIR1M (HBB) we obtain $\mathrm{mAP}_{0.50:0.95}=\mathbf{13.06}$ and $\mathrm{AP}_S=\mathbf{2.93}$ \emph{(class-averaged over ship/airplane)}; on xView (HBB) we report $\mathrm{mAP}=\mathbf{16.69}$. On our UAV dataset, ZODS-RS achieves mask $\mathrm{mIoU}=\mathbf{31.10}$ and improves small-object AP by $\mathbf{+30.70}$ over Grounded-SAM on a single 5090. This work offers a unified, \emph{no-training} solution for horizontal-box detection plus instance segmentation in aerial imagery; provides explicit closed-form formulations for PP/R-SEM/UAM tightly coupled with DINOv3; and demonstrates \emph{consistent} gains on small and crowded targets and under cross-domain shifts while keeping deployment simple.

中文摘要翻译: 遥感与无人机应用需要能够在不同平台和视角下泛化且无需任务特定训练的模型。然而，现有无训练管道在处理有向几何、尺度/旋转变化以及拥挤的港口或机场时常常表现不佳，且很少有方法能统一目标检测与分割任务。本文提出ZODS-RS，一种无需训练、闭式形式的管道，能够同时输出水平边界框（HBB）和实例掩码。基于DINOv3密集特征和SAM风格提案，ZODS-RS链式集成了三个模块：PP（基于Tyler协方差的原型纯化）、R-SEM（使用可分离核与全局匈牙利分配的旋转-尺度等变匹配）以及UAM（具有自适应先验和可选负原型的不确定性感知像素级合并）。一个轻量级的CWLA模块用于融合多个DINOv3层特征。在FAIR1M（HBB）上，我们获得了mAP₀.₅₀:₀.₉₅=13.06和AP_S=2.93（船/飞机类平均）；在xView（HBB）上，mAP=16.69。在我们自建的UAV数据集上，ZODS-RS的掩码mIoU达到31.10，并在单张5090显卡上将小目标AP相比Grounded-SAM提升了+30.70。本工作为航空图像中的水平框检测与实例分割提供了一种统一的、无需训练的解决方案；给出了与DINOv3紧密耦合的PP/R-SEM/UAM的显式闭式公式；在小目标与拥挤目标场景以及跨域迁移下均展示出一致的性能提升，同时保持部署的简单性。

</details>


### [61] [Spatially Selective Self-Training for Unsupervised Building Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10775)
*Wafaa I. M. Hussin,Zhi Lu,Anas M. I. Mohammed,Xiang Zhou,Ratiba A. H. Abubaker,Zhenming Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SST-CD，一种空间选择性自训练框架，将无标签建筑物变化检测转化为在噪声伪监督下的端到端检测器学习，通过局部一致性准则筛选可靠像素进行训练，在多个数据集上取得优于现有无监督和免标签方法的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无标签建筑物变化检测方法通常遵循“差异到掩码”范式，直接使用时序差异、冻结基础模型响应、提示输出或后处理结果作为最终变化图，未能学习任务特定的变化检测器，且难以克服通用时序差异与建筑物结构变化之间的差距——外观偏移、配准误差和非建筑变化会产生强烈但误导性的响应。

Method: SST-CD包含三个核心组件：(1) 将时序差异作为候选伪标签，仅对空间可靠像素进行训练，可靠性由局部一致性准则评估；(2) 轻量级特征适配器重新校准双时相特征以稳定噪声自训练；(3) 基于原型的解码器生成紧凑的变化和无变化特征表示。

Result: 在LEVIR-CD、WHU-CD和DSIFN-CD三个数据集上，SST-CD分别达到83.08%、91.69%和86.60%的F1分数，优于现有的无监督和免标签基线方法。

Conclusion: SST-CD通过空间选择性自训练框架，有效解决了无标签建筑物变化检测中伪标签噪声问题，实现了端到端检测器学习，显著提升了检测性能。

Original Abstract: Unsupervised building change detection aims to learn building-change masks from unlabeled bi-temporal remote sensing images. Existing label-free methods often follow a discrepancy-to-mask paradigm, directly using temporal differences, frozen foundation-model responses, prompt-based outputs, or post-processing results as final change maps. Although these strategies provide annotation-free cues, they do not learn a task-specific building-change detector and remain vulnerable to the gap between generic temporal discrepancies and building-defined structural changes. In practice, such discrepancies are often noisy and task-irrelevant, as appearance shifts, registration errors, and non-building modifications can produce strong but misleading responses. To address this problem, we propose SST-CD, a spatially selective self-training framework that reformulates fully label-free building change detection as end-to-end detector learning under noisy pseudo supervision.
  SST-CD uses temporal discrepancies as candidate pseudo labels and trains the detector only on spatially reliable pixels, whose reliability is estimated by a local consistency criterion that filters inconsistent regions from supervision. To further stabilize noisy self-training, a lightweight feature adapter recalibrates bi-temporal features, while a prototype-based decoder produces compact change and no-change representations. Experiments on LEVIR-CD, WHU-CD, and DSIFN-CD show that SST-CD achieves F1 scores of 83.08\%, 91.69\%, and 86.60\%, respectively, outperforming existing unsupervised and label-free baselines. Code will be made publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 无监督建筑物变化检测旨在从未标记的双时相遥感图像中学习建筑物变化掩码。现有的免标签方法通常遵循"差异到掩码"范式，直接使用时序差异、冻结的基础模型响应、基于提示的输出或后处理结果作为最终变化图。尽管这些策略提供了无标注的线索，但它们并未学习任务特定的建筑物变化检测器，且仍易受通用时序差异与建筑物定义的结构变化之间差距的影响。在实践中，这种差异往往充满噪声且与任务无关，因为外观偏移、配准误差和非建筑修改可能产生强烈但具有误导性的响应。为解决这一问题，我们提出SST-CD，一种空间选择性自训练框架，将完全免标签的建筑物变化检测重新定义为在噪声伪监督下的端到端检测器学习。SST-CD使用时序差异作为候选伪标签，仅对空间可靠像素训练检测器，这些像素的可靠性由局部一致性准则评估，该准则从监督中过滤不一致区域。为进一步稳定噪声自训练，一个轻量级特征适配器重新校准双时相特征，而基于原型的解码器生成紧凑的变化和无变化表示。在LEVIR-CD、WHU-CD和DSIFN-CD上的实验表明，SST-CD分别达到83.08%、91.69%和86.60%的F1分数，优于现有的无监督和免标签基线方法。代码将公开发布。

</details>


### [62] [From Patches to Patients: A study of the tile-to-slide performance transferability in Digital Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10778)
*Sofiène Boutaj,Leo Fillioux,Maria Vakalopoulou,Stergios Christodoulidis,Pierre Marza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在组织病理学全切片图像分析中，是否可以使用高效的图块级线性探测作为滑动级性能的可靠代理，从而避免对每个候选基础模型运行完整的滑动级流水线。通过对19个基础模型在42个滑动级和16个图块级任务上的基准测试，发现图块级与滑动级性能高度相关，表明编码器表示质量是影响WSI性能的主要因素。图块级基准测试可作为高效初筛手段，但滑动级评估对最终临床验证仍不可或缺。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前在组织病理学中选择最优基础模型需要为每个模型进行预处理、计算昂贵的特征提取和多实例学习聚合器训练，流程繁琐且计算开销大。本文旨在探索是否可以用更高效的图块级线性探测来替代完整的滑动级流水线，以降低模型选择的成本。

Method: 对19个最先进的基础模型在42个滑动级任务和16个图块级任务上进行基准测试，比较图块级线性探测指标与滑动级结果（使用ABMIL和均值池化聚合）之间的相关性，并通过敏感性分析评估模型可迁移性的稳定性。

Result: 图块级与滑动级性能之间存在高相关性；编码器表示质量是WSI成功的主要决定因素；模型可迁移性在不同模型间稳定，受队列大小和每张切片的图块数影响大于受平均任务难度影响；图块级基准测试能可靠地筛选出强候选模型。

Conclusion: 图块级基准测试为缩小候选模型范围提供了一种高效实用的初步手段，但滑动级评估对于临床任务的最终验证仍然至关重要。

Original Abstract: Foundation Models (FMs) have recently redefined the state-of-the-art in histopathology by providing robust representations for whole-slide image (WSI) analysis. However, selecting the optimal foundation model (FM) for a specific clinical cohort currently requires multiple preprocessing steps, followed by computationally expensive feature extraction and the training of a Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) aggregator for every model. In this work, we investigate whether efficient tile-level linear probing can serve as a reliable proxy for slide-level performance, reducing the need to run full slide-level pipelines for every candidate encoder. We benchmark 19 state-of-the-art FMs on 42 slide-level and 16 tile-level tasks, comparing tile probing metrics against slide-level outcomes using ABMIL and Mean Pooling aggregations. We observe a high correlation between tile and slide performance across varying task difficulties, indicating that encoder representation quality is the primary determinant of WSI success. Sensitivity analyses show that transferability is stable across models and is more influenced by cohort sizes and numbers of tiles per slide than by average task difficulty. We also measure the agreement in best performing models between tile and slide-level tasks, showing tile benchmarks reliably shortlist strong candidates. Overall, our study indicates that tile-level benchmarking provides an efficient and practical first step for narrowing down candidate models, while slide-level evaluation remains essential for final validation on clinical tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 基础模型（FMs）近期通过为全切片图像（WSI）分析提供鲁棒的表示，重新定义了组织病理学中的最先进技术。然而，为特定临床队列选择最优的基础模型目前需要对每个模型进行多个预处理步骤，随后进行计算昂贵的特征提取并训练多实例学习（MIL）聚合器。在本工作中，我们研究了高效的图块级线性探测是否可以作为滑动级性能的可靠代理，从而减少为每个候选编码器运行完整滑动级流水线的需求。我们在42个滑动级和16个图块级任务上对19个最先进的基础模型进行了基准测试，使用ABMIL和均值池化聚合比较了图块探测指标与滑动级结果。我们观察到在不同任务难度下，图块级与滑动级性能之间存在高相关性，表明编码器表示质量是WSI成功的主要决定因素。敏感性分析显示，模型的可迁移性在不同模型间是稳定的，并且受队列大小和每张切片的图块数影响大于受平均任务难度影响。我们还测量了滑动级与图块级任务中最佳模型的一致性，结果表明图块级基准测试能够可靠地筛选出强候选模型。总体而言，本研究表明图块级基准测试为缩小候选模型范围提供了一种高效实用的初步步骤，而滑动级评估对于临床任务的最终验证仍然至关重要。

</details>


### [63] [A Multimodal RGB and Events Dataset for Hand Detection in First-Person View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10790)
*Bharghav Kota,Yulia Sandamirskaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从现有RGB手部数据集(Egohands)合成事件相机手部数据集的方法，解决了事件相机在物体检测中训练数据不足的问题，并在多模态手部检测任务上取得了与最先进方法相当的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统手部检测算法受限于相机帧率，在移动机器人系统中运动模糊严重，尤其在暗光条件下。事件相机具有高动态范围、高时间分辨率和低功耗等优势，但事件相机在目标检测任务中的主要瓶颈是缺乏足够的训练数据。

Method: 利用v2e工具箱从现有的RGB Egohands数据集合成事件相机手部数据集，通过变化v2e参数提供不同光照条件和尺度的版本；使用在Egohands RGB图像上微调的YOLOv8模型生成真实标注，并通过插值将其映射到高时间分辨率的事件数据上；最后使用现有的多模态（事件相机+RGB相机）目标检测算法进行手部检测。

Result: 使用多模态数据集和现有的事件+RGB相机目标检测算法进行手部检测，展示了与最先进方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 提出的合成事件相机手部数据集方法可以有效解决事件相机训练数据不足的问题，并在多模态手部检测任务中达到与现有最优方法可比的性能。

Original Abstract: Existing hand detection algorithms work on images and the detection rate is restricted by the frame rate of the camera. In hand detection applications for moving robotic systems, conventional cameras cause motion blur, especially in darker lighting conditions. We can leverage the use of event-based cameras which possess a high dynamic range, high temporal resolution, and low power consumption. Recent work has shown that using a stereo setup of an event-based and a frame-based camera improves detection accuracy and the bandwidth-latency tradeoff. The main bottleneck in using event-based cameras in object detection and recognition tasks is a relatively low amount of training data. In this work, we propose a methodology and an exemplary synthetic event-based hand dataset from an egocentric, first-person view perspective. The data is synthesized from the existing RGB Egohands dataset with the v2e toolbox. Parameters of the v2e toolbox are varied to provide versions of the dataset with different lighting conditions and scales. Ground truth detections are generated with a fine-tuned YOLOv8 model which is applied to the RGB images in the Egohands dataset and interpolated on the high-temporal resolution events. We use the multi-modal dataset to perform hand detection with existing object detection algorithms which use a multi-modal setup of event and RGB cameras and demonstrate performance comparable to the state-of-the-art.

中文摘要翻译: 现有手部检测算法基于图像工作，其检测率受限于相机的帧率。在移动机器人系统的手部检测应用中，传统相机会产生运动模糊，尤其在较暗的光照条件下。我们可以利用事件相机，其具有高动态范围、高时间分辨率和低功耗的特点。近期研究表明，使用事件相机和帧相机的立体设置可以提高检测精度并改善带宽-延迟权衡。事件相机在目标检测和识别任务中使用的主要瓶颈是训练数据相对较少。在本工作中，我们提出了一种方法论以及一个从自我中心、第一人称视角合成的示例性事件相机手部数据集。该数据通过v2e工具箱从现有的RGB Egohands数据集合成得到。通过变化v2e工具箱的参数，我们提供了不同光照条件和尺度下的数据集版本。真实标注检测结果通过一个微调的YOLOv8模型生成，该模型应用于Egohands数据集中的RGB图像，并通过插值映射到高时间分辨率的事件数据上。我们利用该多模态数据集，结合现有的事件相机与RGB相机多模态目标检测算法进行手部检测，并展示了与最先进方法相当的性能。

</details>


### [64] [SCAIL-2: Unifying Controlled Character Animation with End-to-end In-Context Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10804)
*Wenhao Yan,Fengjia Guo,Zhuoyi Yang,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCAIL-2提出了一种端到端的角色动画框架，通过直接拼接驱动视频与序列来避免中间表示的信息损失，并利用解耦条件统一子任务、构建MotionPair-60K数据集、采用上下文掩码条件与模式特定RoPE以及Bias-Aware DPO偏好优化，在多种角色动画任务上显著超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有受控角色动画方法严重依赖中间表示（如姿态骨架或掩码背景），这些中间表示不可避免地导致信息损失。作者希望绕过这些中间表示，实现真正端到端的角色动画，从而保留完整的视觉信息。

Method: 1) 直接将驱动视频与参考序列拼接作为输入，实现端到端学习；2) 提出解耦条件统一框架，将角色动画的子任务统一处理；3) 构建MotionPair-60K端到端运动迁移数据集；4) 采用上下文掩码条件（in-context mask conditioning）和模式特定旋转位置编码（mode-specific RoPE）作为软引导；5) 提出Bias-Aware DPO偏好优化方法，在细节区域构建偏好项以缓解合成差异。

Result: 在多种角色动画任务中，该方法显著优于现有的最先进方法。

Conclusion: SCAIL-2通过绕过中间表示实现了端到端角色动画，统一了异质子任务，并通过合成数据和偏好优化有效缓解了细节区域的合成差异问题，展示了强大的泛化性能。

Original Abstract: Controlled character animation requires transferring motion from a driving sequence to a reference character. Prior works heavily rely on intermediate representations, including pose skeletons to represent motion or masked background to represent environment, which inevitably leads to information loss. To address this, we present SCAIL-2, an framework that bypasses those intermediates and achieves \textbf{end-to-end} character animation. By directly concatenating driving videos to the sequence, the model can obtain all the required visual information from the input video. To address lack of end-to-end data, we unify sub-tasks of character animation with decoupled conditions and then curate a pipeline to synthesize MotionPair-60K, an end-to-end motion transfer dataset containing heterogeneous tasks of character animation. To archive the unification, we utilize in-context mask conditioning and mode-specific RoPE as soft guidance beyond textual instructions and raw visual information. To address synthetic discrepancy in detailed regions, we propose Bias-Aware DPO to construct preference items to mitigate the errors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method substantially outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches in various character animation tasks. A large subset of synthetic data as well as model weights will be released at our project page: https://teal024.github.io/SCAIL-2/.

中文摘要翻译: 受控角色动画需要将驱动序列的运动迁移到参考角色上。现有方法严重依赖中间表示，包括用姿态骨架表示运动或用掩码背景表示环境，这不可避免地导致信息丢失。为解决这一问题，我们提出了SCAIL-2，一个绕过这些中间表示并实现端到端角色动画的框架。通过将驱动视频直接拼接到序列中，模型可以从输入视频中获得所有所需的视觉信息。为解决端到端数据缺乏的问题，我们用解耦条件统一了角色动画的子任务，并设计了一条数据合成管线，构建了MotionPair-60K——一个包含角色动画异质子任务的端到端运动迁移数据集。为了实现统一，我们利用上下文掩码条件和模式特定的旋转位置编码，作为文本指令和原始视觉信息之外的软引导。为解决细节区域中的合成差异问题，我们提出了Bias-Aware DPO来构建偏好项以缓解误差。大量实验表明，我们的方法在多种角色动画任务中显著优于现有最先进方法。大量合成数据子集以及模型权重将在项目页面发布：https://teal024.github.io/SCAIL-2/。

</details>


### [65] [Deep learning for echo sounder data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10811)
*Ketil Malde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出深度学习在水声学数据分析中表现平平，原因在于直接套用图像处理方法而忽视了声学数据的固有特性，并强调缺乏标准化数据集和性能基准是领域发展的主要障碍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管深度学习在图像和文本领域取得了革命性进展，但在水下声学（如echograms）数据处理中效果有限，作者认为这是由于声学数据的固有特性被忽视，且领域内缺乏标准化数据格式和高质量公开数据集。

Method: 本文为观点性论文，未提出具体方法，而是通过分析声学数据与图像数据的本质差异，呼吁发展针对声学数据特性的专用深度学习方法，并建立标准化数据组织和性能基准。

Result: 无实验性结果。主要分析结论是：现有深度学习在水声学中的应用效果不佳，根源在于直接复用图像处理模型，而声学数据的固有特性需要专门的方法研发。

Conclusion: 要推动深度学习在水声学领域的突破，必须解决两个核心问题：一是建立标准化的数据格式和组织方式，二是构建高质量、易获取的公开数据集并设定明确的性能目标。

Original Abstract: There is no doubt that over the last decade, techniques from the field of machine learning have revolutionized how we process and interpret data, especially images and text. For underwater observations acoustics is a primary source of information, and naturally, deep learning methods have been applied to echograms and other acoustics data, but so far with rather modest results. Here, we argue that due to intrinsic properties of acoustic data, substantial advances will likely require research into deep learning methods beyond mere recycling of models and techniques from image processing. Currently, the potential for breakthroughs in method development is hindered by the lack of standard data formats and organization, and even more by the lack of readily available, high quality data sets with established performance goals. To advance the field, these shortcomings should be remedied

中文摘要翻译: 毫无疑问，过去十年间，机器学习领域的技术已经彻底改变了我们处理和解释数据的方式，尤其是图像和文本数据。在水下观测中，声学是信息的主要来源，因此深度学习方法自然被应用于echograms及其他声学数据，但迄今为止效果相当有限。本文认为，由于声学数据的固有特性，要想取得实质性进展，就需要研究超越简单复用图像处理模型和技术的深度学习方法。当前，方法研发突破的潜力因缺乏标准化数据格式和组织方式而受到阻碍，更因为缺乏易于获取、高质量且具有既定性能目标的数据集而受限。为了推动该领域发展，这些不足应予以纠正。

</details>


### [66] [Earth-OneVision: Extending Remote Sensing Multimodal Large Language Models to More Sensor Modalities and Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10819)
*Miaoxin Cai,Guanqun Wang,Wei Zhang,Guangyao Zhou,Yin Zhuang,Tong Zhang,Hao Wang,He Chen,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Earth-OneVision是一个2B参数的遥感多模态大语言模型，统一了6种传感器模态和9类任务，通过三个专用机制（FGVLA、SLIS、PCMA）解决瓶颈，在多个基准上达到SOTA或竞争性结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RS-MLLM仅支持窄范围的传感器类型和任务，导致对地球的碎片化理解，且跨模态地球科学知识未被充分利用。

Method: 1) 全粒度视觉-语言对齐(FGVLA)：对齐多层级视觉特征与多维语言空间；2) 空间-语言同构序列化(SLIS)：将异构空间输出统一为自回归令牌；3) 渐进式跨模态适应(PCMA)：将复合域差距分解为视角差距和成像物理差距的顺序阶段。同时构建了包含~34M QA对的MMRS-OneVision数据集。

Result: 在光学视觉定位OPT-RSVG上达87.52% P@0.5，SAR VQA基准SARLANG-Bench上达80.68%（超越7B模型7%以上），多光谱分类BigEarthNet-MS上达75.74%召回率，跨模态推理EarthMind-Bench上达81.94% MCQ准确率。

Conclusion: 仅2B参数的Earth-OneVision在广泛基准上达到与4B-72B RS-MLLM相当或更优的性能，验证了统一多模态框架的有效性。

Original Abstract: RS-MLLMs enable natural-language understanding and spatial reasoning over earth observation imagery. However, existing models support only a narrow range of sensor types and tasks, yielding a fragmented view of the earth and leaving cross-modal geoscientific knowledge largely unexploited. This work presents Earth-OneVision, a 2B RS-MLLM that unifies six sensor modalities (i.e., optical, SAR, infrared, multispectral, temporal, and video) and cross-sensor fusion across 9 task categories within a single autoregressive framework. Three dedicated mechanisms address three bottlenecks. Full-Granularity Vision-Language Alignment (FGVLA) aligns multi-level visual features with the multi-dimensional language space. Spatial-Linguistic Isomorphic Serialization (SLIS) unifies heterogeneous spatial outputs as autoregressive tokens. Progressive Cross-Modality Adaptation (PCMA) decomposes the compound domain gap into sequential stages, tackling the viewpoint and imaging physics gaps in turn. To support joint training, MMRS-OneVision is constructed with ~34M QA pairs spanning all six sensor modalities and cross-sensor fusion across 9 task categories, substantially exceeding existing RS multimodal instruction datasets. With only 2B parameters, Earth-OneVision achieves competitive or state-of-the-art results across extensive benchmarks, consistently matching or outperforming 4B-72B RS-MLLMs. It achieves 87.52% P@0.5 on the OPT-RSVG testset for optical visual grounding and 80.68% on the SAR VQA benchmark SARLANG-Bench, exceeding 7B models by over 7%. It further achieves 75.74% recall on the BigEarthNet-MS testset for multispectral classification, and 81.94% MCQ accuracy on EarthMind-Bench for cross-modality reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: RS-MLLMs实现了对地球观测影像的自然语言理解和空间推理。然而，现有模型仅支持狭窄范围的传感器类型和任务，导致对地球的碎片化理解，且跨模态地球科学知识在很大程度上未被利用。本文提出Earth-OneVision，一个2B参数的RS-MLLM，在单一自回归框架内统一了六种传感器模态（即光学、SAR、红外、多光谱、时序和视频）以及跨传感器融合，覆盖9类任务。三个专用机制解决了三个瓶颈。全粒度视觉-语言对齐（FGVLA）将多层级视觉特征与多维语言空间对齐。空间-语言同构序列化（SLIS）将异构空间输出统一为自回归令牌。渐进式跨模态适应（PCMA）将复合域差距分解为顺序阶段，依次处理视角差距和成像物理差距。为支持联合训练，构建了MMRS-OneVision数据集，包含约340万QA对，涵盖全部六种传感器模态及跨传感器融合的9类任务，大幅超越现有遥感多模态指令数据集。仅凭2B参数，Earth-OneVision在广泛基准上取得了具有竞争力或最先进的结果，持续达到或超越4B-72B的RS-MLLM。其在光学视觉定位数据集OPT-RSVG上达到87.52%的P@0.5，在SAR VQA基准SARLANG-Bench上达到80.68%，超越7B模型7%以上。进一步地，在多光谱分类数据集BigEarthNet-MS上达到75.74%的召回率，在跨模态推理基准EarthMind-Bench上达到81.94%的MCQ准确率。

</details>


### [67] [HarmoView: Harmonizing Multi-View Constraints for Identity-Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10839)
*Cong Wang,Zhentao Yu,Hongmei Wang,Weicong Liang,Zixiang Zhou,Zilin Yang,Jiarong Ou,Rui Chen,Yuan Zhou,Qinglin Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HarmoView 是一个用于身份一致性视频生成的鲁棒框架，通过多级特征注入、可学习代理令牌、Jump-RoPE 和渐进式视角课程训练，结合大规模多视角数据集，显著提升了大视角变化下的人脸身份保真度，达到 SOTA 性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的身份一致性视频生成方法在大视角变化下难以保持外观保真度；引入多视角参考输入虽有潜力，但缺乏有效的多视角输入处理框架且多视角数据稀缺，亟需解决这些问题。

Method: 提出 HarmoView 框架，包含三项架构改进和分阶段训练策略：(1) 多级特征注入 (MFI)，将正面参考的原始 ViT 特征与文本令牌通过交叉注意力注入，提供底层外观锚点；(2) 可学习代理令牌，统一单/多视角设置下的异构参考布局并解决参考视角不匹配问题；(3) Jump-RoPE，实现身份级特征隔离以减少身份串扰。同时提出渐进式视角课程 (Progressive View Curriculum) 的四阶段训练策略，采用视角丢弃机制实现从 T2V 生成到高保真身份一致空间推理的稳定过渡。此外构建了大规模多视角数据集。

Result: 在包含 100 个手动筛选案例、涵盖 52 个不同身份的多视角基准测试上，HarmoView 显著优于开源基线模型，并匹敌领先的闭源引擎，在身份一致性视频生成任务上达到 SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过架构创新（多级特征注入、代理令牌、Jump-RoPE）与渐进式训练课程相结合，HarmoView 有效解决了多视角身份一致性视频生成中的保真度问题，且大规模数据集进一步缓解了数据稀缺挑战。

Original Abstract: Current identity-consistent video generation methods struggle to preserve appearance fidelity under large viewpoint changes. While introducing multi-view reference input offers a natural solution, progress remains constrained by the lack of effective frameworks for multi-view inputs and the scarcity of multi-view data. We address these challenges by proposing HarmoView, a robust framework for identity-consistent video generation that effectively integrates multi-view cues through three architectural refinements complemented by a staged training curriculum. Specifically, we first introduce Multi-level Feature Injection to anchor identity fidelity; by injecting raw ViT features from frontal references alongside text tokens via cross-attention, MFI provides persistent low-level appearance anchors that complement the high-level identity features within DiT blocks, leading to enhanced identity preservation. Then, we employ learnable proxy tokens to unify heterogeneous reference layouts across single-/multi-view settings while simultaneously resolving the reference-view mismatch problem. Jump-RoPE is further developed for identity-wise feature isolation to reduce identity crosstalk. To activate these structural capabilities while preserving the original generative priors, we propose the Progressive View Curriculum. This four-stage training strategy employs view dropout to facilitate a stable transition from vanilla T2V generation to high-fidelity, identity-persistent spatial reasoning. Furthermore, we construct a large-scale multi-view dataset to address the issue of data scarcity. Extensive evaluation on our multi-view benchmark, comprising 100 manually-curated cases spanning 52 unique identities, demonstrates that HarmoView significantly outperforms open-source baselines and matches leading closed-source engines, achieving state-of-the-art performance in identity-consistent video generation.

中文摘要翻译: 现有身份一致性视频生成方法在大视角变化下难以保持外观保真度。虽然引入多视角参考输入提供了自然的解决方案，但由于缺乏有效的多视角输入处理框架以及多视角数据的稀缺性，进展仍受到制约。针对这些挑战，我们提出了 HarmoView——一个鲁棒的身份一致性视频生成框架，通过三项架构改进与分阶段训练课程相结合，有效整合多视角线索。具体而言，我们首先引入多级特征注入（Multi-level Feature Injection, MFI）来锚定身份保真度：通过将正面参考的原始 ViT 特征与文本令牌一起经由交叉注意力注入，MFI 提供了持续的底层外观锚点，补充了 DiT 块内的高层身份特征，从而增强身份保持。其次，我们采用可学习代理令牌（learnable proxy tokens）来统一单/多视角设置下的异构参考布局，同时解决参考视角不匹配问题。进一步地，我们提出了 Jump-RoPE 用于身份级特征隔离，以减少身份串扰。为了在保持原始生成先验的同时激活这些结构能力，我们提出了渐进式视角课程（Progressive View Curriculum）。这一四阶段训练策略采用视角丢弃机制，促进从普通 T2V 生成到高保真、身份一致的空间推理的稳定过渡。此外，我们构建了一个大规模多视角数据集以解决数据稀缺问题。在我们构建的多视角基准测试上的广泛评估表明，该基准包含 100 个手动筛选案例，涵盖 52 个不同身份，HarmoView 显著优于开源基线模型，并匹敌领先的闭源引擎，在身份一致性视频生成任务上达到了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [68] [LIBERO-Occ: Evaluating and Improving Vision-Language-Action Models under Scene-Induced Occlusion via Viewpoint Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10862)
*Taishan Li,Jiwen Zhang,Siyuan Wang,Xuanjing Huang,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LIBERO-Occ遮挡基准和视角想象（VIM）方法，通过生成互补视角提升VLA模型在遮挡场景下的操作鲁棒性，无需额外摄像头。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLA模型假设任务相关物体完全可见，但真实场景中遮挡导致部分可观测，模型性能严重下降，亟需解决场景诱导遮挡问题。

Method: 提出视角想象（VIM），从被遮挡的主视角观测生成互补视角，将观测与想象证据共同用于动作预测，无需部署额外摄像头。

Result: SOTA VLA模型在遮挡下性能大幅下降；VIM跨任务套件、遮挡类型和严重程度均提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 视角想象是一种有前景的感知补全机制，可在部分可观测操作中提升VLA模型的鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong performance on standard manipulation benchmarks, but most evaluations assume that task-relevant objects are fully visible. This assumption often fails in realistic settings, where occlusion makes manipulation partially observable. In this paper, we study \textit{scene-induced occlusion} as a fundamental challenge for VLA models and introduce \textbf{LIBERO-Occ}, an occlusion-oriented extension of LIBERO. Experiments show that state-of-the-art VLAs suffer substantial performance degradation under occlusion. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{Viewpoint Imagination (VIM)}, which generates a complementary view from an occluded primary observation and conditions action prediction on both observed and imagined evidence. VIM improves robustness across task suites, occlusion types, and severity levels without requiring additional cameras at deployment time, suggesting that viewpoint imagination is an promising mechanism for perception completion in partially observable manipulation. Our benchmark and corresponding code are available at: \href{https://github.com/litsh/Libero-Occ}{https://github.com/litsh/Libero-Occ}.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在标准操作基准上表现出色，但大多数评估假设任务相关物体完全可见。这一假设在现实场景中往往不成立，因为遮挡使得操作变为部分可观测。本文研究场景诱导遮挡作为VLA模型的基本挑战，并引入LIBERO-Occ，这是LIBERO的遮挡扩展版本。实验表明，最先进的VLA模型在遮挡下性能大幅下降。为解决此问题，我们提出视角想象（VIM），该方法从被遮挡的主视角观测生成互补视角，并基于观测和想象证据共同进行动作预测。VIM在任务套件、遮挡类型和严重程度上均提升了鲁棒性，且无需在部署时增加额外摄像头，表明视角想象是部分可观测操作中一种有前景的感知补全机制。我们的基准测试及相应代码已开源：https://github.com/litsh/Libero-Occ。

</details>


### [69] [Schmidt Decomposition-Based Methods for Efficient Quantum Image Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10874)
*Ana-Maria Pangeva,Yassine Ferhi,Alexander Geng,Andreas Weinmann,Desislava Ivanova,Ali Moghiseh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过施密特分解的低秩状态近似方法，用于减少量子图像编码（FRQI、QPIE、NEQR）在NISQ设备上的电路复杂度，在保持图像质量的同时实现了显著的资源效率提升，其中FRQI模型电路深度降低97%，MSE仅约0.27。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在NISQ设备上，现有的量子图像编码方法（FRQI、QPIE、NEQR）会导致电路门数多、深度大、量子比特使用量高，限制了实际应用。因此需要一种方法来降低这种复杂度，使量子图像处理能在近期的量子硬件上可行。

Method: 利用施密特分解（Schmidt decomposition）对量子态进行低秩近似，保留量子态纠缠结构中最显著的部分，从而在提高态制备效率的同时保留大部分图像信息。

Result: 比较了三种编码技术在原始形式与低秩近似下的电路深度、CNOT数量、MSE和重构图像视觉质量。FRQI模型实现了电路深度降低97%，同时重构MSE仅约0.27，保持了近乎完美的重建质量。

Conclusion: 低秩近似技术能够有效降低量子图像编码的电路复杂度，在精度与资源效率之间取得了有意义的权衡，为在近期的量子硬件上推进实用的量子图像处理展现了潜力。

Original Abstract: In quantum image processing, a fundamental step is encoding classical image data into quantum states. This can be achieved using methods such as Flexible Representation of Quantum Images (FRQI), Quantum Probability Image Encoding (QPIE), and Novel Enhanced Quantum Representation (NEQR). However, on real quantum hardware, these encodings can quickly lead to circuits with many gates, large circuit depth, and high qubit usage, which is a problem for Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) devices. In this work, we investigate whether low-rank state approximation, formulated via Schmidt decomposition, can help reduce this complexity. The method keeps only the most significant parts of a quantum state's entanglement structure, making state preparation more efficient while preserving most of the image information. We compare the three encoding techniques in their original form and with low-rank approximation, evaluating metrics such as circuit depth, CNOT count, MSE, and visual quality of reconstructed images. The results reveal meaningful trade-offs between accuracy and resource efficiency, with the FRQI model achieving a 97 percent reduction in circuit depth while maintaining a near-perfect reconstruction (MSE of about 0.27). This demonstrates the potential of low-rank techniques for advancing practical quantum image processing on near-term hardware.

中文摘要翻译: 在量子图像处理中，一个基本步骤是将经典图像数据编码为量子态。这可以通过诸如量子图像的灵活表示（FRQI）、量子概率图像编码（QPIE）和新型增强量子表示（NEQR）等方法来实现。然而，在真实的量子硬件上，这些编码方式会迅速导致电路包含大量门、电路深度大以及量子比特使用量高，这对于含噪中等规模量子（NISQ）设备而言是一个问题。本文研究了通过施密特分解构建的低秩状态近似是否有助于降低这种复杂度。该方法仅保留量子态纠缠结构中最显著的部分，使得态制备更加高效，同时保留大部分图像信息。我们比较了三种编码技术在原始形式及低秩近似下的表现，评估了电路深度、CNOT数量、均方误差（MSE）以及重构图像的视觉质量等指标。结果揭示了精度与资源效率之间有意义的权衡，其中FRQI模型在保持近乎完美重建（MSE约为0.27）的同时，电路深度降低了97%。这证明了低秩技术在近期的量子硬件上推进实用量子图像处理的潜力。

</details>


### [70] [Advancing Wood Identification in the Philippines: Utilizing the Xylorix Platform for Efficient AI Model Development and Deployment for Five Key Species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10876)
*Rosalie C. Mendoza,Vivian C. Daracan,Arlene D. Romano,Ronniel D. Manalo,Xin Jie Tang,Yi Hong Wong,Yong Haur Tay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了木材科学家无需编程知识即可利用Xylorix平台构建AI木材宏观识别模型的能力。针对五种菲律宾硬木，基于10,663张截面图像训练二分类器，AUC达0.969-1.000，四/五种达到AA级，证明非程序员也能构建适合现场部署的可靠模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 菲律宾非法采伐和木材贸易问题严峻，准确的木材物种识别对执法至关重要，但传统方法依赖专业设备和专家知识，限制了实际应用。因此需要探索无需编程专长的AI方案以降低技术门槛。

Method: 使用Xylorix平台，由无编程经验的木材科学家对五种菲律宾硬木（Mangium、Rain Tree、Banuyo、Tindalo、Ipil）的10,663张已验证横截面图像训练二分类器。采用样本级平均评分进行评估，以模拟实地操作条件。

Result: AUC范围0.969（Ipil）到1.000（Mangium），AP范围0.589（Samanea）到1.000（Mangium）。四种物种达到AA级（AUC和AP均≥0.90）；Rain Tree因正样本集小（3个标本）导致AP压缩，获AE级。所有分类器以近完美保真度排列目标标本。误差分析显示9个假阴性（Ipil，局部图像伪影）、3个假阳性（Rain Tree）和1个假阳性（Tindalo，共享解剖特征）。

Conclusion: Xylorix平台使非编程专家也能构建操作可靠的木材识别模型，适合在供应链检查点实地部署。

Original Abstract: Illegal logging and timber trade continue to pose significant challenges in the Philippines, where accurate wood species identification is essential for enforcement but limited by the need for specialised equipment and expertise. This study aims to evaluate whether AI models for macroscopic wood identification can be developed and deployed by wood scientists without programming expertise using the Xylorix platform, focusing on five Philippine hardwood species: Mangium (Acacia mangium Willd.), Rain Tree [Samanea saman (Jacq.) Merr.], Banuyo (Wallaceodendron celebicum Koord.), Tindalo [Afzelia rhomboidea (Blanco) Vidal], and Ipil [Intsia bijuga (Colebr.) O. Kuntze]. Binary classifiers were trained on 10,663 verified cross-section images from 260 specimens and evaluated using specimen-level mean scoring to mirror operational field conditions. Area Under the ROC Curve (AUC) values ranged from 0.969 (Ipil) to 1.000 (Mangium), and Average Precision (AP) values ranged from 0.589 (Samanea) to 1.000 (Mangium). Four of five species achieved AA grade (AUC and AP both \geq 0.90); Rain Tree received AE (AUC \geq 0.90, AP < 0.60) due to AP compression from its small positive test set (3 specimens). All five classifiers rank their target specimens above non-target specimens with near-perfect fidelity. Specimen-level error analysis revealed 9 false negatives from Ipil, primarily stemming from localized image artifacts and 3 false positives for Rain Tree and 1 false positive for Tindalo caused by shared tribal-level anatomical traits. These findings demonstrate that Xylorix non-programmers can leverage the Xylorix platform to construct operationally reliable wood identification models suitable for field deployment at supply chain checkpoints.

中文摘要翻译: 非法采伐和木材贸易在菲律宾持续构成重大挑战，准确的木材物种识别对执法至关重要，但受到专业设备和专业知识需求的限制。本研究旨在评估木材科学家无需编程专业知识，能否利用Xylorix平台开发和部署宏观木材识别AI模型，重点关注五种菲律宾硬木：Mangium（Acacia mangium Willd.）、Rain Tree [Samanea saman (Jacq.) Merr.]、Banuyo（Wallaceodendron celebicum Koord.）、Tindalo [Afzelia rhomboidea (Blanco) Vidal]和Ipil [Intsia bijuga (Colebr.) O. Kuntze]。基于260个标本的10,663张已验证横截面图像训练了二分类器，并采用样本级平均评分进行评估以模拟实地操作条件。ROC曲线下面积（AUC）范围从0.969（Ipil）到1.000（Mangium），平均精度（AP）范围从0.589（Samanea）到1.000（Mangium）。五种物种中有四种达到AA级（AUC和AP均≥0.90）；Rain Tree因正样本测试集较小（3个标本）导致AP压缩，获得AE级（AUC≥0.90，AP<0.60）。所有五个分类器均以近完美保真度将目标标本排列在非目标标本之上。样本级误差分析显示，Ipil出现9个假阴性，主要源于局部图像伪影；Rain Tree出现3个假阳性，Tindalo出现1个假阳性，均由共享的族级解剖特征引起。这些发现表明，Xylorix非程序员可以利用Xylorix平台构建操作可靠的木材识别模型，适合在供应链检查点现场部署。

</details>


### [71] [Listen, Look, and Learn: Learning Without Forgetting through SAM-Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10887)
*Avi Gupta,Nilotpal Sinha,Vishnu Raj,Sambuddha Saha,Pratik Joshi,Koteswar Rao Jerripothula,Tammam Tillo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将SAM-Audio多模态基础模型集成到音频-视觉类别增量学习（CIL）框架中的方法，通过引导注意力机制和双层次蒸馏，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘问题，在多个基准上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有类别增量学习（CIL）在音频-视觉模态领域研究不足，且多模态基础模型（如SAM-Audio）虽蕴含丰富的静态先验知识，但其表示在增量学习场景中表现不佳，亟需弥合这一差距。

Method: 1）利用SAM-Audio的密集音频和视觉表示；2）提出新颖的引导注意力策略，让音频特征上下文引导视觉表示；3）在特征层和logit层引入双层次蒸馏目标以缓解灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在音频-视觉CIL基准上的大量评估表明，该方法在所有指标上一致优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 通过将SAM-Audio的先验知识有效集成到CIL框架中，结合引导注意力与双层次蒸馏，能够显著提升音频-视觉增量学习性能。

Original Abstract: Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) aims to continuously learn new classes without forgetting previously acquired knowledge. While recent CIL advances have spurred significant interest across various modalities, the audio-visual setting remains underexplored. Furthermore, although foundational multimodal models like SAM-Audio encapsulate rich static priors, our empirical analysis reveals that these representations struggle in incremental settings. This work bridges this gap by integrating SAM-Audio's audio-visual priors into the CIL setting. Specifically, we leverage its dense audio and visual representations and employ a novel guided attention strategy where the audio features contextually guide the visual representations. To further mitigate catastrophic forgetting, we introduce dual-level distillation objectives at both the feature and logit levels. Extensive evaluations on audio-visual CIL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

中文摘要翻译: 类别增量学习（CIL）旨在持续学习新类别而不遗忘先前获取的知识。尽管近期CIL的进展在不同模态中引发了广泛关注，但音频-视觉场景仍未得到充分探索。此外，尽管像SAM-Audio这样的基础多模态模型包含了丰富的静态先验，我们的经验分析表明，这些表示在增量设置中表现不佳。本文通过将SAM-Audio的音频-视觉先验集成到CIL框架中弥合了这一差距。具体而言，我们利用其密集的音频和视觉表示，并采用一种新颖的引导注意力策略，让音频特征在上下文中引导视觉表示。为了进一步缓解灾难性遗忘，我们在特征层和logit层引入了双层次蒸馏目标。在音频-视觉CIL基准上的大量评估表明，我们的方法持续优于最先进的方法。

</details>


### [72] [Improving Text-Instance Alignment Of Foreground Conditioned Out-Painting Via Customized Concept Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10892)
*Yihao Zhao,Xuan Han,Bin He,Mingyu You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CCE-Diffusion框架，通过定制化概念嵌入扩散模型解决前景条件图像外扩（FCO）中的语义伪影问题，使生成背景与前景实例语义对齐，提升图像质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本驱动的FCO方法在生成背景时存在严重伪影问题，即合成背景中出现与前景区共享相同语义的区域，这削弱了物体显著性并降低图像质量。作者将其归因于给定实例与文本派生概念嵌入之间的错位。

Method: 提出CCE-Diffusion框架，核心是CCE-Module，用于定制概念嵌入，弥合通用名词语义与具体视觉实例之间的差距。采用Instance-Aware Loss指导模块优化，并使用Semantic-Preserving Prompt Template防止定制嵌入扭曲提示中其他词汇。

Result: 定性和定量评估均表明CCE-Diffusion显著减少了输出中的伪影。CCE-Module作为即插即用组件，可与多种FCO方法集成并提升其性能。

Conclusion: CCE-Diffusion通过定制化概念嵌入有效解决了FCO中的语义伪影问题，且CCE-Module具有良好的通用性和可扩展性。

Original Abstract: To showcase products, merchants often incur substantial costs creating high-quality display images. Foreground Conditioned Outpainting (FCO) meets this demand, allowing users to create desired backgrounds for foreground instances at a low cost by adjusting the text prompt. However, existing text-driven FCO methods exhibit critical flaws in their outputs, most notably the presence of artifacts, which refer to regions in the synthesized background that share the same semantics as the foreground instance. Such artifacts diminish the object's prominence and degrade image quality. We attribute the issue to the misalignment between the given instance and text-derived concept embeddings. To address this, we propose the Customized Concept Embedding Diffusion (CCE-Diffusion) framework. Its core is a CCE-Module to customize concept embeddings, bridging the gap between generic noun semantics and a specific visual instance. An Instance-Aware Loss guides the module's optimization, while a Semantic-Preserving Prompt Template prevents customized embeddings from distorting other words in the prompt. Both qualitative and quantitative evaluations demonstrate that CCE-Diffusion significantly reduces artifacts in the outputs. As a plug-and-play component, the CCE-Module can integrate with various FCO methods, enhancing their performance.

中文摘要翻译: 为了展示商品，商家通常需要花费大量成本制作高质量的展示图像。前景条件图像外扩（FCO）满足了这一需求，用户只需调整文本提示，即可为前景实例以低成本创建所需的背景。然而，现有文本驱动的FCO方法在其输出中存在严重缺陷，最显著的是伪影问题——即合成背景中出现了与前景实例共享相同语义的区域。此类伪影会削弱物体的显著性并降低图像质量。我们将此问题归因于给定实例与文本派生概念嵌入之间的错位。为解决这一问题，我们提出了定制化概念嵌入扩散（CCE-Diffusion）框架。其核心是CCE-Module，用于定制概念嵌入，从而弥合通用名词语义与具体视觉实例之间的差距。Instance-Aware Loss指导该模块的优化，而Semantic-Preserving Prompt Template则防止定制化嵌入扭曲提示中的其他词汇。定性和定量评估均表明，CCE-Diffusion显著减少了输出中的伪影。作为一种即插即用组件，CCE-Module可与多种FCO方法集成，提升它们的性能。

</details>


### [73] [The 1st PortraitCraft Challenge: A CVPR 2026 Workshop Competition on Portrait Composition Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10894)
*Zijie Lou,Youyun Tang,Xiaochao Qu,Haoxiang Li,Ting Liu,Luoqi Liu,Xun Zhu,Zheng Zhang,Xi Chen,Miao Li,Ji Wu,Dizhe Zhang,Xian Ge,Sujia Wang,Ruiyang Zhang,Jiaming Wang,Xianshun Wang,Lu Qi,Boao Kang,Wei Zhou,Jinghui Sun,Zhenyu Yan,Jiliang Zhao,Rui Yang,Yipo Huang,Boyuan Liu,Shanglin Li,Zifan Xie,Yichen Zhang,Anlan Wang,Wenfeng Lin,Mingyu Guo,Dong Li,Xinghao Wang,Yanting Li,Shanzhao Tong,Shuai He,Qiu Zhou,Yongqi Yang,Taoyang Mu,Dianqiao Lei,Anlong Ming,Huadong Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在CVPR 2026上举办的PortraitCraft挑战赛，该挑战赛围绕人像构图理解与生成设立两个互补赛道，并发布了包含约5万张真实人像的大规模构图数据集，旨在推动人像美学分析与可控图像合成的研究。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据集和任务主要关注全局美学评分，缺乏对人像构图的结构化理解与可控生成的研究基准，因此需要建立一个统一的评估框架和标准化平台来推动该领域发展。

Method: 构建了一个包含约5万张真实人像的大规模构图数据集，提供多层次监督信息；设立两个互补赛道：Track 1要求模型进行结构化人像构图理解，Track 2要求模型根据结构化构图描述在显式构图约束下生成人像图像。

Result: 本文报告了挑战赛的设置、评估协议、数据集构成和最终结果，并对提交方案的技术特点进行了分析。

Conclusion: PortraitCraft挑战赛为研究人像构图理解与生成提供了标准化、可复现的平台，有望推动人像美学和可控图像生成领域的进一步发展。

Original Abstract: This paper presents an overview of the inaugural PortraitCraft Challenge, held as one of the official competitions at CVPR 2026. The challenge focuses on portrait composition understanding and generation, aiming to advance AI research in portrait aesthetics analysis and controllable image synthesis. Unlike existing datasets and tasks that primarily focus on global aesthetic scoring, PortraitCraft introduces a unified evaluation framework comprising two complementary tracks. Track 1 requires models to perform structured portrait composition understanding, and Track 2 requires models to generate portrait images from structured composition descriptions under explicit compositional constraints. To support the challenge, we constructed and publicly released a large-scale portrait composition dataset consisting of approximately 50,000 curated real portrait images, providing multi-level supervision. This report describes the challenge setup, evaluation protocols, dataset composition, and final results, along with an analysis of the technical characteristics of the submitted solutions. The PortraitCraft Challenge provides a standardized and reproducible platform for research on portrait composition understanding and generation, and is expected to foster further progress in the fields of portrait aesthetics and controllable image generation.

中文摘要翻译: 本文概述了首届PortraitCraft挑战赛，该赛事作为CVPR 2026的官方竞赛之一。挑战赛聚焦于人像构图理解与生成，旨在推动AI在人像美学分析和可控图像合成方面的研究。与现有主要关注全局美学评分的数据集和任务不同，PortraitCraft引入了一个统一的评估框架，包含两个互补赛道。Track 1要求模型进行结构化人像构图理解，Track 2要求模型在显式构图约束下根据结构化构图描述生成人像图像。为支持该挑战赛，我们构建并公开发布了一个大规模人像构图数据集，包含约5万张经过精心挑选的真实人像图像，并提供多层次监督信息。本报告描述了挑战赛的设置、评估协议、数据集构成和最终结果，并对所提交方案的技术特点进行了分析。PortraitCraft挑战赛为研究人像构图理解与生成提供了标准化、可复现的平台，有望进一步推动人像美学和可控图像生成领域的进展。

</details>


### [74] [Pose-ICL: 3D-Aware In-Context Learning for Pose-Controllable Subject Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10902)
*Xuan Han,Yihao Zhao,Mingyu You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Pose-ICL，一种无需微调的框架，通过3D感知上下文学习实现定制化主体的精确姿态控制，核心机制是表面锚定位置嵌入（SAPE）将图像token锚定到体积边界框的表面坐标上，显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有主体定制化方法在姿态控制方面表现不佳，常出现不准确的姿态或跨姿态外观不一致的问题，说明2D主干网络难以实现体素级别的对象理解。

Method: 提出 Pose-ICL 框架，采用3D感知上下文学习（ICL）通过多个配对的图像-姿态参考直接适应新主体；核心机制为表面锚定位置嵌入（SAPE），将图像token锚定到体积边界框的表面坐标，赋予模型显式的3D意识，并经过专门优化以确保与现有DiT模型的兼容性。

Result: 在3D资产和真实主体上的广泛评估表明，Pose-ICL 在姿态准确性和身份一致性上均显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Pose-ICL 通过3D感知上下文学习有效解决了定制化主体生成中的姿态控制难题，为2D生成模型注入了体积理解能力。

Original Abstract: Subject Customization is a foundational task in modern image generation. By providing a few reference images and a text prompt, users can generate images of a specific object in any desired scene. However, existing methods still struggle to achieve effective pose control for customized subjects. In practice, they often exhibit inaccurate poses or inconsistent cross-pose appearances. These limitations suggest that understanding objects in a volumetric manner remains a significant challenge for 2D-native backbones. To address this challenge, we propose Pose-ICL, a tuning-free framework that leverages 3D-aware In-Context Learning (ICL) to directly adapt to new subjects through multiple paired image-pose references. Its core mechanism,Surface-Anchored Position Embedding (SAPE), equips the model with explicit 3D awareness by anchoring image tokens to the surface coordinates of a volumetric bounding box. Dedicated refinements ensure its seamless compatibility with existing DiT models. Extensive evaluations on both 3D assets and real-world subjects demonstrate that Pose-ICL significantly outperforms current methods in both pose accuracy and identity consistency.

中文摘要翻译: 主体定制化是现代图像生成中的基础任务。通过提供少量参考图像和文本提示，用户可以生成特定对象在任何期望场景中的图像。然而，现有方法在实现对定制化主体的有效姿态控制方面仍然存在困难。在实践中，它们常常表现出不准确的姿态或跨姿态外观不一致。这些局限性表明，对于2D原生主干网络而言，以体素方式理解对象仍然是一个重大挑战。为解决这一挑战，我们提出了 Pose-ICL，一种无需微调的框架，利用3D感知上下文学习（ICL）通过多个配对的图像-姿态参考直接适应新主体。其核心机制——表面锚定位置嵌入（SAPE），通过将图像token锚定到体积边界框的表面坐标，赋予模型显式的3D感知能力。专门的优化设计确保了其与现有DiT模型的无缝兼容性。在3D资产和真实主体上的广泛评估表明，Pose-ICL 在姿态准确性和身份一致性上均显著优于现有方法。

</details>


### [75] [Beyond Model Size: Probing the Gaps in Visual in-Context Learning by Training a Tiny Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10905)
*Sunil Khatri,Steven Landgraf,Markus Ulrich,Simon Reiß*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过构建一个仅1百万参数、7万张图像的极小视觉上下文学习（VICL）模型，与7000倍大的模型对比，揭示当前VICL评估方法在任务编码、预训练任务选择和度量标准等方面存在缺陷，指出亟需创新适应性能力的评估方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前VICL方法主要依赖模型和数据规模的扩展，但尚不清楚这些因素是否对视觉模型中的上下文学习能力起关键作用。为了检验这一假设，作者构建了一个极端反例——极小规模的VICL模型，来挑战大规模模型的必要性，并揭示当前评估基准的不足。

Method: 训练一个仅含1百万参数、使用7万张图像的极小VICL模型（tiny model），并将其与7000倍规模（约70亿参数）的大型VICL模型在不同自适应设置下进行比较，包括：(1) 小分布偏移的图像数据，(2) 未见过的任务编码，(3) 全新的任务（VICL所设想的核心场景）。

Result: 实验发现，尽管训练资源差距悬殊，但当前VICL评估方式在任务编码方式、预训练任务选择以及度量标准等方面存在显著缺陷，导致对模型自适应能力的测量不够全面和准确。

Conclusion: 当前VICL基准测试中的这些缺陷表明，亟需在自适应能力的评估方法上进行创新，才能更有效地衡量和推动视觉上下文学习的进展。

Original Abstract: Visual in-Context Learning (VICL) aims at making progress towards adaptive vision models, that can -- based on a few examples -- adapt to a new task at test-time. With the history of in-context learning in natural language processing research, where large, parameter-heavy models are in use, one pathway that current VICL methods take is model- and data-scaling as key ingredients. Yet, it is not clear, whether these ingredients are the key for in-context learning to take shape in vision models. To stress-test such large models, we challenge them with an extreme counterexample: we train a tiny visual in-context model with merely $1$ million parameters and a modest amount of $70,000$ images. We compare the results of this severely capacity capped tiny model to $7,000\times$ larger VICL models in different adaptive settings, (1) on image data with small distribution shifts, (2) on unseen task encodings and (3) on a completely new task, i.e., the setting VICL envisions. With the chasm of training resources between the tiny- and large models, our experiments showcase a lack in how adaptive capabilities are measured, with respect to how tasks are encoded, which tasks were used in pre-training and the choice of metrics. These gaps in current VICL benchmarking underscore a need for innovation in evaluation of adaptive capabilities.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉上下文学习（VICL）旨在推动自适应视觉模型的发展，使其能够基于少量样本在测试时适应新任务。在自然语言处理研究中的上下文学习历史里，大规模、参数密集的模型被广泛使用，当前VICL方法也沿袭了这一路径，将模型和数据规模扩展视为关键要素。然而，尚不清楚这些要素是否确实是视觉模型形成上下文学习能力的关键。为了检验这些大型模型，我们用一个极端反例来挑战它们：我们训练了一个仅含1百万参数、使用区区7万张图像的极小视觉上下文模型。我们将这个容量严重受限的极小模型与7000倍规模的大型VICL模型在不同自适应设置下进行比较：(1) 存在小分布偏移的图像数据，(2) 未见过的任务编码，(3) 全新的任务——即VICL所设想的核心场景。鉴于极小模型与大型模型之间训练资源的巨大鸿沟，我们的实验揭示了当前在自适应能力测量方面的不足，具体体现在任务编码方式、预训练任务选择以及度量标准的选择上。当前VICL基准测试中的这些缺陷表明，亟需在适应性能力的评估方法上进行创新。

</details>


### [76] [PENet+: A Lightweight Residual Transformer Framework for Efficient Image Steganalysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10939)
*Jincheol AN,Dongsu Kim,Haneol Jang,YoungJoon Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PENet+轻量级图像隐写分析框架，通过精简分类器通道、引入激活感知的高通滤波选择和MobileNetV2骨干网络，在保持PENet检测精度的同时，将参数量减少45.5%、FLOPs降低约97%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有残差Transformer隐写分析架构（如PENet）检测精度高，但计算和内存开销大，限制了在资源受限环境下的部署。本文旨在保留PENet判别性结构的前提下大幅提升效率。

Method: (1) 分类器精简阶段：逐步缩小SPP到FC1的输入通道数；(2) 激活感知的高通滤波（HPF）前处理：聚合早期HPF响应，选取平衡的SRM-Gabor top-K子集；(3) 用MobileNetV2风格的反转残差网络替代PENet骨干网络；(4) 从隐写分析角度论证PReLU优于ReLU，保留负响应以捕捉弱隐写线索。

Result: 在ALASKA2 JPEG QF90数据集（512×512分辨率）上，与重新评估的PENet基线相比，PENet+实现参数量减少高达45.5%，FLOPs减少约97%，同时检测精度相当或更优。K=31的平衡配置（16个Gabor+15个SRM滤波器）在更低计算量下达到或超越更重配置的性能。

Conclusion: PENet+为资源受限的隐写分析提供了一种高效的计算方向，在显著降低计算开销的同时保持了检测性能。设备级延迟和功耗测量留作未来工作。

Original Abstract: Image steganalysis, the detection of hidden information embedded in digital images, is a core component of modern cybersecurity and digital forensics. Recent residual Transformer architectures, such as the Pixel-Difference-Convolution and Enhanced-Transformer-Network (PENet) [1], achieve strong detection accuracy, but their computational and memory demands hinder deployment in resource-constrained settings. We present PENet+, a lightweight steganalysis framework that preserves PENet's discriminative structure while substantially improving efficiency. Rather than redesigning or compressing the attention blocks, we retain PENet's self-attention topology for reproducibility and add a classifier-streamlining stage that progressively narrows the SPP-to-FC1 input channels (SPP: spatial pyramid pooling; FC1: first fully connected layer), yielding large reductions in parameters and FLOPs with negligible accuracy loss. We further refine the high-pass-filter (HPF) stem with an activation-aware mechanism that aggregates HPF responses early and selects a balanced SRM-Gabor top-K subset, and we replace PENet's backbone with a MobileNetV2-style inverted residual network. A balanced configuration with K=31 filters (16 Gabor + 15 SRM) matches or surpasses heavier settings at lower compute. Finally, we motivate PReLU from a steganalysis standpoint, arguing that preserving negative responses helps capture weak stego cues that ReLU suppresses. On a disjoint ALASKA2 JPEG QF90 protocol at 512x512 resolution (5,000 cover images for training, validation, and internal testing; a separate 19,000-cover evaluation set), PENet+ achieves up to 45.5% fewer parameters and about 97% fewer FLOPs than the re-evaluated PENet baseline, offering a computationally efficient direction for resource-constrained steganalysis. Device-level latency and power measurements remain future work.

中文摘要翻译: 图像隐写分析（即检测数字图像中嵌入的隐藏信息）是现代网络安全和数字取证的核心组成部分。最近的残差Transformer架构，如像素差卷积与增强Transformer网络（PENet）[1]，取得了较强的检测精度，但其计算和内存需求阻碍了在资源受限环境下的部署。本文提出PENet+，一个轻量级隐写分析框架，在保留PENet判别性结构的同时大幅提升效率。我们没有重新设计或压缩注意力模块，而是保留PENet的自注意力拓扑结构以确保可复现性，并增加了一个分类器精简阶段，逐步缩小SPP到FC1的输入通道数（SPP：空间金字塔池化；FC1：第一个全连接层），从而在精度损失可忽略的前提下大幅减少参数量和FLOPs。我们进一步用激活感知机制优化高通滤波（HPF）前处理模块，该机制早期聚合HPF响应并选取平衡的SRM-Gabor top-K子集，同时将PENet的骨干网络替换为MobileNetV2风格的反转残差网络。K=31个滤波器（16个Gabor+15个SRM）的平衡配置在更低计算量下达到或超越更重配置的性能。最后，我们从隐写分析角度论证PReLU的合理性，认为保留负响应有助于捕捉ReLU抑制的弱隐写线索。在ALASKA2 JPEG QF90协议上，使用不重叠的数据集进行512×512分辨率测试（5,000张封面图像用于训练、验证和内部测试；独立的19,000张封面图像评估集），与重新评估的PENet基线相比，PENet+参数量减少高达45.5%，FLOPs减少约97%，为资源受限的隐写分析提供了一种高效的计算方向。设备级延迟和功耗测量留作未来工作。

</details>


### [77] [Democratising Camera Trap AI: An Open-Source Model for Detecting UK Mammals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10940)
*Paul Fergus,Philip Stephens,Russell A. Hill,Lee Oliver,Katie Appleby,Sarah Beatham,Naomi Davies Walsh,Stuart Nixon,Naomi Matthews,Chris Sutherland,Kelly Hitchcock*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个面向英国常见哺乳动物和鸟类的开源YOLO26x目标检测模型，涵盖31个类别，基于48,165个标注实例训练，在验证集上达到mAP@0.5=0.984，旨在打破商业平台垄断，降低生态学家的使用门槛。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AI生态监测模型多被商业平台锁定，或训练数据与英国物种不匹配，阻碍了广泛采用。作者旨在移除这些障碍，提供一个免费、开源、易用的替代方案。

Method: 使用YOLO26x检测器，基于48,165个标注实例（31个类别，包括28种英国常见鸟兽及人、校准杆、车辆），按80/10/10类别分层划分训练/验证/测试集进行训练和评估。模型权重以ONNX格式发布，支持本地桌面和实时摄像头，面向无机器学习经验的生态学家。

Result: 验证集上mAP@0.5=0.984，mAP@0.5:0.95=0.956，精确率0.988，召回率0.965。测试集上各类别平均置信度0.96–0.99，假阴性率0.17%，主要出现在夜间、远距离或被遮挡图像中。但数据来自同一站点池，在新站点上的性能尚待验证。

Conclusion: 该开源模型在已知站点上表现优异，可作为付费商业模型的有力替代，降低生态监测中AI技术的使用门槛。未来需评估其在全新站点上的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Camera traps have become a cornerstone of biodiversity monitoring, but the artificial intelligence that turns vast quantities of images into usable ecological data is often locked behind commercial platforms or trained on fauna that does not match that of the British Isles. In an attempt to remove barriers and increase uptake, we release an open-source object detection model for 31 classes, 28 common UK mammal and bird species, plus utility classes for humans, calibration poles, and vehicles, drawn from a curated dataset of 48,165 labelled instances assembled from multiple sites over a decade of operational deployment through Conservation AI and its successor, Trap Tracker. The model, a YOLO26x detector trained and tested on an 80/10/10 class-stratified split, achieves a mean Average Precision of 0.984 at Intersection over Union (IoU) of 0.5 (0.956 at IoU 0.5-0.95) on the held-out validation set, with precision 0.988 and recall 0.965. On an unseen held-out test split, mean per-species confidence ranged from 0.96 to 0.99 across the 31 classes, with a 0.17% false-negative rate concentrated in difficult night-time, distant, or occluded images. These metrics are from data from the same pool of sites and cameras as training, so performance at entirely new sites is left to future work. We release the trained weights in ONNX format under a non-commercial licence, with local desktop and real-time camera support, aimed explicitly at ecologists with no machine-learning experience. This release is a deliberate counterweight to the multiple paid for models that have developed over the last decade.

中文摘要翻译: 相机陷阱已成为生物多样性监测的基石，但将海量图像转化为可用生态数据的人工智能往往被锁定在商业平台之后，或基于与不列颠群岛物种不匹配的动物群进行训练。为了消除障碍并提高采用率，我们发布了一个面向31个类别的开源目标检测模型，包括28种英国常见哺乳动物和鸟类，以及人类、校准杆和车辆等实用类别。这些数据来自一个由48,165个标注实例组成的精编数据集，该数据集汇集了多个站点在Conservation AI及其后续系统Trap Tracker长达十年的实际部署中所积累的数据。该模型采用YOLO26x检测器，按80/10/10的类别分层划分进行训练和测试，在保留验证集上实现了平均精度（mAP）@IoU=0.5为0.984（mAP@IoU=0.5–0.95为0.956），精确率为0.988，召回率为0.965。在一个未见过的保留测试集上，31个类别的平均各类别置信度范围为0.96至0.99，假阴性率为0.17%，主要集中在困难的夜间、远距离或被遮挡图像中。这些指标来自与训练数据相同的站点和相机池，因此在新站点上的性能留待未来研究。我们以非商业许可的ONNX格式发布训练好的权重，支持本地桌面和实时摄像头，明确面向无机器学习经验的生态学家。此次发布是对过去十年中涌现的多个付费模型的有意制衡。

</details>


### [78] [Quo Vadis, Visual In-Context Learning? A Unified Benchmark Across Domains and Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10967)
*Pradnya Halady,Jiale Wei,Zdravko Marinov,Alexander Jaus,Simon Reiß*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个视觉上下文学习综合基准测试集VIBE，包含14个数据集、12种任务和106种数据集-任务组合，对六种视觉上下文学习模型进行统一评估，揭示了现有模型的局限性、系统性失败模式和有前景的研究方向。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉上下文学习模型的评估局限于狭窄的设置，主要反映预训练阶段已见过的任务或图像域，无法真正衡量模型的适应能力。本文旨在填补这一空白，构建一个覆盖多样化成像域和广泛任务的基准来全面评估模型的适应性。

Method: 构建VIBE基准测试集，涵盖14个数据集、12种任务（共106种数据集-任务组合），在统一的、可复现的评估协议下，对六种视觉上下文学习模型进行一次性（one-shot）设置的对比评估。

Result: 通过大规模系统性评估，揭示了视觉上下文学习模型的局限性、系统性失败模式和有前景的研究方向。

Conclusion: 现有视觉上下文学习模型在面对新的图像分布和任务分布时仍存在显著局限，VIBE基准为未来研究提供了更清晰的评估框架和有价值的洞察。

Original Abstract: Visual in-context learning has been proposed as a pathway towards dynamic models that can generate predictions based on a provided context and thereby can adapt to new vision tasks at test-time. Yet, the evaluation of the adaptation capabilities of these models has been limited to narrow setups that mainly mirror tasks or image domains from pre-training for which real adaptation is not required. We address this gap by constructing a broad Visual In-Context BEnchmark (VIBE) with a focus on diverse imaging domains and a wide range of tasks. With this, we are able to get a much clearer picture of the adaptive capabilities of visual in-context models when faced with new image- and task distributions. We stress test six models on $14$ datasets and $12$ tasks (in total, we explore $106$ dataset-task combinations) and compare them under a unified, reproducible evaluation protocol, in an one-shot setting. Our evaluation uncovers key insights on the state of visual in-context learning, including limitations, systematic failure modes and promising directions. To foster broader evaluation, we will openly release our VIBE toolkit.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉上下文学习被提出作为一种通往动态模型的路径，这类模型能够基于提供的上下文生成预测，从而在测试时适应新的视觉任务。然而，对这些模型适应能力的评估一直局限于狭窄的设置，主要反映来自预训练阶段的任务或图像域，而这些情况下实际上并不需要真正的适应。我们通过构建一个涵盖多样化成像域和广泛任务的综合视觉上下文学习基准测试集（VIBE）来填补这一空白。借此，我们能够更清晰地了解视觉上下文学习模型在面对新的图像分布和任务分布时的适应能力。我们对六种模型在14个数据集和12种任务上进行了压力测试（总共探索了106种数据集-任务组合），并在统一的、可复现的评估协议下，以一次性（one-shot）设置进行比较。我们的评估揭示了视觉上下文学习的现状，包括其局限性、系统性失败模式以及有前景的方向。为促进更广泛的评估，我们将公开发布VIBE工具包。

</details>


### [79] [AnimaSpark: A Feed-Forward Method for Animating Arbitrary 3D Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10988)
*Yiming Zhao,Haoyu Sun,Aoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnimaSpark是一种新颖的类别无关3D动画生成管线，通过将关节变换投影到二维子空间进行建模，利用视频生成模型和关键点跟踪算法，实现了高质量、高效率的文本驱动3D动画生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前类别无关3D动画生成方法在推理速度、运动质量和文本提示遵循性方面存在严重局限性，导致3D资产生产仍依赖于劳动密集型的手工制作，亟需一种高效、高质量的自动化动画生成方案。

Method: 1) 将带骨架的静态3D模型渲染为多层图像表示（网格和骨骼）；2) 将渲染结果输入视频生成模型；3) 在生成视频上使用关键点跟踪算法捕捉骨骼关节在摄像机投影平面上的运动；4) 从跟踪的关键点中提取平面平移和旋转，将其从2D域提升到3D空间以驱动角色动画。

Result: 在文本-运动对齐、运动质量和计算效率等关键指标上，该方法显著优于现有的最先进技术。

Conclusion: AnimaSpark通过将3D关节变换建模在二维子空间中，成功实现了类别无关的3D动画高效生成，在多个指标上达到最优性能，有效解决了当前自动化动画生成的瓶颈问题。

Original Abstract: While recent advancements in generative AI have substantially accelerated static 3D model creation workflows, the synthesis of category-agnostic 3D animations remains a significant bottleneck in 3D asset production. Current methods for category-agnostic animation generation exhibit critical limitations in inference speed, motion quality, and adherence to textual prompts, thereby leaving the process dependent on labor-intensive manual artistry. To address these challenges, this paper introduces AnimaSpark, a novel pipeline for category-agnostic 3D animation generation. Our approach is motivated by the key insight that for many fundamental motions in the 3D world, the corresponding joint transformations can often be effectively modeled within a two-dimensional subspace. The pipeline begins by rendering a rigged static 3D model into multi-layered image representations of its mesh and skeleton, which are subsequently fed into a video generation model. We then employ a keypoint tracking algorithm on the generated video to capture the motion of the skeletal joints projected onto the camera's viewing plane. In the final stage, we distill the planar translations and rotations from these tracked keypoints and lift them from the 2D domain into 3D space to animate the character. Comprehensive evaluations reveal that our method achieves superior performance over existing state-of-the-art techniques across key metrics, including text-motion alignment, quality of motion, and computational efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管生成式AI的最新进展已大幅加速了静态3D模型创建流程，但类别无关的3D动画合成仍然是3D资产生产中的一个显著瓶颈。当前用于类别无关动画生成的方法在推理速度、运动质量以及文本提示遵循性方面存在严重局限性，导致该过程仍依赖于劳动密集型的手工制作。为解决这些挑战，本文介绍了AnimaSpark——一种新颖的类别无关3D动画生成管线。我们的方法基于一个关键洞察：对于3D世界中许多基础运动，相应的关节变换通常可以在二维子空间内有效建模。该管线首先将带骨架的静态3D模型渲染为网格和骨骼的多层图像表示，随后将其输入视频生成模型。接着，我们在生成的视频上采用关键点跟踪算法，捕捉骨骼关节投影到摄像机观察平面上的运动。在最后阶段，我们从这些跟踪的关键点中提取平面平移和旋转，并将其从2D域提升到3D空间以驱动角色动画。综合评估表明，我们的方法在文本-运动对齐、运动质量和计算效率等关键指标上均优于现有最先进技术。

</details>


### [80] [IPSM-Bench: A New Intermediate Phase Segmentation Benchmark in Microstructure Images of Zinc-Based Absorbable Biomaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11001)
*Jinglin Xu,Shangyan Zhao,Jiabo Wang,Xinghong Mu,Yulong Lei,Jiacheng Zhang,Hongbo Sun,Yageng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对锌合金中间相分割面临的数据集稀缺、低对比度、小目标检测难和形态异质等挑战，构建了最大规模的高质量数据集IPSM-Bench，并提出空间上下文先验引导的SAM方法SCoP-SAM，将梯度结构与灰度特征融入编码-解码全过程，在锌合金及多种合金场景下均达到最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 锌基合金作为可吸收金属生物材料，其宏观性能由微观组织特征决定，中间相是关键微结构组分。然而中间相分割面临标注数据稀缺、对比度低、小目标检测难、形态异质等严峻挑战，亟需高质量数据集和高效分割方法。

Method: 构建最大规模锌合金中间相分割高质量数据集IPSM-Bench；提出SCoP-SAM方法，利用中间相的梯度结构和灰度特性捕获空间上下文先验，并将其融入SAM的整个编码-解码过程，提升分割性能。

Result: 在IPSM-Bench和额外公开合金基准上的大量实验表明，SCoP-SAM在锌合金中间相分割上达到SOTA性能，且在其它合金场景中具有优异的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文通过构建大规模基准数据集和提出空间上下文先验引导的SAM方法，有效解决了锌合金中间相分割难题，并建立了系统的评估基准，推动了锌合金微观组织分析研究。

Original Abstract: Zinc-based alloys are indispensable emerging absorbable metallic biomaterials, and their macroscopic performance is governed by microstructural characteristics. Intermediate phases-key microstructural constituents-are pivotal in regulating mechanical and functional properties. However, intermediate phase segmentation in zinc alloy microstructures faces formidable challenges: scarce annotated datasets, low contrast, difficulty detecting small targets, and heterogeneous morphologies. To this end, we construct IPSM-Bench, the largest high-quality dataset for zinc-alloy intermediate phase segmentation. Furthermore, we propose SCoP-SAM, a new Spatial Context Prior-guided SAM method that leverages the gradient structure and grayscale properties of intermediate phases to capture spatial context priors and incorporates them into the entire SAM encoding-decoding process, improving segmentation performance. Based on the proposed IPSM-Bench, we establish a new benchmark for intermediate phase segmentation to systematically evaluate state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods and advance research on zinc alloy microstructure analysis. Extensive experiments on IPSM-Bench and additional public alloy benchmarks demonstrate that our SCoP-SAM not only achieves SOTA performance for zinc-alloy intermediate phase segmentation but also generalizes remarkably well to other alloy scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 锌基合金是不可或缺的新型可吸收金属生物材料，其宏观性能受微结构特征支配。中间相作为关键微结构组分，对调控力学和功能性能至关重要。然而，锌合金微结构中中间相分割面临严峻挑战：标注数据集稀缺、对比度低、小目标检测困难以及形态异质性强。为此，我们构建了IPSM-Bench——目前最大规模的锌合金中间相分割高质量数据集。此外，我们提出了SCoP-SAM，一种新的空间上下文先验引导的SAM方法，利用中间相的梯度结构和灰度特性捕获空间上下文先验，并将其融入整个SAM编码-解码过程，从而提升分割性能。基于所提出的IPSM-Bench，我们建立了中间相分割的新基准，以系统评估当前最先进方法并推进锌合金微结构分析研究。在IPSM-Bench和额外公开合金基准上的大量实验表明，我们的SCoP-SAM不仅在锌合金中间相分割上取得了最先进性能，而且在其他合金场景中也展现出优异的泛化能力。

</details>


### [81] [An Uncertainty Estimation Framework for Dose Accumulation in Adaptive Radiotherapy: Application to CBCT-Guided Radiotherapy for Cervical Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11012)
*Cedric Hemon,Delphine Lebret,Jean-Claude Nunes,Valentin Boussot,Karine Peignaux,Nathalie Mesgouez-Nebout,Chantal Hanzen,Antoine Simon,Anaïs Barateau,Renaud de Crevoisier,Caroline Lafond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IMPACT-DoseAcc框架，一种面向在线自适应放疗（oART）的、考虑DIR不确定性的剂量累积方法，并在宫颈癌CBCT引导的oART中验证其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的oART日间计划适应能够处理分次间解剖变异，但累积剂量估计仍受限于DIR、分割和解剖不确定性。缺乏不确定度量化的剂量累积框架限制了临床对累积剂量的可信解释。

Method: 基于IMPACT平台，聚焦DIR不确定性（不建模vCT生成不确定性），测试两种DIR不确定性策略：贝叶斯分割引导的概率模型（量化解剖不确定性）和分割模型集成（捕获认知变异性）。通过体素级不确定性图传播至剂量变形与累积，生成概率性剂量-体积直方图（pDVH）。集成不确定性由变形场的体素级标准差量化，几何误差通过变形后轮廓与验证轮廓的表面距离评估，并采用解剖变异加权聚合进行细化。

Result: 集成DIR不确定性与几何误差相关，CTVt和膀胱的Pearson系数分别为0.63和0.66。CTVt的pDVH覆盖率达96.3±3.9%，表明传播的不确定性得到良好校准。加权方法稳定了各分次和器官的估计值。

Conclusion: IMPACT-DoseAcc将配准驱动的不确定性传播至累积剂量指标，提升了在解剖变异下累积剂量的可解释性。其3DSlicer集成支持可重复、不确定性感知的ART工作流。

Original Abstract: Background and purpose: oART enables daily plan adaptation to interfraction anatomical variations, but cumulative dose estimation remains limited by DIR, segmentation, and anatomical uncertainties. We introduce IMPACT-DoseAcc, an uncertainty-aware dose accumulation framework, within IMPACT for semantic feature-driven image analysis. The framework is modality- and disease-agnostic and is applied to CBCT-guided oART for cervical cancer (LACC).
  Material and Methods: Nine LACC patients were retrospectively analyzed using daily CBCT-derived virtual CTs for dose recalculation. IMPACT-DoseAcc focuses on uncertainty from DIR, without modeling vCT-generation uncertainty. Two DIR uncertainty strategies were tested within IMPACT-Reg: a Bayesian segmentation-guided approach using one probabilistic model to quantify anatomical uncertainty, and an ensemble of segmentation models targeting structures to capture epistemic variability. Voxel-wise uncertainty maps were propagated through dose warping and accumulation to generate probabilistic dose-volume histograms. Ensemble uncertainty was quantified from voxel-wise standard deviation across deformation fields, and geometric error was assessed using surface distance between warped and validated contours. Anatomical-variability weighting refined aggregation.
  Results: Ensemble DIR uncertainty correlated with geometric error, with Pearson coefficients of 0.63 for CTVt and 0.66 for bladder. For CTVt, pDVHs achieved 96.3 +/- 3.9% coverage, showing calibration of propagated uncertainty. Weighting stabilized estimates across fractions and organs.
  Conclusions: IMPACT-DoseAcc propagates registration-driven uncertainty to cumulative dose metrics, improving interpretation of accumulated dose under anatomical variations. Its 3DSlicer integration supports reproducible, uncertainty-informed ART workflows.

中文摘要翻译: 背景与目的：在线自适应放疗（oART）能够实现日常计划对分次间解剖变异的适应，但累积剂量估计仍然受到可变形图像配准（DIR）、分割和解剖不确定性的限制。我们在IMPACT框架内引入IMPACT-DoseAcc——一种不确定性感知的剂量累积框架，用于语义特征驱动的图像分析。该框架具有模态和疾病无关性，并应用于宫颈癌（LACC）的锥形束CT（CBCT）引导的oART。
  材料与方法：回顾性分析9例LACC患者，使用每日CBCT衍生的虚拟CT（vCT）进行剂量重新计算。IMPACT-DoseAcc聚焦于DIR带来的不确定性，不建模vCT生成的不确定性。在IMPACT-Reg中测试了两种DIR不确定性策略：一种采用贝叶斯分割引导的方法，使用单个概率模型量化解剖不确定性；另一种采用针对靶结构的分割模型集成，以捕获认知变异性。体素级不确定性图通过剂量变形与累积过程传播，生成概率性剂量-体积直方图（pDVH）。集成不确定性通过变形场跨模型体素级标准差进行量化，几何误差通过变形后轮廓与验证轮廓之间的表面距离进行评估。解剖变异加权方法进一步细化了聚合结果。
  结果：集成DIR不确定性与几何误差相关，临床靶体积（CTVt）和膀胱的Pearson相关系数分别为0.63和0.66。对于CTVt，pDVH覆盖率达到96.3±3.9%，表明传播的不确定性得到了良好的校准。加权方法稳定了各分次和器官的估计值。
  结论：IMPACT-DoseAcc将配准驱动的不确定性传播至累积剂量指标，提升了在解剖变异下对累积剂量的解释能力。其与3DSlicer的集成支持可重复、不确定性感知的自适应放疗工作流。

</details>


### [82] [U-TTT: Towards Generalizable PET Image Denoising via Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11032)
*Zhiwen Yang,Jiayin Li,Hao Lu,Hui Zhang,Zihua Wang,Bingzheng Wei,Yan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出U-TTT模型，通过集成测试时训练（TTT）层和双域适应机制（空间域S-TTT和频域F-TTT），在推理阶段动态调整模型参数，实现了PET图像去噪在分布偏移（不同剂量水平和扫描仪类型）下的鲁棒泛化，达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PET图像去噪深度学习模型在分布偏移下性能严重下降，因为固定参数模型无法在训练后适应测试数据的变化（如剂量水平或扫描仪类型），限制了临床部署的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出U-TTT模型，采用U形架构集成测试时训练（TTT）层，在推理阶段通过自监督方式动态调整模型参数以适应每个测试样本。同时设计双域适应机制：S-TTT层捕获并校正空间结构退化，F-TTT层抑制全局噪声谱并恢复高频细节。

Result: 在多种分布偏移场景（未见剂量水平和未见扫描仪）下，U-TTT实现了最先进的PET去噪性能，展现出优越的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将测试时训练引入PET图像去噪能有效解决分布偏移下的泛化问题，双域适应机制可全面捕捉3D PET数据的复杂退化，为模型鲁棒临床部署提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Existing deep learning models for Positron Emission Tomography (PET) image denoising often suffer from severe performance degradation under distribution shifts, fundamentally restricting their robust clinical deployment. This lack of generalization stems from the conventional paradigm of fixed-parameter models that cannot adapt to variations in test data (e.g., dose levels or scanner types) after training. To overcome this limitation and achieve robust generalization, we introduce U-TTT, a novel U-shaped model that integrates Test-Time Training (TTT) layers to dynamically adjust model parameters during inference through self-supervision, thereby adapting to the specific characteristics of each test instance. Furthermore, to comprehensively capture the complex degradations of 3D PET data, U-TTT features a dual-domain adaptation mechanism comprising a Spatial Test-Time Training (S-TTT) layer and a Frequency Test-Time Training (F-TTT) layer. The S-TTT layer captures and corrects spatial structural degradations, while the F-TTT layer suppresses global noise spectra and restores delicate high-frequency details. Extensive experiments demonstrate that U-TTT achieves state-of-the-art PET denoising performance and exhibits superior generalization under challenging distribution shifts, including both unseen dose levels and unseen scanners. Our code will be available at https://github.com/Yaziwel/U-TTT.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的用于正电子发射断层扫描（PET）图像去噪的深度学习模型在分布偏移下常常遭受严重的性能退化，从根本上限制了其鲁棒的临床部署。这种泛化能力的缺乏源于传统范式下的固定参数模型无法在训练后适应测试数据的变化（例如剂量水平或扫描仪类型）。为了克服这一限制并实现鲁棒泛化，我们引入了U-TTT，一种新颖的U形模型，它集成了测试时训练（TTT）层，通过自监督方式在推理过程中动态调整模型参数，从而适应每个测试样本的特定特征。此外，为了全面捕捉3D PET数据的复杂退化，U-TTT设计了双域适应机制，包括空间测试时训练（S-TTT）层和频率测试时训练（F-TTT）层。S-TTT层捕获并校正空间结构退化，而F-TTT层则抑制全局噪声谱并恢复精细的高频细节。大量实验表明，U-TTT在PET去噪中取得了最先进的性能，并在包括未见剂量水平和未见扫描仪在内的具有挑战性的分布偏移下展现出优越的泛化能力。我们的代码将在https://github.com/Yaziwel/U-TTT上提供。

</details>


### [83] [IDEAL: In-DEpth ALignment Makes A Discrete Representation AutoEncoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11096)
*Yitong Chen,Zijie Diao,Junke Wang,Lingyu Kong,Yixuan Ren,Bo He,Yu-Gang Jiang,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Ideal框架，通过联合对齐离散视觉token与浅层和深层VFM特征，在保持丰富语义的同时恢复细粒度视觉细节，在图像重建和自回归生成上均达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于预训练视觉基础模型（VFM）的表示自编码器（RAE）在构建图像生成潜在空间时，重建质量不佳，主要因为深层VFM表示丢失了细粒度视觉细节，且量化后这一问题更加严重。而浅层VFM特征保留了丰富的局部外观和结构细节，与深层特征的高层语义互补。

Method: 提出Ideal（In-depth Alignment框架），通过将量化后的离散token同时与VFM的浅层和深层特征进行联合对齐，使离散视觉token同时保留视觉保真度和丰富语义。

Result: 在ImageNet上重建rFID达到0.61，比此前最佳方法提升0.28；用于自回归图像生成时gFID达到1.89，创下自回归图像生成新SOTA。

Conclusion: 利用浅层与深层VFM特征的互补性进行联合对齐，能显著提升离散表示自编码的重建质量和生成性能。

Original Abstract: Built on pretrained vision foundation models (VFMs), representation autoencoders (RAEs) have recently emerged as a promising approach for constructing semantically rich latent spaces for image generation. However, their reconstruction quality often remains suboptimal, largely because deep VFM representations do not preserve sufficient fine-grained visual detail. This limitation becomes even more severe after discretization, where missing low-level information is difficult to recover. In fact, we observe that shallow VFM features retain considerably richer local appearance and structural detail, which complements the high-level semantics carried by deep features used in existing RAEs. Motivated by this complementary property, we propose Ideal, an In-depth Alignment framework for discrete representation autoencoding. By jointly aligning quantized tokens with both shallow and deep VFM features, Ideal enables the resulting discrete visual tokens to preserve both visual fidelity and rich semantics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Ideal yields superior reconstruction performance, achieving 0.61 rFID on ImageNet and outperforming the previous best method by 0.28. When used for autoregressive image generation, Ideal further produces a gFID of 1.89, establishing a new state of the art for autoregressive image generation.

中文摘要翻译: 基于预训练视觉基础模型（VFM）构建的表示自编码器（RAE）近期已成为一种构建图像生成语义丰富潜在空间的有前景方法。然而，其重建质量往往仍不理想，主要原因是深层VFM表示未能保留足够的细粒度视觉细节。这种局限在离散化后变得更加严重，缺失的低层信息难以恢复。事实上，我们观察到浅层VFM特征保留了更丰富的局部外观和结构细节，这与现有RAE中使用的深层特征所携带的高层语义形成互补。受这一互补特性的启发，我们提出了Ideal，一种用于离散表示自编码的深度对齐框架。通过将量化后的token与浅层和深层VFM特征联合对齐，Ideal使生成的离散视觉token既能保持视觉保真度，又能保留丰富的语义信息。大量实验表明，Ideal取得了优越的重建性能，在ImageNet上达到0.61 rFID，比此前最佳方法提升0.28。当用于自回归图像生成时，Ideal进一步取得了1.89的gFID，创下了自回归图像生成的新最先进水平。

</details>


### [84] [FADA: Accessible fetal ultrasound interpretation and annotation with a selectively distilled unified vision-language model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11106)
*Mahmood Alzubaidi,Uzair Shah,Raden Muaz,Ines Abbes,Nader Mohammed,Abdullatif Magram,Khalid Alyafei,Mowafa Househ,Marco Agus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FADA是一个基于Qwen3.5-VL的统一视觉-语言模型，通过单一解释优先的流水线实现产前超声的临床解读、分类、检测和分割，无需外部标签；通过选择性蒸馏和边缘部署（0.8B模型可在手机上60秒离线运行），为资源匮乏地区提供了可及的AI辅助胎儿评估方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 全球范围内 trained 超声技师短缺，导致中低收入国家超半数孕妇无法获得专业产前超声筛查。现有深度学习方法各自独立处理检测、分割或分类任务，每个任务都需要单独的模型和专家指定的标签，无法形成统一、实用的临床工作流。

Method: FADA基于Qwen3.5-VL构建统一视觉-语言模型，采用解释优先流水线。通过离线预计算特征缓存从四个领域专用基础模型（FetalCLIP, UltraSAM, USF-MAE, UltraFedFM）蒸馏知识。提出选择性蒸馏策略：注释任务（分类、检测、分割）进行特征对齐，而解释任务依赖标准微调。推荐变体为FADA-SKD。

Result: 分割平均Dice达0.8820，检测mAP@0.50达0.7671，结构化解释合规率100%。237张图像的专家验证确认临床可接受输出，在临床医生指导下73.5%的解释获得完美评分。压缩后的0.8B模型可在骁龙7 Gen 1手机（12GB RAM）上离线运行，完整5阶段流水线约60秒完成。

Conclusion: FADA建立了将AI辅助胎儿评估与便携式超声设备集成的实用路径，直接解决了资源受限环境中诊断可及性差距。系统可在单张消费级GPU上训练、无需云连接即可部署，为产前超声筛查的普惠化提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: A global shortage of trained sonographers limits prenatal ultrasound screening in low- and middle-income countries, where over half of pregnant women receive no skilled sonography. Current deep learning approaches address detection, segmentation, or classification in isolation, each demanding a separate model and expert-specified labels at inference. We present FADA, a unified vision-language model built on Qwen3.5-VL that performs clinical interpretation, classification, detection, and segmentation through a single interpretation-first pipeline without external labels. FADA distills knowledge from four domain-specific foundation models (FetalCLIP, UltraSAM, USF-MAE, UltraFedFM) via offline pre-computed feature caching. Selective distillation, which applies feature alignment only to annotation tasks while interpretation relies on standard fine-tuning, consistently outperforms full distillation across most evaluation axes. The recommended variant, FADA-SKD, achieves 0.8820 mean Dice for segmentation, 0.7671 mAP@0.50 for detection, and 100% structured interpretation compliance. Expert sonographer validation across 237 images confirms clinically acceptable outputs in both autonomous and human-in-the-loop modes, with 73.5% of interpretations scoring perfectly under clinician guidance. The system is trainable on a single consumer GPU and deployable without cloud connectivity. We validate edge deployment by running the compressed 0.8B model on a commodity smartphone (Qualcomm Snapdragon 7 Gen 1, 12 GB RAM) using llama.cpp with GGUF quantization, completing the full 5-phase pipeline in approximately 60 seconds entirely offline. This establishes a practical pathway for integrating AI-assisted fetal assessment with portable ultrasound devices, directly addressing diagnostic access gaps in resource-constrained settings. Code, models, and data are available at https://github.com/mahmoodphd/FADA.

中文摘要翻译: 训练有素的超声技师在全球范围内的短缺限制了中低收入国家的产前超声筛查，这些国家超过半数的孕妇无法获得专业超声检查。当前的深度学习方法各自独立处理检测、分割或分类任务，每个任务都需要单独的模型和推理时由专家指定的标签。我们提出FADA，一个基于Qwen3.5-VL构建的统一视觉-语言模型，通过单一的解释优先流水线执行临床解读、分类、检测和分割，无需外部标签。FADA通过离线预计算特征缓存，从四个领域专用基础模型（FetalCLIP、UltraSAM、USF-MAE、UltraFedFM）蒸馏知识。选择性蒸馏策略——仅对注释任务应用特征对齐，而解读任务依赖标准微调——在大多数评估指标上持续优于完全蒸馏。推荐变体FADA-SKD在分割任务上达到0.8820的平均Dice系数，检测任务上达到0.7671的mAP@0.50，结构化解读合规率达到100%。专家超声技师对237张图像的验证确认了其在自主模式和人在环路模式下均能产生临床可接受的输出，在临床医生指导下73.5%的解读获得完美评分。该系统可在单张消费级GPU上训练，无需云连接即可部署。我们通过将压缩后的0.8B模型在商用智能手机（高通骁龙7 Gen 1，12 GB RAM）上使用llama.cpp结合GGUF量化运行来验证边缘部署能力，完整5阶段流水线在完全离线条件下约60秒完成。这为将AI辅助胎儿评估与便携式超声设备集成建立了实用路径，直接解决了资源受限环境中的诊断可及性差距。代码、模型和数据可在https://github.com/mahmoodphd/FADA获取。

</details>


### [85] [WorldOlympiad: Can Your World Model Survive a Triathlon?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11129)
*Yuke Zhao,Wangbo Zhao,Weijie Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Dakai An,Akide Liu,Yinghao Yu,Jiasheng Tang,Fan Wang,Wei Wang,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldOlympiad基准，从物理真实性、几何一致性和交互保真度三个维度系统评估视频生成世界模型，揭示现有模型在物理推理、3D一致性和长程交互方面的显著不足。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频生成评估基准主要关注视觉质量、语义对齐或短期时间一致性，缺乏对生成视频是否遵循物理规则、保持连贯3D结构以及支持长程可控交互的系统性评估。

Method: WorldOlympiad将世界模型评估分解为三个互补维度：物理轨迹（利用目标分割和MLLM评判器评估力学、热学、材料属性）、几何轨迹（利用3D高斯泼溅重建评估结构一致性、跨视角一致性和相机轨迹对齐）、交互轨迹（评估模型是否遵循复杂动作提示并维持连续视频块间的平滑过渡）。覆盖游戏、机器人和通用真实世界视频三大下游场景。

Result: 在最先进模型上的实验表明，现有模型在物理推理、3D一致性和长程交互方面存在显著差距，暴露了超越通用视频质量的失效模式。

Conclusion: 需要为生成式世界模型设计更加结构化的评估协议，以推动模型在物理规则遵循、3D结构保持和可控交互方面的能力提升。

Original Abstract: We introduce WorldOlympiad, a benchmark for diagnosing video-based world models across physical faithfulness, geometric consistency, and interaction fidelity. While existing benchmarks often focus on visual quality, semantic alignment, or short-term temporal coherence, they provide limited insight into whether generated videos obey physical rules, preserve coherent 3D structure, and sustain controllable interactions over long horizons. To address this gap, WorldOlympiad decomposes world-model evaluation into three complementary dimensions. The physical track uses object segmentation and MLLM-as-judge to assess whether generated videos follow interpretable rules in mechanics, thermal phenomena, and material properties. The geometry track reconstructs generated videos with Gaussian splatting and evaluates structural consistency, cross-view coherence, and camera-trajectory alignment. The interaction track assesses whether generated rollouts follow complex action prompts and maintain smooth, coherent transitions across consecutive video chunks. WorldOlympiad further covers three major downstream scenarios, including gaming, robotics, and general real-world videos, capturing diverse challenges from interactive control and embodied manipulation to open-domain motion and camera dynamics. Together, these tracks and scenarios form a scalable and interpretable evaluation suite that exposes failure modes beyond generic video quality. Experiments on state-of-the-art models reveal substantial gaps in physical reasoning, 3D consistency, and long-horizon interaction, underscoring the need for more structured evaluation protocols for generative world models.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了WorldOlympiad，一个用于诊断基于视频的世界模型在物理真实性、几何一致性和交互保真度方面的基准。现有基准通常关注视觉质量、语义对齐或短期时间一致性，但它们对于生成视频是否遵循物理规则、保持连贯的3D结构以及在长程范围内维持可控交互的洞察有限。为填补这一空白，WorldOlympiad将世界模型评估分解为三个互补维度。物理轨迹利用目标分割和多模态大语言模型评判器，评估生成视频在力学、热现象和材料属性方面是否遵循可解释的规则。几何轨迹通过3D高斯泼溅重建生成视频，评估结构一致性、跨视角一致性和相机轨迹对齐。交互轨迹评估生成序列是否遵循复杂动作提示，并在连续视频块之间维持平滑、连贯的过渡。WorldOlympiad还涵盖三大下游场景，包括游戏、机器人和通用真实世界视频，捕捉从交互控制和具身操作到开放域运动和相机动态的多样化挑战。这些轨迹和场景共同构成了一个可扩展且可解释的评估套件，能够揭示超越通用视频质量的失效模式。在最新模型上的实验揭示了物理推理、3D一致性和长程交互方面的显著差距，突显了为生成式世界模型设计更结构化评估协议的必要性。

</details>


### [86] [UniPET: a universal network for high-quality PET image denoising across varied dose reduction factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11131)
*Zhiwen Yang,Yang Zhou,Haowei Chen,Hui Zhang,Dan Zhao,Bingzheng Wei,Yan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniPET，一种基于域泛化的通用PET图像去噪网络，通过风格对齐网络（SAN）和区域感知学习策略（RALS）解决不同剂量衰减因子（DRF）下低剂量PET图像去噪中的风格错位和过度平滑问题，实现跨DRF的高质量去噪。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PET图像去噪方法通常假设固定的剂量衰减因子（DRF），当实际应用中DRF变化时性能显著下降。虽然已有研究尝试训练通用模型处理不同DRF，但这些模型因不同DRF数据间的风格错位，出现“风格消除问题”，导致严重的过度平滑效应。因此，需要一种能自适应恢复不同DRF风格并实现高质量去噪的通用方法。

Method: UniPET包含两个核心创新：（1）风格对齐网络（SAN），利用域泛化中的风格对齐技术，在不同DRF数据间对齐并恢复风格，确保模型跨DRF的泛化能力；（2）区域感知学习策略（RALS），区分平坦区域和风格化区域，仅在风格化区域上进行对抗学习，引导模型更有效地学习风格化区域，增强风格恢复效果。

Result: 综合实验表明，UniPET在特定DRF下能达到与专用DRF模型相当的性能，并在通用PET图像去噪任务中实现了最先进的性能，定量指标、感知质量和临床评估均表现优异。

Conclusion: UniPET能够自适应地恢复不同DRF的风格，实现跨DRF的高质量PET图像去噪，为通用PET去噪提供了有效解决方案。

Original Abstract: Most existing deep learning-based PET image denoising methods assume a fixed and known dose reduction factor (DRF) for low-dose PET images. However, these methods encounter significant performance degradation when the DRF varies beyond the assumed one in practical applications. To address the challenge posed by varied DRFs, several preliminary studies focus on the task of universal PET image denoising, aiming to train a universal model over low-dose data across DRFs. Nonetheless, these vanilla universal models often struggle with misaligned styles present in different DRF data, leading to the \textit{style elimination issue} with a significant over-smoothing effect. To deal with this issue, we innovatively introduce domain generalization to PET image denoising and propose a universal PET image denoising network (UniPET) to achieve high-quality PET image denoising across diverse DRFs. UniPET comprises two primary innovations: a style alignment network (SAN) and a region-aware learning strategy (RALS). Specifically, SAN utilizes style alignment techniques derived from domain generalization to align and recover styles across different DRFs, ensuring the model's generalizability across various DRFs while effectively preserving styles. Furthermore, to enhance style recovery, RALS distinguishes between flat and stylized regions, exclusively conducting adversarial learning on the latter, thereby more effectively guiding the model's focus towards learning stylized regions. It is demonstrated that our proposed UniPET can adaptively recover different DRF styles and achieve high-quality PET image denoising across DRFs. Comprehensive experiments show that UniPET exhibits comparable performance to individual DRF-specific models at specific DRFs and realizes state-of-the-art performance in universal PET image denoising quantitatively, perceptually, and clinically.

中文摘要翻译: 大多数基于深度学习的PET图像去噪方法假设低剂量PET图像具有固定且已知的剂量衰减因子（DRF）。然而，当实际应用中DRF超出假设范围时，这些方法会出现显著的性能下降。为应对不同DRF带来的挑战，一些初步研究聚焦于通用PET图像去噪任务，旨在跨DRF的低剂量数据上训练通用模型。然而，这些朴素通用模型常常难以处理不同DRF数据中存在的风格错位问题，导致出现“风格消除问题”，并伴随严重的过度平滑效应。为解决该问题，我们创新地将域泛化引入PET图像去噪领域，并提出了一个通用PET图像去噪网络（UniPET），以实现跨不同DRF的高质量PET图像去噪。UniPET包含两个主要创新：风格对齐网络（SAN）和区域感知学习策略（RALS）。具体而言，SAN利用源自域泛化的风格对齐技术，在不同DRF之间对齐并恢复风格，确保模型在多种DRF下的泛化能力，同时有效保留风格。此外，为增强风格恢复效果，RALS区分平坦区域和风格化区域，仅在风格化区域上进行对抗学习，从而更有效地引导模型专注于学习风格化区域。实验证明，我们提出的UniPET能够自适应地恢复不同DRF风格，实现跨DRF的高质量PET图像去噪。综合实验表明，UniPET在特定DRF下展现出与专用DRF模型相当的性能，并在通用PET图像去噪任务中实现了最先进的定量、感知和临床性能。

</details>


### [87] [MOFA-VTON: More Fashion Possibilities with Fine-Grained Adaptations in Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11148)
*Xiaoyu Han,Chenyang Wang,Jing Wang,Shunyuan Zheng,Quanling Meng,Shengping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOFA-VTON 是一种通过用户简单草图实现细粒度服装适配的虚拟试穿方法，利用曲线草图生成双区域掩码替代传统掩码，并通过布局调整块优化空间排列，在 VITON-HD 和 DressCode 数据集上超越现有方法，提供更多时尚可能性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有虚拟试穿方法仅进行固定模式的直接替换，缺乏对服装适配方式的灵活控制，导致试穿结果单调、缺乏多样性，无法满足真实场景中多样化的穿着风格和个性化时尚需求。

Method: 1) 提出掩码构建策略，将用户绘制的曲线草图转换为双区域掩码，替代传统服装无关掩码，提供细粒度布局引导；2) 提出布局调整块，利用交叉注意力机制独立学习人体上半身和下半身的布局对应关系，细化两个区域的空间排列。

Result: 在 VITON-HD 和 DressCode 数据集上的大量实验表明，MOFA-VTON 优于之前的 state-of-the-art 方法，为虚拟试穿提供了更多时尚可能性。

Conclusion: MOFA-VTON 通过简单用户草图实现了灵活且细粒度的服装适配，克服了固定布局的约束，为虚拟试穿带来了更丰富的时尚可能性。

Original Abstract: Virtual try-on aims to fit an in-shop clothing image onto a specific human body. An optimal virtual try-on method should provide diverse and flexible dressing options, accurately reflecting the varied wearing styles encountered in real-life scenarios, tailored to individual preferences and fashion aspirations. However, current methods predominantly perform a direct replacement of the original clothing with the target clothing, following the same dressing pattern. This limited control over clothing adaptation may result in fixed and monotonous try-on outputs. To delve into More Fashion Possibilities with Fine-Grained Adaptations in Virtual Try-On, we propose a novel virtual try-on method, termed MOFA-VTON, which allows adjustment for clothing adaptations in try-on results through simple sketches by users. Specifically, we first design a mask construction strategy that transforms user-drawn curve sketches into a dual-region mask, replacing the traditional clothing-agnostic mask and providing fine-grained layout guidance for the subsequent generation process. Further, we propose layout adjustment blocks that utilize the cross-attention mechanism to independently learn layout correspondences for upper and lower regions of the human body, refining the spatial arrangement of the two regions. With these implementations, our method enables flexible and fine-grained adaptations of target clothing, overcoming the constraints of a fixed layout. Extensive experiments on VITON-HD and DressCode datasets demonstrate that our proposed MOFA-VTON outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods and provides more fashion possibilities for virtual try-on.

中文摘要翻译: 虚拟试穿旨在将商店内的服装图像适配到特定人体上。一个理想的虚拟试穿方法应提供多样且灵活的穿着选项，准确反映真实场景中遇到的各种穿着风格，并根据个人偏好和时尚诉求进行定制。然而，当前方法主要按照相同的穿着模式，将原始服装直接替换为目标服装。这种对服装适配的有限控制可能导致固定且单调的试穿输出。为了探索虚拟试穿中具有细粒度适配的更多时尚可能性，我们提出了一种新颖的虚拟试穿方法，称为 MOFA-VTON，该方法允许用户通过简单的草图调整试穿结果中的服装适配方式。具体来说，我们首先设计了一种掩码构建策略，将用户绘制的曲线草图转换为双区域掩码，替代传统的服装无关掩码，为后续生成过程提供细粒度的布局引导。此外，我们提出了布局调整块，利用交叉注意力机制独立学习人体上半身和下半身区域的布局对应关系，细化两个区域的空间排列。通过这些实现，我们的方法能够实现目标服装的灵活且细粒度的适配，克服了固定布局的约束。在 VITON-HD 和 DressCode 数据集上进行的大量实验表明，我们提出的 MOFA-VTON 优于之前的 state-of-the-art 方法，为虚拟试穿提供了更多时尚可能性。

</details>


### [88] [P3D-Bench: Benchmarking MLLMs for Parametric 3D Generation and Structural Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11152)
*Yikang Yang,Zhanpeng Hu,Youtian Lin,Mengqi Zhou,Jingxi Xu,Feihu Zhang,Jiaheng Liu,Yao Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了P3D-Bench基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型通过代码生成参数化3D程序的能力，涵盖文本、图像和装配三种任务，发现模型在装配推理和精确参数几何方面表现较弱。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准很少评估通过代码进行3D建模的能力，而这种任务不仅要求生成可执行代码，还需要模型从文本或视觉规范生成几何精确、语义对齐且装配一致的参数化3D程序。

Method: P3D-Bench基准在统一协议下覆盖三类任务（Text-to-3D、Image-to-3D和Assembly-3D），对每个输出从可执行性、几何保真度、拓扑结构、文本约束、多视角语义对齐和部件级结构六个维度进行评分。

Result: 在400个文本案例、400个图像案例和203个带注释的装配体上评估前沿MLLM和纯文本LLM，得到三个发现：(1) 装配是最难的设置，模型无法将多个部件组合成连贯结构；(2) 模型能恢复全局形状和语义身份，但无法复现精确的参数化几何；(3) 部件级建模在装配任务上较弱，既无法恢复每个部件的几何也无法恢复正确的部件数量。

Conclusion: P3D-Bench可作为评估参数化3D生成中精确参数几何和部件级结构的基准，揭示了当前模型在精密几何理解和装配推理方面的局限性。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models can write code to produce complex programs as well as use programs to do 3D modeling, which opens up a new avenue for 3D generation powered by their priors, world knowledge and reasoning. Yet existing benchmarks rarely evaluate 3D modeling through code. Such modeling demands more than runnable code: from a text or visual specification, a model must generate a parametric 3D program that is geometrically precise, semantically aligned and assembly-consistent. We introduce P3D-Bench, a benchmark for parametric 3D generation.
