中文摘要翻译: 在时间序列生成领域，现有方法通常为每个数据集手工设计或训练单独的模型，这阻碍了其可扩展性，也无法跨领域利用共享的时间结构。为了解决这种碎片化问题，我们提出了UPLOTS——一个统一的、基于提示引导的语言模型框架，用于跨不同领域的有约束时间序列生成。UPLOTS不构建特定任务的模型，而是利用由学习到的约束提示引导的单个预训练Transformer骨干网络，实现了具有精确模式控制的按需生成。其中一个关键创新是我们的动态多数据集损失重加权和提示到模式映射技术，这使得UPLOTS能够在训练过程中内化多种时间结构，并在推理时有条件地生成它们。我们在四个真实世界基准和多种约束设置（包括峰值周期、日历、负载水平和波动模式）上评估了UPLOTS。额外的保留约束组合和下游预测实验进一步证明，UPLOTS超越了原始峰值模式设置，并在真实数据稀缺场景下改善了数据增强效果。我们的代码和基线可在匿名GitHub仓库获取：https://anonymous.4open.science/r/UPLOTS-6C36。

</details>


### [206] [Advancing the State-of-the-Art in Empirical Privacy Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10481)
*Nicole Mitchell,Galen Andrew,Arun Ganesh,Brendan McMahan,Peter Kairouz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出通过高温采样生成合成“金丝雀”样本，以增强大语言模型微调中的隐私审计，并引入基于辅助模型微调的合成数据审计方法，系统研究了模型容量和金丝雀熵对记忆化的交互影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 参数高效微调的大语言模型存在记忆单个训练样本的风险，而现有的经验性隐私审计方法在设计与隐私敏感训练数据混合的“金丝雀”样本方面存在挑战。

Method: (1) 通过高温采样（T≥0.8）从LLM生成合成金丝雀样本，并使用针对隐私敏感训练数据定制的提示；(2) 提出基于在合成数据上微调辅助模型来审计合成数据隐私泄露的方法。

Result: 合成金丝雀作为高影响异常值，具有高可识别性，从而提供强审计能力；辅助模型审计方法能有效估计通过合成数据产生的隐私泄露；系统研究了模型容量与金丝雀熵对记忆化的交互影响。

Conclusion: 合成金丝雀是非私有的、可检查的，可重复插入而不危及真实数据的隐私，为LLM微调中的隐私审计提供了实用且强大的方法。

Original Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) can exhibit problematic memorization of individual training examples. Empirical privacy auditing (EPA) quantifies this risk by measuring realistic data leakage on membership inference (MI) or reconstruction attacks. A key challenge in EPA is designing ``canary'' examples that are mixed with the privacy-sensitive training data. We propose generating synthetic canaries via high-temperature sampling ($T \geq 0.8$) from LLMs, using prompts tailored to the privacy-sensitive training data. These canaries act as high-influence outliers, ensuring high identifiability and hence strong audits. Further, since the canaries are themselves non-private, they are inspectable and can be inserted with repetition without jeopardizing the privacy of the real data. An important use of models fine-tuned on privacy-sensitive data is the generation of synthetic data. This also comes with privacy risk. We introduce a powerful synthetic data audit based on fine-tuning an auxiliary model on the synthetic data. Auditing the auxiliary model for the original canaries then provides a strong estimate of the privacy leakage through the synthetic data. Finally, leveraging our strong auditing methodologies, we perform a systematic investigation into the interacting effects of model capacity and canary entropy on memorization.

中文摘要翻译: 参数高效微调大语言模型（LLM）会表现出对单个训练样本的问题性记忆。经验性隐私审计（EPA）通过度量成员推断（MI）或重构攻击上的实际数据泄露来量化这一风险。EPA的一个关键挑战是如何设计与隐私敏感训练数据混合的“金丝雀”样本。我们提出通过从LLM中进行高温采样（T≥0.8），并使用针对隐私敏感训练数据定制的提示来生成合成金丝雀。这些金丝雀作为高影响异常值，确保了高可识别性，从而提供强审计能力。此外，由于金丝雀本身是非私有的，它们是可检查的，并且可以重复插入而不会危及真实数据的隐私。在隐私敏感数据上微调的模型的一个重要用途是生成合成数据，这也带来了隐私风险。我们提出了一种强大的合成数据审计方法，其基于在合成数据上微调一个辅助模型。审计该辅助模型中的原始金丝雀，可以为通过合成数据产生的隐私泄露提供强有力的估计。最后，利用我们强大的审计方法，我们系统研究了模型容量和金丝雀熵对记忆化的交互影响。

</details>


### [207] [Stop Early, Spend Less: Hidden-State Probes as a Practical Recipe for Streaming Moderation of LLM Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10487)
*Huizhen Shu,Xuying Li,Piao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于LLM内部隐层状态的轻量级token级安全探测器，可在解码循环中实时检测不安全输出，避免后处理的双倍推理开销。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有安全过滤方法依赖生成后额外运行一个审查模型，导致推理成本翻倍且只能在生成完成后检测违规，延迟高、效率低。

Method: 利用LLM中间层隐藏状态中已存在的安全信号，训练轻量级token级线性探针，直接在内部激活上产生逐token安全分数，无需额外前向传播，可在解码循环内以亚毫秒级速度进行安全检查。

Result: 单个中层探针即可恢复强审查模型的大部分决策能力；与后处理和流式审查模型相比，计算开销降低数个数量级，延迟成本极小；探针的线性分量对应残差空间中的一个方向，可同时用于检测和激活引导。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为高延迟审查模型的高效低成本替代方案，实现连续token级监控，在流式生成中可提前中断或修改不安全输出。

Original Abstract: Deploying large language models in user-facing systems requires efficient output safety filtering. Existing approaches typically rely on a separate moderation model applied after generation, which doubles inference cost and only detects violations after generation completes. We observe that the signal needed for moderation is already present in the model hidden states. Based on this, we train lightweight token-level probes that operate directly on internal activations, producing per-token safety scores that can be aggregated for both offline evaluation and online intervention. The probe reuses activations from the generator and requires no additional forward pass, enabling sub millisecond per-token safety checks inside the decoding loop. A probe applied to a single mid layer recovers most decisions of a strong guard model, acting as a low cost surrogate optimized for latency rather than accuracy. In streaming settings, it can halt or modify unsafe outputs before they are fully generated, replacing end of sequence moderation with continuous token level monitoring. Compared to post hoc and streaming guard models, our method achieves orders of magnitude lower compute overhead with minimal latency cost. We also provide a practical deployment recipe, including layer selection, aggregation strategy, probing frequency, and triggering thresholds. Finally, we show that the probe linear component corresponds to a direction in residual space, enabling both detection and activation steering at negligible cost.

中文摘要翻译: 在面向用户的系统中部署大语言模型需要高效的输出安全过滤。现有方法通常依赖在生成后单独运行的审查模型，这不仅使推理成本翻倍，而且只能在生成完成后检测违规。我们发现审查所需的信号已经存在于模型的隐藏状态中。基于此，我们训练了轻量级的token级探针，直接作用于内部激活，生成逐token的安全分数，这些分数可被聚合用于离线评估和在线干预。该探针复用生成器的激活，无需额外的前向传播，从而在解码循环内实现亚毫秒级的逐token安全检查。应用于单个中间层的探针能够恢复强审查模型的大部分决策能力，成为一种以延迟而非准确性为优化目标的低成本替代方案。在流式场景中，它可以在不安全输出完全生成之前暂停或修改它们，用连续的token级监控取代序列结束后的审查。与后处理和流式审查模型相比，我们的方法计算开销降低了数个数量级，延迟成本极小。我们还提供了一套实用的部署方案，包括层选择、聚合策略、探测频率和触发阈值。最后，我们证明探针的线性分量对应残差空间中的一个方向，从而能够以极低的成本同时实现检测和激活引导。

</details>


### [208] [MoE Enhanced Federated Learning for Spatiotemporal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10499)
*Zhehao Dai,Xiao Han,Zhaolin Deng,Zijian Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao,Guojiang Shen,Xiangjie Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MoE-FedTP，一种基于轻量级混合专家网络（MoE）的个性化联邦跨城市时空预测框架，通过动态门控融合多源城市专家知识，在保护隐私的同时有效建模城市异质性，提升数据稀缺城市的交通预测精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 许多城市因传感器部署有限和城市发展不均衡而面临交通数据稀缺问题，现有集中式跨城市知识迁移方法存在隐私风险，而联邦学习方法难以应对城市间显著的时空异质性。

Method: 首先使用时空神经网络提取源城市和目标城市的特征；然后通过部分参数共享从不同源城市派生出多个专家网络；最后引入门控机制动态融合各专家输出，以捕获多样化的交通动态，实现细粒度的城市异质性建模，同时通过联邦学习保护数据隐私。

Result: 在四个真实交通数据集上，MoE-FedTP consistently outperforms state-of-the-art cross-city and federated learning baselines，显著提升了数据稀缺城市的预测准确性。

Conclusion: MoE-FedTP通过轻量级MoE网络和门控机制有效解决了联邦跨城市时空预测中的隐私保护与城市异质性挑战，为数据稀缺城市提供了一种高效、准确且隐私友好的交通预测方案。

Original Abstract: Traffic prediction is fundamental to intelligent transportation systems and urban computing, yet many cities continue to suffer from traffic data scarcity due to limited sensor deployment and uneven urban development. Cross-city knowledge transfer has thus attracted increasing attention, enabling data-rich cities to assist data-scarce ones. However, centralized approaches raise privacy concerns, while existing federated methods struggle with pronounced spatiotemporal heterogeneity across cities. To address these challenges, we propose MoE-FedTP, a personalized federated cross-city spatiotemporal prediction framework based on lightweight Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) networks. MoE-FedTP first employs spatiotemporal neural networks to extract features from both source and target cities, then introduces a set of expert networks derived from different source cities through partial parameter sharing. A gating mechanism dynamically fuses the experts to capture diverse traffic dynamics, achieving fine-grained modeling of urban heterogeneity while preserving privacy. Experiments on four real-world traffic datasets show that MoE-FedTP consistently outperforms state-of-the-art cross-city and federated learning baselines, demonstrating its effectiveness in enhancing prediction accuracy for data-scarce cities.

中文摘要翻译: 交通预测是智能交通系统和城市计算的基础，然而由于传感器部署有限和城市发展不均衡，许多城市仍然面临交通数据稀缺的问题。跨城市知识迁移因此受到越来越多的关注，使得数据丰富的城市能够帮助数据稀缺的城市。然而，集中式方法引发了隐私担忧，而现有的联邦方法难以应对城市间显著的时空异质性。为应对这些挑战，我们提出MoE-FedTP，一种基于轻量级混合专家网络（Mixture-of-Experts, MoE）的个性化联邦跨城市时空预测框架。MoE-FedTP首先采用时空神经网络提取源城市和目标城市的特征，然后通过部分参数共享引入一组源自不同源城市的专家网络。门控机制动态融合各专家以捕获多样化的交通动态，在保护隐私的同时实现城市异质性的细粒度建模。在四个真实交通数据集上的实验表明，MoE-FedTP持续优于最先进的跨城市和联邦学习基线方法，证明了其在提升数据稀缺城市预测准确性方面的有效性。

</details>


### [209] [Representation-Aware Advantage Estimation: Your Reward Model Provides More Than A Scalar Output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10528)
*Guozheng Li,Xiyan Fu,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GraphAE方法，利用奖励模型（RM）的隐藏状态作为辅助信号，通过图传播机制改进组级强化学习中的优势估计，在多个基准上取得显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RLHF方法依赖标量奖励信号，但标量奖励噪声大且无法捕捉细粒度的偏好差异，而RM隐藏状态包含更丰富的语义和偏好信息，因此希望利用RM隐藏状态来改进优势估计。

Method: 提出基于图的优势估计（GraphAE）方法：将每组采样响应构建为图，节点为响应，边为RM隐藏空间中的相似度；通过图传播计算每个样本的优势值，使其能融入邻居的上下文信息。该方法轻量级且可无缝集成到GRPO、GSPO、RLOO等现有组级RL算法中。

Result: 在Arena-Hard-v0.1上提升高达+6.3，AlpacaEval 2.0上+8.27，MT-Bench上+0.22，表明利用RM表示能实现更高效、更鲁棒的RLHF。

Conclusion: 利用RM隐藏状态作为辅助信号进行基于图的优势估计，可以显著提升组级RLHF算法的样本效率和鲁棒性，GraphAE是一种轻量且有效的改进方案。

Original Abstract: Current reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) methods primarily rely on scalar rewards from a trained reward model (RM). While effective, scalar rewards are often noisy and fail to capture fine-grained preference differences, whereas RM hidden states encode richer semantic and preference information. We introduce the representation-aware advantage estimation, which leverages RM hidden states and models them as auxiliary signals for better advantage estimation. Specifically, we propose the Graph-based Advantage Estimation (GraphAE), treat each sampled group as a graph, where nodes correspond to responses and edges capture their similarity in the RM hidden space. Then advantages are computed via graph propagation, enabling each sample to incorporate contextual information from its neighbors. GraphAE is lightweight and can be seamlessly integrated into existing group-based RL algorithms. We apply GraphAE to GRPO, GSPO and RLOO, and conduct extensive experiments on different models and benchmarks. Empirical results show consistent improvements across three benchmarks, with gains of up to + 6.3 on Arena-Hard-v0.1, + 8.27 on AlpacaEval 2.0, and + 0.22 on MT-Bench. These results demonstrate that leveraging RM representations leads to more sample efficient and robust RLHF.

中文摘要翻译: 当前基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）方法主要依赖于从训练好的奖励模型（RM）中获取的标量奖励。尽管有效，但标量奖励通常噪声较大，且难以捕捉细粒度的偏好差异，而RM的隐藏状态则编码了更丰富的语义和偏好信息。我们提出了表示感知的优势估计方法，该方法利用RM的隐藏状态并将其建模为辅助信号，以实现更优的优势估计。具体而言，我们提出了基于图的优势估计（GraphAE），将每个采样组视为一个图，其中节点对应响应，边捕获它们在RM隐藏空间中的相似性。然后通过图传播计算优势值，使每个样本能够纳入来自其邻居的上下文信息。GraphAE轻量级且可无缝集成到现有的组级RL算法中。我们将GraphAE应用于GRPO、GSPO和RLOO，并在不同模型和基准上进行了大量实验。实验结果表明，在三个基准上均取得了一致的改进，在Arena-Hard-v0.1上提升高达+6.3，在AlpacaEval 2.0上提升+8.27，在MT-Bench上提升+0.22。这些结果表明，利用RM表示能够实现更高效、更鲁棒的RLHF。

</details>


### [210] [Machine Learning Methods for Studying Latent Neural Activity Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10530)
*Shufeng Kong,Fumei Deng,Xinyi Dong,Caihua Liu,Weiwei Chen,Yingheng Wang,Daniel Cao,Azahara Oliva,Antonio Fernandez-Ruiz,Carla Gomes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了从早期状态空间模型到现代深度生成模型的潜变量模型（LVM）在神经解码中的发展轨迹，涵盖单区域潜动力学、多区域通信以及行为对齐建模三大领域，并讨论了大规模神经基础模型（如Transformer和扩散模型）以及未来的基准测试与开放挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 脑记录技术的快速发展迫切需要能够解码大规模神经元群体潜在结构的机器学习工具，本文旨在系统梳理潜变量模型在神经科学中的应用演变脉络，为相关研究提供全面的文献综述。

Method: 本文采用文献综述的方法，将相关研究组织为三个紧密相关的领域：单区域潜动力学（线性动力系统、RNN、神经常微分方程）、多区域通信（概率方法与子空间方法）和行为对齐建模（监督或对比学习），并涵盖大规模预训练基础模型（Transformer、扩散模型）。

Result: 综述了从线性状态空间模型到深度生成模型的演进路径；总结了多区域通信中考虑突触传播延迟和网络连接的信息传递方法；梳理了通过监督/对比学习分离任务相关神经活动的方法；分析了大规模神经基础模型在不同主体间的最优性能表现。

Conclusion: 本文总结了现有基准测试和评估标准，并指出未来研究方向——包括识别因果连接或通信方向性——以促进可解释的脑动力学与可靠的神经解码之间的桥梁建设。

Original Abstract: Recent developments in brain recording are driving a demand for machine learning tools capable of decoding the latent structure of large populations of neurons. In this paper, we provide a comprehensive survey that outlines the trajectory of Latent Variable Models (LVMs) from early state-space models to more recent deep generative models. We organize the literature into three closely related domains: (1) Single-Region Latent Dynamics, which includes models such as linear dynamical systems to more complex dynamics represented by Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) and Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs); (2) Multi-Region Communication, which employs probabilistic as well as subspace methods to study how information is transferred across different brain areas considering synaptic propagation delays and network connectivity; and (3) Behavior-Aligned Modeling, which seeks to disentangle neural activity related to task performance from other internal states via supervised or contrastive learning. This survey also includes large-scale neural foundation models, such as Transformers and diffusion models, that rely on large-scale pre-training for optimal performance across subjects. Finally, we conclude and discuss benchmarks, evaluation criteria, and open challenges, such as the ability to identify causal links or directionality of communication, to facilitate future research for bridging interpretable brain dynamics with reliable neural decoding.

中文摘要翻译: 脑记录技术的近期发展推动了对能够解码大规模神经元群体潜在结构的机器学习工具的需求。本文提供了一项全面的综述，勾勒出潜变量模型（LVM）从早期状态空间模型到更近期深度生成模型的发展轨迹。我们将文献组织为三个紧密相关的领域：（1）单区域潜动力学，涵盖从线性动力系统到由循环神经网络（RNN）和神经常微分方程所表示的更复杂动力学模型；（2）多区域通信，利用概率方法以及子空间方法来研究信息如何在不同脑区之间传递，同时考虑突触传播延迟和网络连接；（3）行为对齐建模，旨在通过监督或对比学习将与任务表现相关的神经活动从其他内部状态中分离出来。本综述还涵盖大规模神经基础模型，例如Transformer和扩散模型，这些模型依赖大规模预训练以实现跨主体的最优性能。最后，我们总结了基准测试、评估标准以及开放挑战，例如识别因果连接或通信方向性的能力，旨在促进未来研究以弥合可解释的脑动力学与可靠的神经解码之间的鸿沟。

</details>


### [211] [Flexible Flows for Biological Sequence Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10543)
*Yogesh Verma,Dani Korpela,Harri Lähdesmäki,Vikas Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结构化耦合的离散流匹配方法，结合潜在编辑速率参数化和分类器无引导机制，在变量长度生成和细粒度控制上实现领先性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有离散流匹配方法依赖生物信息学上无意义的耦合，且缺乏变长序列生成和细粒度控制的能力。

Method: 1) 结构化耦合编码序列元素间的领域偏好，偏置源分布至合理区域；2) 潜在编辑速率参数化，通过基于共享全局潜变量的编辑操作建模变长生成；3) 潜在分类器无引导机制在连续潜空间中引导生成；4) Dirichlet先验温度缩放实现测试时编辑操作控制。

Result: 在密度估计、无条件/条件DNA序列生成、肽序列生成等多种生物序列任务上取得了最先进性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法在不修改流目标和训练过程的情况下，通过结构化耦合和潜在控制机制显著提升了离散流匹配在生物序列生成任务上的性能和灵活性。

Original Abstract: Designing functional biological sequences requires navigating vast discrete spaces under strict evolutionary and biophysical constraints. Discrete Flow Matching (DFM) offers a generative framework over such spaces, but existing approaches rely on biologically uninformative couplings and offer limited flexibility for variable-length sequence generation and fine-grained control. We propose a structured coupling that encodes domain-specific preferences among sequence elements, biasing the source distribution toward plausible regions without modifying the flow objective or training procedure. Building on this, we introduce a latent edit-based rate parameterization that models variable-length generation via edit operations conditioned on a shared global latent, akin to a latent variable model, while remaining tractable. We further introduce a latent classifier-free guidance mechanism that steers generation coherently in continuous latent space, along with Dirichlet-prior temperature scaling for test-time control over edit operations. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse biological sequence tasks, including density estimation, unconditional and conditional DNA sequence generation, and peptide sequence generation.

中文摘要翻译: 设计功能性生物序列需要在严格的进化和生物物理约束下探索庞大的离散空间。离散流匹配（DFM）为此类空间提供了生成框架，但现有方法依赖于生物学上无信息的耦合，且对变长序列生成和细粒度控制的灵活性有限。我们提出一种结构化耦合，编码序列元素间的领域特定偏好，将源分布偏置向合理区域，同时不修改流目标或训练流程。在此基础上，我们引入一种基于潜在编辑的速率参数化，通过基于共享全局潜在变量的编辑操作对变长生成进行建模，类似于潜在变量模型，同时保持可计算性。我们进一步引入潜在分类器无引导机制，在连续潜在空间中连贯地引导生成，以及基于Dirichlet先验的温度缩放，用于对编辑操作进行测试时控制。我们的方法在多种生物序列任务上取得了最先进性能，包括密度估计、无条件与条件DNA序列生成以及肽序列生成。

</details>


### [212] [Convergence of Monte Carlo Optimistic Policy Iteration: Beyond Uniform State-Action Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10580)
*Octave Oliviers,Glenn Vinnicombe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文严格放宽了蒙特卡洛乐观策略迭代（MC-O-PI）收敛到最优性的条件，证明只需在每个状态下对动作进行均匀更新（而非整个状态-动作空间），算法仍可收敛到最优，这在实际大状态空间场景中更具可行性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MC-O-PI的渐近行为长期是开放问题。现有唯一保证收敛到最优性的条件要求episode在整个状态-动作空间均匀初始化，这在实践中不可行。本文旨在严格放宽这一要求。

Method: 首先证明当更新在每个状态下对动作均匀时，MC-O-PI的均场动态产生单调改进的策略；然后通过扩展组合稳定性-ODE方法的锁定论证（lock-in argument），证明噪声无法持续阻止这种改进。

Result: 证明了初始访问MC-O-PI（initial-visit MC-O-PI）在仅需每个状态下对动作均匀更新的条件下即可收敛到最优性，允许episode以任意频率从不同状态起始。

Conclusion: 本文给出了一种研究乐观策略迭代算法的新途径，所提出的方法为分析更一般的乐观策略迭代算法提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: The asymptotic behaviour of Monte Carlo optimistic policy iteration (MC-O-PI) is a long-standing open question. When the model of the environment is unknown, as is common in practice, the only known condition that guarantees convergence to optimality is impractical. In its canonical form, this condition requires that the episodes used for policy evaluation be initialised uniformly over the entire state-action space. This paper strictly relaxes that requirement. Specifically, we prove that initial-visit MC-O-PI converges to optimality even when updates are uniform only over the actions within each state. This allows episodes to start in different states at arbitrary frequencies; a realistic implementation when the state space is large or unknown but the action space in each state is manageable. The proof departs from the classical analysis of Tsitsiklis whose central commutativity argument no longer applies when states are updated at different frequencies. Instead, we first show that the mean-field dynamics of MC-O-PI generate monotonically improving policies when updates are uniform over the actions in each state, and then prove that noise cannot consistently prevent this improvement by extending the lock-in argument of the combined stability-ODE method. This approach suggests a new way to study optimistic policy-iteration algorithms in general.

中文摘要翻译: 蒙特卡洛乐观策略迭代（MC-O-PI）的渐近行为是一个长期存在的开放问题。当环境模型未知时（这在实践中很常见），唯一已知的保证收敛到最优性的条件是不切实际的。在其规范形式中，该条件要求用于策略评估的episode在整个状态-动作空间上均匀初始化。本文严格放宽了这一要求。具体地，我们证明即使更新仅在每个状态内对动作均匀，初始访问MC-O-PI（initial-visit MC-O-PI）也能收敛到最优性。这允许episode以任意频率从不同状态起始；当状态空间很大或未知但每个状态的动作空间可控时，这是一种现实的实现方式。该证明脱离了Tsitsiklis的经典分析，其核心交换性论证（commutativity argument）在状态以不同频率更新时不再适用。相反，我们首先证明当更新在每个状态下对动作均匀时，MC-O-PI的均场动态（mean-field dynamics）产生单调改进的策略；然后通过扩展组合稳定性-ODE方法（combined stability-ODE method）的锁定论证（lock-in argument），证明噪声无法持续阻止这种改进。该方法为一般性地研究乐观策略迭代算法提供了新思路。

</details>


### [213] [Drawing with Strangers: Population Scaling Drives Zero-Shot Mutual Intelligibility in Emergent Sketching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10582)
*Jooyeon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了涌现通信中的零样本相互可理解性（ZMI），即独立训练的智能体群体之间无需事先接触即可成功通信的能力。通过涌现素描作为视觉模态，发现扩大训练群体规模能显著提升ZMI，因为群体内变异增加防止了共适应，同时跨群体变异减少促进结构收敛，最终通过感知锚定（视觉相似性）实现通用性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有涌现通信研究主要关注新输入或新语言结构的泛化，但独立训练的、来自完全不同群体的智能体之间能否实现通信（即零样本相互可理解性）仍未被充分探索。

Method: 形式化了零样本相互可理解性（ZMI）的概念，采用涌现素描（智能体通过绘制笔画集进行通信）作为视觉接地模态，通过扩大独立训练群体的规模来研究ZMI的变化。

Result: 1) 扩大训练群体规模显著提升ZMI；2) 群体内交际变异增加，防止共适应导致的同质化；3) 跨群体变异减少，表明结构趋向某种通用性；4) 这种通用性通过感知接地实现：大规模群体越来越将其涌现素描锚定在目标图像的客观视觉相似性上。

Conclusion: ZMI是涌现通信中一个独立的泛化维度，为构建社会可互操作的人工智能体提供了可行路径。

Original Abstract: Generalization in emergent communication has largely focused on novel inputs or linguistic structures, yet the capacity for agents to communicate with strangers from strictly disjoint communities remains relatively unexplored. In this work, we formalize this capability as \textit{zero-shot mutual intelligibility (ZMI)}: successful communication between independently trained populations without prior exposure. Leveraging emergent sketching -- in which agents communicate through sets of drawn strokes -- as a visually grounded modality, we find that scaling the training population substantially improves ZMI across independent groups. Crucially, as we scale the population size, in-group communicative variation increases, preventing co-adaptation into homogeneity. Simultaneously, cross-group variation decreases, indicating a structural convergence toward a certain type of universality. Further analysis reveals that this universality is achieved through perceptual grounding: scaled populations increasingly anchor their emergent sketches on the objective visual resemblance of the target images. Together, these results position ZMI as a distinct axis of generalization in emergent communication and suggest a route toward socially interoperable artificial agents.

中文摘要翻译: 涌现通信中的泛化能力主要集中在对新输入或语言结构的研究上，然而，来自完全不同群体的智能体之间进行通信的能力仍然相对未被探索。在本文中，我们将这种能力形式化为\textit{零样本相互可理解性（ZMI）}：即独立训练的群体之间无需事先接触即可成功通信的能力。我们利用涌现素描（智能体通过绘制笔画集进行通信）作为一种视觉接地模态，发现扩大训练群体规模能够显著提高独立群体之间的ZMI。关键在于，随着群体规模的扩大，群体内的交际变异增加，从而阻止了共适应导致的同质化；同时，跨群体的变异减少，表明结构上趋向于某种通用性。进一步分析表明，这种通用性是通过感知接地实现的：扩大规模的群体越来越将其涌现素描锚定在目标图像的客观视觉相似性上。综合这些结果，ZMI被定位为涌现通信中一个独立的泛化维度，并为构建社会可互操作的人工智能体提供了一条可行路径。

</details>


### [214] [NOVA: Symbolic Regression Discovery of Interpretable Car-Following and Lane-Change Models with Driver Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10583)
*Ishak Abassi,Nassim Ali Bouazzouni,Farah Ibelaiden,Nadir Farhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NOVA是一个自主符号回归框架，能从原始轨迹数据中自动发现可解释的跟车和换道结构，在NGSIM大规模数据集上超越了现有符号回归和换道基线模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有驾驶行为模型多依赖预定义结构或大量行为先验，缺乏从数据中自主发现可解释数学表达的能力，难以在复杂真实场景中兼顾精度与可解释性。

Method: 提出NOVA框架，采用基于Rust的高性能确定性搜索引擎，在无大量行为先验条件下，从原始轨迹数据中搜索超过10,000个候选代数结构，识别出紧凑的两项加速度模型，并扩展至多项式逻辑斯特框架下的换道建模。

Result: 在4,765,788条NGSIM驾驶观测上，NOVA在意图预测任务中RMSE=1.376 m/s²（R²=15.57%），比最优基线SR-LLM低0.135 m/s²；换道建模在502名未见驾驶员上达到67.4%平衡准确率，超越基线+29.8个百分点；发现的特征算子可在高速路站点间零样本迁移，R²损失低于3个百分点。

Conclusion: NOVA证明了自主符号回归能够在极少行为先验下从真实轨迹数据中发现可解释的驾驶行为结构，且发现的非线性项可作为人类跟车行为的鲁棒骨干，并链接到既有的心理物理学碰撞规避理论。

Original Abstract: We present NOVA, an autonomous symbolic regression framework that identifies interpretable car-following and lane-change structures from raw trajectory data with minimal behavioral priors. Applied to 4,765,788 active driving observations from the NGSIM I-80 and US-101 datasets, NOVA's deterministic Rust-powered search engine evaluates over 10,000 candidate algebraic structures and identifies a compact two-term acceleration model under a forward-shifted rolling-mean prediction target. Evaluated under two complementary preprocessing pipelines, NOVA achieves $RMSE = 1.376 m/s^2$ ($R^2 = 15.57\%$) on the intent-forecasting benchmark, outperforming the best recalibrated symbolic-regression baseline (SR-LLM, PNAS~2025) by 0.135 m/s$^2$ in RMSE under an identical evaluation protocol. Across eight independent experiments, a single dominant nonlinear term emerges as a robust backbone of human car-following; a residual-guided extension further links the selected structure to an established psychophysical theory of collision avoidance. The discovered feature operators transfer zero-shot between freeway sites with under 3 pp $R^2$ loss. Extended to lane-change modelling within a multinomial logit framework, NOVA achieves 67.4\% balanced accuracy under strict vehicle-ID holdout on 502 unseen drivers, surpassing existing lane-changing baselines by +29.8 percentage points on a three-class problem.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了NOVA，一个自主符号回归框架，能够从原始轨迹数据中识别出可解释的跟车和换道结构，且仅需极少的行为先验。在来自NGSIM I-80和US-101数据集的4,765,788条活跃驾驶观测上，NOVA基于Rust的确定性搜索引擎评估了超过10,000个候选代数结构，并在前向平移滚动均值预测目标下识别出一个紧凑的两项加速度模型。在两种互补的预处理流水线下评估，NOVA在意图预测基准上达到了RMSE=1.376 m/s²（R²=15.57%），在相同的评估协议下，其RMSE比最优的重校准符号回归基线（SR-LLM, PNAS 2025）低0.135 m/s²。在八个独立实验中，一个单一的主导非线性项作为人类跟车行为的鲁棒骨干出现；残差引导的扩展进一步将所选结构联系到一种已建立的碰撞规避心理物理学理论。发现的特征算子可在高速路站点间实现零样本迁移，R²损失低于3个百分点。扩展到多项式逻辑斯特框架下的换道建模，NOVA在502名未见驾驶员的严格车辆ID留出测试中达到了67.4%的平衡准确率，在三类问题上超越了现有换道基线+29.8个百分点。

</details>


### [215] [Towards Diverse Scientific Hypothesis Search with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10587)
*Haorui Wang,Parshin Shojaee,Kazem Meidani,Kunyang Sun,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato,Teresa Head-Gordon,Jiajun He,Chandan K. Reddy,Chao Zhang,Yuanqi Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种将假设搜索建模为采样问题的进化框架，借鉴并行回火算法，通过多温度水平搜索和跨温度信息交换，在固定验证预算下同时提升假设的质量和多样性，避免多样性坍塌。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有进化搜索方法在假设生成中偏向优化而忽视探索，导致选择压力造成多样性坍塌；而科学发现中需要一组多样化、高质量的候选假设来对冲下游验证的不确定性。

Method: 提出\ours框架，将假设搜索形式化为采样问题，受经典并行回火算法启发，在多个温度水平上并行搜索假设，并通过跨温度的原则性信息交换来增强探索性，同时不破坏收敛性。

Result: 在分子发现、方程发现和算法发现三个领域，该方法在相同验证预算下同时提升了假设的质量和多样性，生成的候选假设在更昂贵的下游计算验证中保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将假设搜索重新定义为采样问题，并利用多温度并行回火策略，能有效平衡探索与利用，在固定验证预算下实现高质量且多样化的假设生成。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are on the rise for accelerating scientific discovery, most recently in advanced tasks such as generating valid scientific hypotheses. Yet in many discovery settings, the goal is not to identify a single best hypothesis since validation can be noisy and expensive, and scientists benefit from a set of high-quality alternative hypotheses that hedge against downstream uncertainty for the best solutions. Nevertheless, commonly used evolutionary search recipes tend to prioritize optimization over exploration in hypothesis generation, and the resulting selection pressure during the search process leads to diversity collapse. Motivated by these limitations, we formulate hypothesis search as a sampling problem, where the objective is to efficiently produce diverse, high-quality hypotheses under a fixed validation budget. Building on this perspective, we propose \ours, an evolutionary framework inspired by the classical parallel tempering algorithm that searches hypotheses at multiple temperature levels and enables principled information exchange across temperatures to improve exploration without disrupting convergence. Across domains including molecular discovery, equation discovery, and algorithm discovery, our approach consistently improves both hypothesis quality and diversity under the same validation budget, and produces candidates that remain robust under more expensive downstream computational validations.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLMs）正在加速科学发现，最近已应用于生成有效科学假设等高级任务。然而，在许多发现场景中，目标并非识别单一最佳假设——因为验证过程可能带有噪声且成本高昂，而科学家受益于一组高质量的替代假设，这些假设能够对冲下游不确定性以找到最优方案。然而，常用的进化搜索策略在假设生成中往往优先优化而非探索，搜索过程中产生的选择压力导致多样性坍塌。受这些局限性驱动，我们将假设搜索形式化为一个采样问题，其目标是在固定验证预算下高效地生成多样化、高质量的假设。基于这一视角，我们提出\ours，这是一个受经典并行回火算法启发的进化框架，它在多个温度水平上搜索假设，并实现跨温度的原则性信息交换以提高探索性，同时不破坏收敛性。在分子发现、方程发现和算法发现等多个领域，我们的方法在相同验证预算下持续提升了假设的质量和多样性，并且生成的候选假设在更昂贵的下游计算验证中仍保持鲁棒性。

</details>


### [216] [Dirichlet-Guided Group Forecasting for Alleviating Over-smoothing in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10592)
*Xingyu Zhang,Jingyao Wang,Xin Yu,Zeen Song,Jianqi Zhang,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dirichlet引导的群体预测（DGF）框架，通过显式建模多种模态条件预测分布及其选择概率的不确定性，解决了时间序列预测中因潜在动态模式压缩导致的过平滑问题，在保持预测精度的同时提升了动态一致性与多样性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 时间序列预测常出现过平滑问题，尤其在预测未来多模态动态时，预测结果虽能捕捉粗粒度趋势，但无法保留尖锐变化、振荡、转折点和机制转换等关键动态特征。作者从潜在动态模式压缩的角度重新审视过平滑：在部分观测和单样本监督下，多个合理的未来模式在预测过程中被削弱、合并或平均。

Method: 提出Dirichlet引导的群体预测（DGF）框架，显式建模多个模态条件预测分布及其选择概率的 uncertainty。采用Dirichlet引导的分层采样机制和基于奖励的优化，鼓励预测结果精确、动态一致且模态区分度高。

Result: 在真实世界预测基准上的大量实验表明，DGF在减少过平滑的同时，提升了预测准确性、多样性和动态一致性。

Conclusion: DGF框架通过显式建模多模态动态及其选择不确定性，有效缓解了时间序列预测中的过平滑问题，实现了更准确、更多样且动态一致的预测。

Original Abstract: Time series forecasting often suffers from over-smoothing, especially when future dynamics are multi-modal. Forecasts may follow the coarse trend of the observed future, but fail to preserve sharp changes, oscillations, turning points, and regime transitions that define plausible dynamic evolution. In this work, we revisit over-smoothing from the perspective of latent dynamical mode compression: under partial observation and single-realization supervision, multiple plausible future modes can be weakened, merged, or averaged during forecasting. Based on this view, we propose Dirichlet-Guided Group Forecasting (DGF), a mode-preserving forecasting framework that explicitly models multiple mode-conditioned predictive distributions and uncertainty over their selection probabilities. DGF uses a Dirichlet-guided hierarchical sampling mechanism and reward-based optimization to encourage forecasts that are accurate, dynamically consistent, and mode-distinct. Extensive experiments on real-world forecasting benchmarks show that DGF reduces over-smoothing while improving forecasting accuracy, diversity, and dynamical consistency.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列预测常常遭受过平滑问题，尤其当未来动态呈现多模态特征时。预测结果可能遵循观测未来的粗粒度趋势，但无法保留定义合理动态演变的尖锐变化、振荡、转折点和机制转换。在本文中，我们从潜在动态模式压缩的角度重新审视过平滑问题：在部分观测和单样本监督下，多个合理的未来模式在预测过程中被削弱、合并或平均。基于这一观点，我们提出了Dirichlet引导的群体预测（DGF），一种保留模式的预测框架，显式建模多种模态条件预测分布及其选择概率的不确定性。DGF采用Dirichlet引导的分层采样机制和基于奖励的优化，鼓励预测结果精确、动态一致且模态区分度高。在真实世界预测基准上的大量实验表明，DGF在减少过平滑的同时，提升了预测准确性、多样性和动态一致性。

</details>


### [217] [Embedding Hybrid Systems into Continuous Latent Vector Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10596)
*Sangli Teng,Hang Liu,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明当 $m>2n$ 时，$n$ 维混合系统可嵌入到 $m$ 维欧氏空间中的连续向量场，并据此提出一种含潜在空间与状态空间一致性损失的潜在神经常微分方程方法，用于从时间序列数据中学习混合系统的流，实验表明该方法优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 混合系统本质上是离散的，难以直接进行可微优化。作者希望找到一种连续的外在表示，使得混合系统能够通过可微优化方法进行学习。

Method: 首先证明嵌入定理：当 $m>2n$ 时，$n$ 维混合系统可嵌入到 $m$ 维欧氏空间中的连续向量场。基于该存在性定理，提出一种潜在神经常微分方程（Latent Neural ODE）方法，在潜在空间和状态空间同时施加一致性损失，以准确恢复混合系统的流。

Result: 理论结果表明混合系统存在连续的外在表示；大量实验表明，所提方法在学习具有不同几何结构的混合系统时优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 混合系统虽本质不连续，但可嵌入到更高维的连续向量场中，从而支持可微优化；所提出的潜在神经ODE方法能有效从时间序列数据中学习混合系统的动力学。

Original Abstract: This work proves that an $n$-dimensional hybrid system can be embedded into an $m$-dimensional Euclidean space equipped with a continuous vector field on its embedded image whenever $m>2n$. This result suggests that an intrinsically discontinuous hybrid system generically admits a continuous extrinsic representation that is well-posed for differentiable optimization. Building on this existence theorem, we show that a latent Neural ODE with consistency loss in both the latent and state space can accurately recover the flow of hybrid systems. Extensive experiments suggest the proposed method outperforms the existing method in learning hybrid systems with varying geometries from only time series data.

中文摘要翻译: 本文证明，当 $m>2n$ 时，一个 $n$ 维混合系统可以嵌入到一个 $m$ 维欧氏空间中，且其嵌入像上存在一个连续的向量场。这一结果表明，本质上不连续的混合系统通常存在一个连续的外在表示，该表示对可微优化是良态的。基于这一存在性定理，我们证明了一种在潜在空间和状态空间均具有一致性损失的潜在神经常微分方程能够准确恢复混合系统的流。大量实验表明，所提方法在仅从时间序列数据学习具有不同几何结构的混合系统方面优于现有方法。

</details>


### [218] [Causal Ensemble Agent: Hierarchical Causal Discovery with LLM-guided Expert Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10607)
*Xinyu Li,Yuanyuan Wang,Haoxuan Li,Chuan Zhou,Erdun Gao,Bo Han,Tongliang Liu,Kun Zhang,Howard Bondell,Mingming Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为因果集成代理（CEA）的框架，通过线性意见池化聚合多个统计因果发现专家的结构信息，并利用大语言模型作为元裁判在决策边界附近动态重新加权专家，从而组合出更优且更完整的因果图。实验表明CEA在多种因果发现方法上取得了最强综合性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有因果发现算法结果多样甚至相互冲突，而传统方法依赖数值统计假设，忽略了特征描述等丰富的领域特定信息。近期使用大语言模型直接查询因果关系的做法因缺乏与真实数据对齐而不可靠。为此，需要一种能够融合统计方法与领域知识、提升因果图准确性的框架。

Method: 提出Causal Ensemble Agent (CEA)框架，分为两步：(1) 在不同图级别（边、团、骨架）通过线性意见池化聚合多个统计因果发现专家的结构见解；(2) 当聚合置信度接近决策边界时，使用大语言模型作为元裁判（meta-referee）动态重新调整各专家的权重，从而组合出改进且更完整的因果图。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，CEA在广泛的因果发现方法中取得了最强的整体性能，验证了使用大语言模型进行因果发现元分析的有效性。

Conclusion: CEA框架通过将统计因果发现专家的结构性见解与大语言模型的元分析能力相结合，有效克服了单一算法的局限性，构建了更准确、更完整的因果图，为因果发现提供了一种新的集成范式。

Original Abstract: Causal discovery aims to uncover causal structures from observational data, which is crucial for real-world decision-making. However, different causal discovery algorithms can produce divergent results that conflict with each other, complicating the identification of accurate causal graphs. Traditional approaches rely on numerical values and statistical assumptions, often ignoring rich domain-specific information, such as feature descriptions, which could also help structure learning. While recent works explore using Large Language Models (LLMs) to infer causal relations via direct queries, such methods can be unreliable due to a lack of alignment with the actual data. To address these limitations, we propose Causal Ensemble Agent (CEA), a novel framework that aggregates structural insights from statistical discovery experts across different graph levels via linear opinion pooling, and uses an LLM as a meta-referee to dynamically reweight experts when the aggregated confidence is close to the decision boundary, thereby composing an improved and more complete causal graph. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that CEA achieves the strongest overall performance across a wide range of causal discovery methods, highlighting the effectiveness of using LLMs for meta-analysis in causal discovery.

中文摘要翻译: 因果发现旨在从观测数据中揭示因果结构，这对现实世界中的决策至关重要。然而，不同的因果发现算法可能产生相互冲突的多样化结果，这增加了识别准确因果图的复杂性。传统方法依赖于数值和统计假设，往往忽略了丰富的领域特定信息（如特征描述），而这些信息同样有助于结构学习。虽然近期研究探索利用大语言模型通过直接查询来推断因果关系，但由于缺乏与实际数据的对齐，这类方法可能不可靠。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了因果集成代理（CEA）这一新颖框架，该框架通过线性意见池化在不同图级别上聚合来自统计发现专家的结构性见解，并在聚合置信度接近决策边界时，利用大语言模型作为元裁判动态重新加权各专家，从而组合出改进且更完整的因果图。在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，CEA在广泛的因果发现方法中取得了最强的整体性能，凸显了使用大语言模型进行因果发现元分析的有效性。

</details>


### [219] [Fast and Highly Expressive Policy Learning for Offline Reinforcement Learning via Bootstrapped Flow Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10613)
*Thanh Nguyen,Tri Ton,Hongbin Choe,Tung M. Luu,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Bootstrapped Flow Q-Learning (BFQ)框架，通过分治视角学习流路径上的短程位移并自举组合，实现单步动作生成，无需辅助网络或蒸馏过程，在D4RL基准上显著降低计算成本的同时提升性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散的Q-learning方法依赖多步去噪，训练和推理昂贵且脆弱；近期加速方法通常引入辅助网络、策略蒸馏或多阶段训练，损害了简洁性、稳定性或性能。

Method: BFQ采用分治策略处理流路径上的位移向量：先学习可从Flow Matching边际速度准确估计的短程位移，再通过自举（bootstrapping）将这些组件组合，直接学习从噪声到动作的单步映射，从而消除多步去噪过程。

Result: 在D4RL基准上的广泛评估表明，BFQ相比多步扩散基线方法在提升性能的同时显著降低了计算成本，证明单步动作生成足以实现高性能离线强化学习。

Conclusion: 单步动作生成足以胜任高性能离线强化学习，BFQ通过避免多步去噪实现了更快、更简单、更鲁棒的学习过程。

Original Abstract: Diffusion-based Q-learning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for offline reinforcement learning, but its reliance on multi-step denoising makes both training and inference computationally expensive and brittle. Recent efforts to accelerate diffusion Q-learning toward single-step action generation typically introduce auxiliary networks, policy distillation, or multi-phase training, which frequently compromise simplicity, stability, or performance. To address these limitations, we introduce Bootstrapped Flow Q-Learning (BFQ), a novel framework that enables accurate single-step action generation during both training and inference, without auxiliary networks or distillation procedures. BFQ adopts a divide-and-conquer view of the displacement vector along the flow path: it begins by learning short-range displacements that can be accurately estimated from the Flow Matching marginal velocity, and bootstraps these components to directly learn a noise-to-action mapping in a single step. This formulation eliminates multi-step denoising, resulting in a learning procedure that is substantially faster, simpler, and more robust. Extensive D4RL evaluations show that BFQ improves performance while significantly reducing computational cost compared to multi-step diffusion baselines, demonstrating that single-step action generation suffices for high-performance offline Reinforcement Learning.

中文摘要翻译: 基于扩散的Q-learning已成为离线强化学习的一种强大范式，但其对多步去噪的依赖使得训练和推理过程计算代价高昂且脆弱。近年来，将扩散Q-learning加速至单步动作生成的尝试通常引入辅助网络、策略蒸馏或多阶段训练，这往往损害了简洁性、稳定性或性能。为克服这些局限，我们提出了Bootstrapped Flow Q-Learning (BFQ)——一个新颖的框架，能够在训练和推理过程中实现精确的单步动作生成，无需辅助网络或蒸馏过程。BFQ对流路径上的位移向量采用分治视角：首先学习可以从Flow Matching边际速度中准确估计的短程位移，再通过自举（bootstrapping）将这些组件组合，直接学习从噪声到动作的单步映射。这一设计消除了多步去噪，从而得到一个显著更快、更简洁且更鲁棒的学习过程。在D4RL基准上的广泛评估表明，BFQ相比多步扩散基线方法在提升性能的同时显著降低了计算成本，证明单步动作生成足以实现高性能的离线强化学习。

</details>


### [220] [Is Fairness Truly Fair? Towards Reliable Lipschitz Fairness in Multi-Task Learning via Fixed-\texorpdfstring{$δ$}{delta} Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10632)
*Junbo Ding,Xin Zang,Chenchen Pan,Donghao Song,Jiaxin Zhu,Danhuai Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现多任务学习中基于Lipschitz的个体公平性评估存在阈值混淆问题（不同算法因表示尺度不同而在不同语义阈值下被比较），提出ReLiF框架，通过固定δ审计和违规率反馈控制器实现可比较的公平性评估与可控正则化，实验表明固定δ审计能揭示方法依赖阈值所掩盖的效用-公平性权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Lipschitz个体公平性评估在多任务学习中因各模型表示尺度不同导致阈值漂移，使得不同算法在不对等的语义阈值下被比较，公平性排名可能发生反转，亟需一种语义一致的审计协议。

Method: 提出ReLiF框架：1) 评估阶段采用固定δ审计（基于共享参考容差）实现可比较的跨方法公平性评估；2) 训练阶段采用违规率反馈控制器动态调控Lipschitz正则化强度，避免其主导随机训练；3) 理论层面分析阈值漂移、参考容差选择以及Huber化训练代理与未平滑正间隔代理之间的关系。

Result: 在临床时间序列基准和NYUv2密集预测任务上：固定δ审计暴露了方法依赖阈值所掩盖的效用-公平性权衡；ReLiF在ResNet50骨干的NYUv2上在保持竞争力的同时显著降低了对齐偏差；在临床基准上实现了可控的公平正则化权衡，同时固定δ审计揭示了任务平衡基线有时能获得更低偏差，且真实的效用-公平性权衡依然存在。

Conclusion: 固定δ审计可作为多任务学习中评估Lipschitz公平性的语义一致协议，ReLiF框架能有效实现可控的公平性正则化，避免阈值混淆带来的评估偏差。

Original Abstract: Lipschitz-style individual fairness formalizes the idea that semantically similar examples should receive similar predictions, but its evaluation in multi-task learning (MTL) can be confounded by method-induced representation scales. This paper identifies threshold confounding: when the auditing tolerance is derived from each model's own representation distances, different algorithms are compared under different semantic thresholds. A threshold-drift analysis further shows how Bias rankings can change and identifies sufficient conditions for ranking preservation.
  We propose \textbf{ReLiF}, a reliability-aware framework that separates evaluation-time fixed-$δ$ auditing from training-time controlled regularization. ReLiF uses a shared reference tolerance for comparable auditing and a violation-rate feedback controller to keep the Lipschitz surrogate active without letting it dominate stochastic training. This work also develops supporting analysis for threshold drift, reference-tolerance selection, and the relationship between the huberized training surrogate and its unsmoothed positive-margin counterpart.
  Experiments on clinical time-series benchmarks and NYUv2 (NYU Depth V2) dense prediction show that fixed-$δ$ auditing exposes utility--fairness trade-offs that method-dependent thresholds can obscure. On NYUv2 with a ResNet50 backbone, ReLiF achieves competitive utility while substantially reducing aligned bias under shared fixed thresholds. On clinical benchmarks, ReLiF yields controlled fairness-regularized trade-offs, while fixed-$δ$ auditing reveals that task-balancing baselines can sometimes achieve lower bias and that genuine utility--fairness trade-offs persist. These results support fixed-$δ$ auditing as a semantically consistent protocol for evaluating Lipschitz fairness in MTL.

中文摘要翻译: Lipschitz风格的个体公平性将“语义相似的样本应获得相似预测”这一思想形式化，但在多任务学习（MTL）中，其评估可能因方法引入的表示尺度而产生混淆。本文识别出阈值混淆问题：当审计容差源自各模型自身的表示距离时，不同算法实际上是在不同的语义阈值下进行比较。进一步的阈值漂移分析展示了偏差排名如何发生变化，并识别出排名保持的充分条件。
  我们提出\textbf{ReLiF}，一个可靠性感知框架，将评估时的固定δ审计与训练时的受控正则化分离。ReLiF采用共享参考容差实现可比较的审计，并通过违规率反馈控制器保持Lipschitz代理活跃的同时避免其主导随机训练。本文还针对阈值漂移、参考容差选择以及Huber化训练代理与其未平滑正间隔代理之间的关系进行了支撑性分析。
  在临床时间序列基准和NYUv2（NYU Depth V2）密集预测任务上的实验表明，固定δ审计能够揭示方法依赖阈值所掩盖的效用-公平性权衡。在使用ResNet50骨干网络的NYUv2上，ReLiF在共享固定阈值下实现了具有竞争力的效用，同时显著降低了对齐偏差。在临床基准上，ReLiF实现了受控的公平正则化权衡，而固定δ审计揭示了任务平衡基线有时能实现更低的偏差，且真正的效用-公平性权衡依然存在。这些结果支持将固定δ审计作为评估多任务学习中Lipschitz公平性的语义一致协议。

</details>


### [221] [PhysMetrics.Weather: An Evaluation Framework for Physical Consistency in ML Weather Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10642)
*Emma Kasteleyn,Timo Maier,Axel Lauer,Veronika Eyring,Pierre Gentine,Ana Lucic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PhysMetrics.Weather，一个评估机器学习天气预报模型物理真实性的框架，涵盖守恒、谱和动力学三类指标，用于指导物理信息架构开发并评估模型运行可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLWP模型主要依赖数据驱动且仅用像素级误差指标（如RMSE）评估，无法保证预测结果符合已知物理定律，缺乏对物理真实性的量化评估手段。

Method: 构建三类物理真实性评估指标：守恒指标（检查物理量守恒性）、谱指标（分析空间尺度上的能量分布）、动力学指标（评估动力过程合理性），形成统一的评估框架PhysMetrics.Weather。

Result: 开源了该评估框架（GitHub），通过量化物理真实性指导物理信息架构的开发，并帮助判断MLWP模型是否适合业务化运行。

Conclusion: PhysMetrics.Weather能够有效评估MLWP模型的物理真实性，弥补了传统像素级误差指标的不足，对推动物理信息机器学习模型发展和保障业务应用可靠性具有重要意义。

Original Abstract: Machine learning weather prediction (MLWP) models have achieved impressive forecasting performance at a small fraction of the computational costs required for traditional physics-based methods. However, they are primarily (1) data-driven and (2) evaluated using pixel-wide error metrics (e.g., RMSE), so there are no guarantees that their forecasts are consistent with known physical laws. We introduce PhysMetrics.Weather, an evaluation framework that assesses the physical realism of MLWP models across three types of metrics: conservation, spectral, and dynamical. By quantifying physical realism, this tool guides the development of physics-informed architectures and helps evaluate whether MLWP models are reliable for operational use. Our framework is available on Github at https://github.com/Emmakast/PhysMetrics.Weather.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习天气预报（MLWP）模型在仅需传统物理方法一小部分计算成本的情况下，已取得了令人瞩目的预报性能。然而，这些模型主要（1）依赖数据驱动且（2）使用像素级误差指标（如均方根误差RMSE）进行评估，因此无法保证其预报结果与已知物理定律一致。我们提出了PhysMetrics.Weather，一个从守恒指标、谱指标和动力学指标三类指标评估MLWP模型物理真实性的评估框架。通过量化物理真实性，该工具可指导物理信息架构的开发，并帮助评估MLWP模型是否适合业务化运行。我们的框架已在GitHub上开源，地址为https://github.com/Emmakast/PhysMetrics.Weather。

</details>


### [222] [How Does Reasoning Flow? Tracing Attention-Induced Information Flow for Targeted RL in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10646)
*Zhichen Dong,Yang Li,Yuhan Sun,Weixun Wang,Yijia Luo,Zinian Peng,Taiheng Ye,Chao Yang,Wenbo Su,Yu Cheng,Bo Zheng,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowTracer提出了一种基于注意力图的信息流追踪框架，通过构建注意力诱导的有向无环图，利用全局信息传播结构为LLM强化学习中的token分配细粒度信用，提升推理任务性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM强化学习方法对token施以均等奖励，无法区分关键推理步骤与格式性填充词；而近期基于模型内部信号的细粒度信用分配方法多为点式启发，忽略了信息传播的全局结构。

Method: 构建注意力诱导的有向无环图（节点为token，边容量为聚合注意力权重），通过重加权边容量保留能到达答案区域的影响力并强制局部流守恒，提取从问题到答案的信息流骨干，依据流吞吐量为token评分，进而塑造token级别的奖励信号。

Result: 在多种推理任务上取得一致的性能提升，能够精准识别高影响枢纽节点和聚合检查点，使学习信号聚焦于将信息路由向（或远离）正确答案的token。

Conclusion: 基于全局信息传播结构的token信用分配方法优于现有均等奖励和点式启发方法，为LLM强化学习提供了更精准的细粒度学习信号。

Original Abstract: Token-level credit assignment remains a key obstacle for reinforcement learning (RL) in large language models (LLMs), where RL recipes typically treat all tokens equally, failing to distinguish decisive reasoning steps from routine formatting or fluent filler. Recent attempts leverage model-internal signals to assign finer-grained credit, but these are often point-wise heuristics that ignore the global structure of information propagation. We propose FlowTracer, an RL framework that traces answer-targeted reasoning flow on an attention-induced directed acyclic graph in which nodes correspond to tokens and edge capacities come from aggregated attention weights and derives token credit from this global structure. The edge capacities are reweighted to retain only the influence that can reach the answer region, while enforcing local flow conservation so intermediate tokens neither lose nor gain effective mass due to path length or irrelevant branches. On this graph, FlowTracer extracts an information-flow backbone connecting the question to the answer and scores tokens by flow throughput, revealing high-impact hubs and aggregation checkpoints that mediate long-range dependencies. These derived importances are used to shape token-level rewards, enabling learning signals to focus precisely on the tokens that route information toward (or away from) correct answers and delivering consistent performance gains across a range of reasoning tasks.

中文摘要翻译: Token级别的信用分配仍然是大型语言模型（LLM）强化学习（RL）中的关键障碍——RL方案通常平等对待所有token，无法区分决定性推理步骤与常规格式或流畅填充词。近期研究试图利用模型内部信号进行更细粒度的信用分配，但这些方法多为点式启发，忽略了信息传播的全局结构。我们提出FlowTracer，一种在注意力诱导的有向无环图上追踪面向答案的推理流的RL框架，其中节点对应token，边容量来自聚合注意力权重，并从这一全局结构中推导token信用。边容量被重新加权，以仅保留能够到达答案区域的影响力，同时强制局部流守恒，使中间token不因路径长度或无关分支而损失或增加有效质量。在此图基础上，FlowTracer提取连接问题与答案的信息流骨干，并依据流吞吐量为token评分，揭示介导长程依赖关系的高影响枢纽节点和聚合检查点。这些推导出的重要性用于塑造token级别的奖励，使学习信号能够精准聚焦于那些将信息路由向（或远离）正确答案的token，并在多种推理任务上取得一致的性能提升。

</details>


### [223] [In Defense of Information Leakage in Concept-based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10669)
*Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了概念模型中的信息泄漏问题，指出传统认为泄漏有害的观点存在缺陷，并首次提出"良性泄漏"的概念，证明在概念不完整场景下，适当的泄漏对于构建准确且可干预的概念模型是必要且有益的。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统观点认为概念模型中的概念无关信息泄漏会导致模型不可解释，应予以消除。但作者认为这一观点存在两个问题：(1) 泄漏导致模型可解释性降低的证据并不充分；(2) 在现实世界中概念不完整是常态，完全消除泄漏会导致模型不实用。因此需要重新审视泄漏现象。

Method: 通过重新表述典型的概念模型训练目标，优化训练过程以鼓励和利用良性泄漏，在不牺牲准确性和可干预性的前提下实现概念建模。

Result: 通过优化重新表述的训练目标，概念模型能够利用良性泄漏，同时保持模型的准确性和可干预性。

Conclusion: 概念模型中存在良性泄漏这一概念，并且通过适当的训练目标优化，模型可以利用这种泄漏来提升在概念不完整场景下的实用性，而不会损害准确性和可干预能力。

Original Abstract: Concept-based models (CMs), deep neural networks that ground their predictions on representations aligned with human-understandable concepts (e.g., "round", "stripes", etc.), have been shown to learn representations that leak concept-irrelevant information. As the traditional narrative goes, this leakage is undesirable and should be eradicated as it leads to uninterpretable models. In this paper, we posit that this conventional view of leakage in CMs is not only ill-posed, as the evidence of how leakage makes a model less interpretable is often inconclusive, but also bound to lead to impractical CMs under common real-world constraints. Specifically, we argue that in real-world settings where concept incompleteness is the norm, some leakage is often necessary for constructing accurate and intervenable CMs. To this end, we propose that there is such a thing as benign leakage and show that, by optimizing a reframing of the typical CM training objective, CMs can encourage and exploit this form of leakage without sacrificing accuracy or intervenability.

中文摘要翻译: 概念模型（CMs）是一类深度神经网络，其预测基于与人类可理解概念（如"圆形"、"条纹"等）对齐的表征。已有研究表明，这类模型会学习到包含概念无关信息的表征。按照传统观点，这种信息泄漏是不理想的，应当被消除，因为它会导致模型不可解释。在本文中，我们认为这种关于概念模型中泄漏的传统观点不仅存在问题——因为泄漏如何降低模型可解释性的证据往往并不确凿——而且在常见的现实约束下必然导致概念模型不实用。具体而言，我们认为在概念不完整是常态的现实场景中，一定程度的泄漏对于构建准确且可干预的概念模型通常是必要的。为此，我们提出存在所谓的"良性泄漏"，并证明通过优化对典型概念模型训练目标的重新表述，概念模型可以在不牺牲准确性或可干预性的前提下鼓励并利用这种形式的泄漏。

</details>


### [224] [One Step Closer to Ground Truth: A Multi-Scale Residual-Aware Representation Learning Pipeline for Predicting Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10678)
*Amrijit Biswas,Mustafa Kamal,Robin Krambroeckers,M. M. Lutfe Elahi,Sifat Momen,Nabeel Mohammed,Shafin Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段、模型无关的框架，将预测与残差学习解耦，通过专门的元校正器迭代修正基Transformer的残差偏差，扩展假设空间，在8个基准数据集上达到最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 单阶段Transformer预测架构存在系统性残差偏差，源于结构差异、未建模随机成分或多尺度时间表示不足，而传统方法将残差视为不可约噪声，无法自适应修正结构化误差模式。

Method: 两阶段模型无关框架：第一阶段基Transformer生成初始预测；第二阶段专用元校正器动态建模多变量通道的结构化误差模式，保留跨变量依赖关系，迭代精炼基Transformer的残差偏差。该流程被形式化为假设空间扩展。

Result: 在8个基准数据集上使用标准协议评估，达到最先进性能，在MSE和MAE指标上有显著提升。框架能缓解系统偏差、增强对复杂时间动态的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该两阶段框架通过显式解耦预测与残差学习，克服了单阶段架构的近似局限性，无需依赖限制性假设，实现了复杂误差动态的端到端学习，提升了Transformer预测模型的实用适用性。

Original Abstract: Transformer-based models have emerged as leading paradigms in time-series forecasting in recent years, employing self-attention mechanisms to capture long-range dependencies. Despite their success, these single-stage forecasting architectures exhibit persistent systematic residual biases arising from structural discrepancies, unmodeled stochastic components, or inadequate multi-scale temporal representations. This limitation persists when residuals are treated as irreducible noise, precluding adaptive correction of structured error patterns. To address this limitation, we introduce a two-stage, model-agnostic framework that explicitly decouples forecasting and residual learning into distinct stages of representation learning. A base transformer first generates the initial predictions. Subsequently, a dedicated meta-corrector dynamically models structured error patterns across multivariate channels, preserves cross-variable dependencies, and iteratively refines the residual bias of the base transformer. By formalizing this pipeline as a hypothesis space expansion, our framework addresses approximation limitations inherent in single-stage architectures, removes reliance on restrictive assumptions, and enables end-to-end learning of complex error dynamics. Evaluated on eight popular benchmark datasets using established protocols, our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, with significant improvements in standard metrics (MSE, MAE). The results demonstrate the framework's ability to mitigate systematic biases and enhance robustness to complex temporal dynamics, advancing the practical applicability of transformer-based forecasting models.

中文摘要翻译: 基于Transformer的模型近年来已成为时间序列预测的主流范式，利用自注意力机制捕获长程依赖关系。尽管取得了成功，这些单阶段预测架构仍存在持续的系统性残差偏差，这些偏差源于结构差异、未建模的随机成分或不充分的多尺度时间表示。当残差被视为不可约噪声时，这一局限性持续存在，阻碍了对结构化误差模式的自适应修正。为解决此局限性，我们引入了一个两阶段、模型无关的框架，将预测和残差学习显式解耦为表征学习的不同阶段。基础Transformer首先生成初始预测。随后，专用元校正器动态建模跨多变量通道的结构化误差模式，保留跨变量依赖关系，并迭代精炼基础Transformer的残差偏差。通过将该流程形式化为假设空间扩展，我们的框架解决了单阶段架构固有的近似局限性，消除对限制性假设的依赖，并实现了复杂误差动态的端到端学习。在八个流行的基准数据集上使用既定协议进行评估，我们的方法达到了最先进性能，在标准指标（MSE、MAE）上有显著提升。结果表明，该框架能够缓解系统偏差并增强对复杂时间动态的鲁棒性，推动了基于Transformer的预测模型的实用适用性。

</details>


### [225] [PL-KKT-hPINN: Enforcing Nonlinear Equality Constraints on Neural Networks via Piecewise-Linear Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10682)
*Fateme Mohammad Mohammadi,Hector Budman,Joshua L. Pulsipher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PL-KKT-hPINN框架，通过分段线性投影严格强制非线性等式约束，以解决PINN无法保证推理时约束满足的问题。在CSTR案例上验证了该方法在保持预测精度的同时大幅降低约束违反，并在小样本场景下表现出更好的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 物理信息神经网络（PINN）在过程建模中仅将物理方程作为软约束参与训练，无法保证推理时约束得到满足，这在需要严格物理一致性的工程应用中存在隐患。

Method: 提出PL-KKT-hPINN框架：通过分段线性投影（piecewise-linear projection）来严格强制非线性等式约束，将神经网络输出正交投影到约束可行域上，并以KKT条件保证约束的精确满足。

Result: 在连续搅拌釜反应器（CSTR）案例中，PL-KKT-hPINN保持了与标准神经网络相当的预测精度，同时约束违反率大幅降低；在训练样本有限的小样本场景下，RMSE低于无约束神经网络，鲁棒性更优。

Conclusion: PL-KKT-hPINN为非线性化工系统的代理建模提供了一种计算高效且物理一致的框架，能够在保持精度的同时严格满足物理约束。

Original Abstract: While physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have shown strong potential for process modeling, physical equations are only enforced as soft constraints during training, and thus, they do not guarantee constraint satisfaction at inference. We propose a framework, called piecewise-linear Karush--Kuhn--Tucker hard-constrained PINNs (PL-KKT-hPINNs), that strictly enforces nonlinear equality constraints through piecewise-linear projection. This extends the KKT-hPINN framewor, which exactly enforces linear equalities through the Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) conditions associated with orthogonally projecting neural network outputs onto the constraint feasible region. The method is demonstrated on a continuous stirred-tank reactor (CSTR) case study for both one and two inputs. Results show that PL-KKT-hPINN preserves predictive accuracy comparable to that of a standard neural network while achieving substantially lower constraint violations. In addition, the proposed model shows improved robustness in low-data regimes, yielding lower RMSE than the unconstrained neural network for limited training sample sizes. These results demonstrate that PL-KKT-hPINN provides a computationally efficient and physically consistent framework for surrogate modeling of nonlinear chemical engineering systems.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在过程建模中展现出巨大潜力，但物理方程仅在训练阶段作为软约束施加，因此无法保证推理时约束得到满足。本文提出一种称为分段线性Karush–Kuhn–Tucker硬约束PINN（PL-KKT-hPINN）的框架，通过分段线性投影严格强制非线性等式约束。该工作扩展了KKT-hPINN框架，后者通过Karush–Kuhn–Tucker（KKT）条件将神经网络输出正交投影到约束可行域上，从而精确强制线性等式约束。该方法在连续搅拌釜反应器（CSTR）案例研究中进行了单输入和双输入场景的验证。结果表明，PL-KKT-hPINN在保持与标准神经网络相当的预测精度的同时，实现了显著更低的约束违反。此外，所提模型在低数据场景下表现出更强的鲁棒性，在有限训练样本量下获得了比无约束神经网络更低的均方根误差（RMSE）。这些结果表明，PL-KKT-hPINN为非线性化工系统的代理建模提供了一种计算高效且物理一致的框架。

</details>


### [226] [Divide and Cooperate: Role-Decomposed Multi-Agent LLM Training with Cross-Agent Learning Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10684)
*Jaewan Park,Solbee Cho,Jay-Yoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DAC（Divide and Cooperate），一种角色解耦的多智能体训练框架，将智能体搜索拆分为搜索和生成两个子任务，分别由专用智能体处理，通过结构化跨智能体学习信号解决信用分配问题，在知识密集型问答中取得强性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将证据获取和答案生成耦合在单一策略中，导致单个模型扮演多个潜在冲突的角色，引发策略空间的组合爆炸和低效探索；同时带来信用分配问题：搜索动作即使检索到足够证据也可能因生成失败而被惩罚，反之亦然。

Method: 提出DAC框架，将智能体搜索分解为两个协作子任务：生成器同时承担答案生成和证据充分性验证的双重角色，当检索证据不足时弃权；弃权信号被纳入搜索智能体的奖励，提供结构化跨智能体学习信号以改善信用分配。搜索器通过难正例证据增强，向生成器暴露多样且具挑战性的证据环境，提升其鲁棒性。基于共享骨干网络通过参数高效LoRA模块实现。

Result: 在通用和多跳问答基准上，基于参数高效LoRA实现的DAC优于依赖全参数微调单一模型的先前基线方法。

Conclusion: 角色解耦的多智能体训练框架（DAC）通过分工协作和结构化跨智能体学习信号，有效解决了耦合策略中的信用分配和探索效率问题，在知识密集型问答中取得了更强性能。

Original Abstract: Modern language agents which perform multi-step reasoning have shown strong performance in knowledge-intensive question answering. However, existing approaches typically couple evidence acquisition and answer generation within a single policy. This forces a single model to play multiple potentially conflicting roles, inducing a combinatorial explosion in the policy space and hindering efficient exploration. It also introduces a credit assignment problem during training: a search action that retrieves sufficient evidence may still be penalized when generation fails, and vice versa. We propose DAC (Divide and Cooperate), a role-decomposed multi-agent training framework that divides agentic search into two cooperative subtasks, each handled by a dedicated agent trained with role-specific learning signals. The generator serves a dual role as both an answer producer and an evidence sufficiency verifier, abstaining when retrieved evidence is insufficient. This abstention signal is incorporated into the search agent's reward, providing structured cross-agent learning signals that improve credit assignment. Conversely, the searcher exposes the generator to diverse and challenging evidence environments by hard-positive evidence augmentation, improving its robustness. Experiments on general and multi-hop QA benchmarks show that DAC, implemented via parameter-efficient LoRA modules over a shared backbone, achieves strong performance against prior baselines that rely on full fine-tuning of monolithic models.

中文摘要翻译: 现代执行多步推理的语言智能体在知识密集型问答中展现出强劲性能。然而，现有方法通常将证据获取和答案生成耦合在单一策略中。这迫使单个模型扮演多个潜在冲突的角色，引发策略空间的组合爆炸并阻碍高效探索。同时，训练过程中引入信用分配问题：检索到足够证据的搜索动作仍可能因生成失败而受到惩罚，反之亦然。我们提出DAC（Divide and Cooperate），一种角色解耦的多智能体训练框架，将智能体搜索分解为两个协作子任务，每个子任务由专用智能体处理，并通过角色特定的学习信号进行训练。生成器同时承担答案生成和证据充分性验证的双重角色，当检索证据不足时弃权。这一弃权信号被纳入搜索智能体的奖励，提供结构化跨智能体学习信号以改善信用分配。反之，搜索器通过难正例证据增强，使生成器暴露于多样且具挑战性的证据环境中，提升其鲁棒性。在通用和多跳问答基准上的实验表明，基于共享骨干网络通过参数高效LoRA模块实现的DAC，在性能上优于依赖全参数微调单一模型的先前基线方法。

</details>


### [227] [From Observation to Intervention: A Causal Audit of Expert Importance in Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10703)
*Leonard Engmann,Christian Medeiros Adriano,Holger Giese*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批判性地检验了可解释性方法中从群体层面的观察性统计推断个体层面的因果结论这一常见做法。通过在混合专家（MoE）模型剪枝场景下进行具体的干预性审计，发现所有常用的路由统计指标均无法预测专家节点的因果重要性，表明现有剪枝方法的成功并非源于识别了可丢弃的专家，而是利用了早期层的冗余性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 可解释性方法普遍使用群体层面的观测性统计数据（如利用率、激活范数、路由权重分布）来支持关于特定计算干预效果的因果主张，但这种从"关联"（rung-1）跳跃到"干预"（rung-2）的推断有效性极少被检验。本文旨在通过具体案例实证检验这一推断步骤的可靠性。

Method: 本文采用token级别的干预性审计方法。具体针对三种高冗余MoE架构（OLMoE-1B-7B-0924、Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B、DeepSeek-V2-Lite），对比观测性路由统计指标与因果专家重要性之间的对应关系，并使用Bonferroni多重比较校正控制假阳性。此外还通过每个token的路由权重控制来排除统计效力不足的问题，以及考察早期层冗余性对剪枝成功的影响。

Result: 在所有三个模型、60个度量-层组合中，没有任何观测指标在多重比较校正后能预测专家的因果重要性，效应量均低于Cohen's d = 0.17。仅在对每个token的路由权重进行控制后，在OLMoE的最后一层MoE层恢复了一个显著的Bonferroni信号（d = +0.231, p = 0.0013）。现有剪枝方法的成功并非因为识别了可丢弃的专家，而是由于早期层的冗余性使得大多数选择标准可以互换。

Conclusion: 本文为从群体层面观测性总结到token层面干预性主张这一常见推断步骤提供了一个明确的反例，说明可解释性研究中普遍存在的"以关联代因果"的推断是不可靠的。同时展示了干预性审计如何校准可解释性主张的证据标准。

Original Abstract: Interpretability methods routinely use population-level summary statistics over observed model behaviour to license claims about the effects of targeted interventions on specific computations; in Pearl's terms, they treat rung-1 associational evidence as if it supported rung-2 interventional conclusions, a move whose validity is rarely tested. We examine one concrete instance: the use of routing statistics in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) pruning, where utilization rates, activation norms, and routing weight distributions are treated as predictors of which experts can be removed without functional cost. A token-level interventional audit across three high-redundancy MoE architectures (OLMoE-1B-7B-0924, Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B, DeepSeek-V2-Lite) finds no observational metric predicts causal expert importance after multiple-comparison correction in any model, with effect sizes below Cohen's $d = 0.17$ across all 60 metric-layer combinations. A per-token routing weight control rules out insufficient power, recovering a single Bonferroni-significant signal at OLMoE's final MoE layer ($d = +0.231$, $p = 0.0013$). Existing pruning methods succeed in this regime not by identifying dispensable experts but because early-layer redundancy renders most selection criteria interchangeable. Our results provide an explicit counterexample to the common inferential step from population-level observational summaries to token-level interventional claims about expert importance, and illustrate how interventional audits can calibrate the evidential standards for interpretability claims.

中文摘要翻译: 可解释性方法通常使用观测到的模型行为在群体层面的汇总统计量，来支持关于特定计算上定向干预效果的论断；用Pearl的术语来说，它们将第一层级（rung-1）的关联性证据当作支持第二层级（rung-2）的干预性结论来使用，而这种推断的有效性极少受到检验。本文考察了一个具体实例：混合专家模型（MoE）剪枝中的路由统计量——利用率、激活范数和路由权重分布——被用作预测哪些专家可以被移除而不产生功能损失的指标。我们在三种高冗余MoE架构（OLMoE-1B-7B-0924、Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B、DeepSeek-V2-Lite）上进行了token级别的干预性审计，结果发现经过多重比较校正后，没有任何观测指标能在任意模型中预测专家的因果重要性，全部60个度量-层组合的效应量均低于Cohen's d = 0.17。通过对每个token的路由权重进行控制，排除了统计效力不足的可能，仅在OLMoE的最后一个MoE层恢复了一个经Bonferroni校正后显著的信号（d = +0.231, p = 0.0013）。现有剪枝方法在此场景下之所以成功，并非因为它们识别出了可丢弃的专家，而是因为早期层的冗余性使得大多数选择标准可以互换。我们的结果为从群体层面观测性总结到token层面关于专家重要性的干预性主张这一常见推断步骤提供了一个明确的反例，并展示了干预性审计如何能够校准可解释性主张的证据标准。

</details>


### [228] [Event-Driven Reinforcement Learning Enables Long-Horizon Control in Semiconductor Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10705)
*Yavar Yeganeh,Mahsa Shekari,Nicla Frigerio,Daniele Pagano,Andrea Matta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向半导体制造系统的深度强化学习框架，通过事件驱动的时间差分公式实现多目标策略优化，在工业级高保真仿真中显著提升了吞吐量和设备利用率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 半导体制造系统具有随机性、高度约束、异构晶圆多步骤加工、长时域延迟反馈等复杂特性，导致传统的生产规划与控制方法难以应对高维决策问题，亟需一种可扩展且通用的优化方法。

Method: 将半导体制造控制建模为集中式智能体问题，核心策略协调全局决策；系统演化表示为离散事件驱动的互联时序过程。提出事件驱动的时间差分（temporal-difference）公式，可集成多种无模型策略优化方法，支持离线与在线训练。

Result: 在多种工业实际运行场景的高保真仿真中，离线与在线训练的智能体在吞吐量和设备利用率上均取得显著且一致的提升；进一步评估了不同训练阶段的性能和泛化能力，阐明了各强化学习算法与公式的相对优势。

Conclusion: 所提框架在控制事件驱动的复杂自适应系统方面具有可扩展性、通用性和可迁移性，验证了深度强化学习在半导体制造等大规模随机约束系统中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning promises to optimize sequential decisions in large-scale systems. Semiconductor manufacturing systems are stochastic and highly constrained environments where heterogeneous wafers traverse hundreds of processing steps across extensive equipment networks. These characteristics yield complex, high-dimensional decision problems with delayed feedback and long-horizon requirements, complicating production planning and control. We propose a deep reinforcement learning framework for multi-objective policy optimization at this scale. Specifically, we formulate control as a centralized-agent problem, where a core policy coordinates system-wide decisions, while system evolution is represented as an interconnected temporal process driven by discrete events. Accordingly, we develop a tailored event-driven temporal-difference formulation that remains general and can be integrated with various policy optimization methods under relevant training settings. We investigate several core model-free algorithms incorporated into this framework and evaluate their effectiveness using high-fidelity simulations of diverse, industry-real operating scenarios. Across extensive validation experiments, agents trained in both offline and online settings show significant and consistent gains in throughput and utilization. We further evaluate performance and generalization across training phases, clarifying the relative strengths of alternative reinforcement learning formulations and algorithms. Overall, the results support the scalability, generality, and transferability of the proposed framework for controlling event-driven complex adaptive systems.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习有望优化大规模系统中的序列决策。半导体制造系统是随机且高度约束的环境，异构晶圆在广泛的设备网络中经历数百个加工步骤。这些特征带来了复杂的高维决策问题，具有延迟反馈和长时域要求，使生产规划与控制复杂化。我们提出了一种面向此规模的深度强化学习框架，用于多目标策略优化。具体而言，我们将控制问题建模为集中式智能体问题，由一个核心策略协调全局决策，同时将系统演化表示为离散事件驱动的互联时序过程。据此，我们开发了一种定制的事件驱动时间差分公式，该公式保持通用性，可在相关训练设置下与多种策略优化方法集成。我们研究了融入该框架的若干核心无模型算法，并利用涵盖多种工业实际运行场景的高保真仿真评估了它们的有效性。在广泛的验证实验中，在离线与在线设置下训练的智能体在吞吐量和设备利用率上均表现出显著且一致的提升。我们进一步评估了跨训练阶段的性能和泛化能力，阐明了不同强化学习公式和算法的相对优势。总体而言，实验结果支持了所提框架在控制事件驱动的复杂自适应系统方面的可扩展性、通用性和可迁移性。

</details>


### [229] [Unifying Data, Memory, and Compute Efficiency in LLM training: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10706)
*Vanessa Schmidt,Huy Hoang Nguyen,Cédric Jung,Shirin Salehi,Anke Schmeink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本综述从资源约束视角出发，围绕数据效率、内存效率和计算预算感知三个相互耦合的瓶颈，系统梳理了近期大语言模型训练与推理中的效率优化方法，并提出资源条件决策的统一原则。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的效率研究往往关注孤立的技术，而忽略了资源约束（数据、内存、计算预算）之间相互耦合、共同限制模型训练与部署的现实。本文旨在提供一个约束导向的系统性视角，整合这些瓶颈并揭示其内在联系。

Method: 采用约束导向的分类框架，将研究进展组织在三个耦合瓶颈下：(1) 数据效率：基于学习动力学的可扩展代理信号、梯度和影响力评分、难度感知与课程学习策略；(2) 内存效率：联合减少权重存储、优化器状态和激活内存；(3) 计算预算感知：计算最优分配与早期停止规则，以及计算感知的数据选择与自适应推理。

Result: (1) 不同数据质量定义在不同训练体制下占主导地位，最优数据子集依赖于任务目标和资源预算；(2) GPU内存（而非原始算力）通常是微调中的主要瓶颈；(3) 计算应在边际性能增益低于预算依赖阈值时停止或重新分配。

Conclusion: 计算感知的数据选择、缩放定律和自适应推理可以在资源条件决策的统一原则下整合，最优决策取决于任务目标与可用资源预算。

Original Abstract: Resource constraints increasingly determine what can be trained, fine-tuned, and deployed in large language models (LLMs), yet efficiency is often studied through isolated techniques rather than as an interacting system of limits. This survey adopts a constraint-centric perspective and organizes recent progress around three coupled bottlenecks: data efficiency (what to train on), memory efficiency (how to fit training), and compute budget awareness (when and where to spend FLOPs). On the data axis, we review selection and pruning methods that maximize learning per token, ranging from scalable proxy signals based on learning dynamics to gradient- and influence-based scoring, as well as difficulty-aware and curriculum-style strategies. We highlight emerging evidence that different notions of good data dominate in different regimes, implying that optimal subsets depend on the task objective and resource budget rather than being universal. On the systems side, we show that GPU memory, not raw compute, is often the dominant bottleneck in fine-tuning, and that effective scaling requires jointly reducing weight storage, optimizer states, and activation memory rather than optimizing any single component in isolation. Beyond memory, we frame training and inference as compute-governed processes in which optimization, data selection, and decoding must explicitly account for finite FLOP budgets. We review evidence for compute-optimal allocation and stopping rules, where computation should be halted or reallocated once marginal performance gains fall below a budget-dependent threshold. Together, these results unify compute-aware data selection, scaling laws, and adaptive inference under a common principle of resource-conditioned decision-making.

中文摘要翻译: 资源约束日益决定了大型语言模型（LLM）中可以训练、微调和部署的内容，然而效率问题往往是通过孤立的技术而非作为相互作用的限制系统来研究的。本综述采用约束导向的视角，围绕三个耦合的瓶颈组织近期进展：数据效率（训练什么）、内存效率（如何适应训练）以及计算预算感知（何时何地花费FLOPs）。在数据轴上，我们回顾了最大化每个token学习收益的选择和剪枝方法，范围从基于学习动力学的可扩展代理信号到基于梯度和影响力的评分，以及难度感知和课程式策略。我们强调新出现的证据表明，不同的良好数据概念在不同体制中占主导地位，这意味着最优数据子集取决于任务目标和资源预算，而非通用的。在系统方面，我们表明GPU内存（而非原始算力）通常是微调中的主要瓶颈，并且有效的扩展需要联合减少权重存储、优化器状态和激活内存，而非孤立地优化任何单一组件。除了内存之外，我们将训练和推理视为计算主导的过程，其中优化、数据选择和解码必须明确考虑有限的FLOPs预算。我们回顾了计算最优分配和停止规则的证据，其中一旦边际性能增益低于预算依赖的阈值，计算应被停止或重新分配。这些结果共同将计算感知的数据选择、缩放定律和自适应推理统一在资源条件决策的共同原则之下。

</details>


### [230] [Transformer Based Model for Spatiotemporal Feature Learning in EEG Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10718)
*Xinglong Cui,Dian Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EEG-TransNet，一种结合ResNet、局部自注意力和模糊注意力同步Transformer的架构，用于EEG信号的时空特征提取与分类，在三个数据集上取得最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 脑电图(EEG)数据复杂，现有方法难以同时有效捕获其时域、区域和同步特征，亟需一种能高效建模EEG时空依赖关系的深度学习架构。

Method: EEG-TransNet包含三个核心模块：(1)基于ResNet和小波去噪的预处理与特征提取模块；(2)局部自注意力块(Local Self-Attention Block)用于区域特征学习；(3)模糊注意力同步Transformer(FAST)用于建模时空依赖关系。解码器中引入深度可分离卷积以降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在BETA、SEED和DepEEG三个EEG数据集上，该模型在分类准确率和鲁棒性方面一致优于其他方法。消融实验证实了局部自注意力块的贡献，深度可分离卷积在保持高精度的同时降低了计算复杂度。模型具有良好的跨被试泛化能力。

Conclusion: EEG-TransNet作为一种鲁棒的EEG脑活动分类和情绪识别工具，能够有效捕获EEG信号的时空特征，具有良好的跨被试泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a widely adopted technique for monitoring brain activity, offering valuable insights into neurological states due to its high temporal resolution and cost-effectiveness. To enhance the analysis of complex EEG data, we propose EEG-TransNet, an architecture designed to capture temporal, regional, and synchronous features of EEG signals. EEG-TransNet introduces three key modules: 1) a preprocessing and feature extraction module leveraging ResNet and wavelet-based denoising, 2) a Local Self-Attention Block for regional feature learning, and 3) a Fuzzy-Attention Synchronous Transformer (FAST) to model spatiotemporal dependencies. Through extensive experiments on three EEG datasets (BETA, SEED, and DepEEG), the proposed model consistently outperforms other methods in terms of classification accuracy and robustness across varying signal lengths. Ablation studies confirm the contribution of the Local Self-Attention Block in improving performance, and the inclusion of depthwise separable convolutions in the decoder reduces computational complexity while maintaining high accuracy. EEG-TransNet's ability to generalize across subjects with minimal performance variation highlights its potential as a robust tool for EEG-based brain activity classification and emotion recognition tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 脑电图(EEG)是一种广泛采用的脑活动监测技术，凭借其高时间分辨率和低成本优势，为神经状态的评估提供了宝贵信息。为增强复杂EEG数据的分析能力，本文提出EEG-TransNet架构，旨在捕获EEG信号的时域特征、区域特征和同步特征。EEG-TransNet引入了三个关键模块：(1)基于ResNet和小波去噪的预处理与特征提取模块；(2)用于区域特征学习的局部自注意力块；(3)用于建模时空依赖关系的模糊注意力同步Transformer(FAST)。通过在三个EEG数据集（BETA、SEED和DepEEG）上进行大量实验，该模型在不同信号长度下的一致分类准确率和鲁棒性均优于其他方法。消融研究证实了局部自注意力块对性能提升的贡献，解码器中引入的深度可分离卷积在保持高精度的同时降低了计算复杂度。EEG-TransNet在不同被试间具有良好泛化能力且性能波动小，突显了其作为基于EEG的脑活动分类和情绪识别任务的鲁棒工具的潜力。

</details>


### [231] [Pre-AF 13: An Interpretable Atrial Fibrillation Risk Score Mined from Discharge Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10725)
*Olga Shakhmatova,Dmitrii Kriukov,Daniil Larionov,Nikita Khromov,Iaroslav Bespalov,Alexander Zolotarev,Kirill Grishchenkov,Ekaterina Ivanova,Miron Kuznetsov,Ilya Sochenkov,Elizaveta Panchenko,Artem Shelmanov,Dmitry V. Dylov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用常规医院电子健康记录数据，开发了可解释的机器学习模型（Pre-AF 13和Pre-AF 9），用于预测心血管疾病患者24个月及整个随访期内的房颤风险， outperforms 现有临床风险评分（CHARGE-AF、C2HEST、MHS、HAVOC），ROC AUC达0.735（24个月）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有房颤风险评分依赖老年、高血压等心血管疾病患者普遍存在的因素，在该高危人群中区分度有限；且多数评分针对长期（5-10年）而非中期预测。因此需要在心血管疾病患者中开发可解释的机器学习模型，利用常规收集的医院数据进行中期（24个月）房颤风险预测。

Method: 单中心回顾性研究，纳入2012年1月至2019年5月期间多次住院的≥18岁心血管疾病（无既往房颤）患者。使用自定义NLP流程（结合基于规则的解析器和基于Transformer的命名实体识别）将非结构化出院报告转换为73个结构化特征。基于LightAutoML构建：全模型（73特征）、简单模型（缩减特征子集）和线性模型（床边风险评分）。性能评估采用ROC AUC，与CHARGE-AF、C2HEST、MHS、HAVOC比较，并使用SHAP进行可解释性分析。

Result: 17,562名患者纳入，1,438名（8.19%）发生房颤。全模型24个月ROC AUC为0.735，整个随访期为0.696；简单模型表现几乎一致（0.725, 0.696）。所有非线性模型均优于四种临床风险评分（ROC AUC 0.53-0.64）。简单模型（Pre-AF 13）使用13个特征，SHAP识别出年龄和左心房容积为最重要预测因子。线性风险评分（Pre-AF 9）将24个月房颤发生率的观察范围从~7%分层至36%。

Conclusion: 基于常规电子健康记录数据构建的可解释机器学习模型能够识别高房颤风险的心血管疾病患者，且表现优于既有的临床风险评分。

Original Abstract: Background. Atrial fibrillation (AF) is the most prevalent cardiac arrhythmia and a major determinant of prognosis. Established AF risk scores rely on factors (older age, hypertension) nearly ubiquitous among patients with cardiovascular disease (CVD), offering limited stratification in this high-risk group. Most target long-term (5-10 year) rather than medium-term prediction. We developed interpretable ML models predicting AF risk over a 24-month and entire follow-up horizon in CVD patients using routinely collected hospital data.
  Methods. Single-center retrospective study of electronic health records from the National Research Cardiology Center (Russia) for patients aged >=18 with CVD but without pre-existing AF, hospitalized more than once between January 2012 and May 2019. A custom NLP pipeline transformed unstructured discharge reports into 73 structured features, combining a rule-based parser with transformer-based NER. Using LightAutoML we built a full model (73 features), a simple model (reduced subset), and a linear model for a bedside risk score. Performance was assessed by ROC AUC, compared with CHARGE-AF, C2HEST, MHS, and HAVOC, and interpreted via SHAP.
  Results. Of 80,576 records from 45,000 patients, 17,562 met inclusion criteria; 1,438 (8.19%) developed AF. The full model reached ROC AUC 0.735 (24-month) and 0.696 (entire follow-up); the simple model was nearly identical (0.725, 0.696). All non-linear models outperformed the four clinical risk scores (ROC AUC 0.53-0.64). The simple model uses 13 features and is named Pre-AF 13. SHAP identified age and left atrial volume as dominant predictors. A linear risk score (Pre-AF 9) stratified observed 24-month AF incidence from ~7% to 36%.
  Conclusion. Interpretable ML models built from routinely collected EHR data identify high-AF-risk CVD patients, outperforming established clinical risk scores.

中文摘要翻译: 背景。心房颤动（AF）是最常见的心律失常，也是预后的主要决定因素。既有的AF风险评分依赖于心血管疾病（CVD）患者中几乎普遍存在的因素（年龄较大、高血压），在该高危人群中的分层能力有限。大多数评分针对长期（5-10年）而非中期预测。我们利用常规收集的医院数据，开发了可解释的机器学习模型，用于预测CVD患者在24个月及整个随访期内的AF风险。
  方法。对俄罗斯国家心脏病学研究中心的电子健康记录进行单中心回顾性研究，纳入2012年1月至2019年5月期间多次住院、年龄≥18岁、患有CVD但无既往AF的患者。一个自定义的NLP流程将非结构化出院报告转换为73个结构化特征，结合了基于规则的解析器和基于Transformer的命名实体识别。使用LightAutoML，我们构建了全模型（73个特征）、简单模型（缩减子集）和用于床边风险评分的线性模型。性能通过ROC AUC评估，与CHARGE-AF、C2HEST、MHS和HAVOC进行比较，并通过SHAP进行解释。
  结果。在来自45,000名患者的80,576条记录中，17,562条符合纳入标准；1,438名（8.19%）发生了AF。全模型的ROC AUC达到0.735（24个月）和0.696（整个随访期）；简单模型几乎相同（0.725, 0.696）。所有非线性模型均优于四种临床风险评分（ROC AUC 0.53-0.64）。简单模型使用13个特征，命名为Pre-AF 13。SHAP识别出年龄和左心房容积为主导预测因子。线性风险评分（Pre-AF 9）将24个月AF发生率的观察范围从约7%分层至36%。
  结论。从常规收集的EHR数据构建的可解释机器学习模型能够识别高AF风险的CVD患者，且优于既有的临床风险评分。

</details>


### [232] [SPACR: Single-Pass Adaptive Training of Uncertainty-Aware Conformal Regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10734)
*Soundouss Messoudi,Sylvain Rousseau,Sébastien Destercke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPACR方法，将共形预测直接集成到回归模型训练中，无需后处理即可生成多个置信水平下的高效预测区间，显著降低计算成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统共形预测通常作为后处理步骤使用，导致模型训练与共形预测目标（产生窄而高效的预测区间）不一致，且现有方法（如DOICR）需要针对不同置信水平重复训练，计算成本高昂。

Method: SPACR（单次自适应共形回归器）设计了一种可微损失函数，直接训练具有不确定性感知能力的回归模型。该方法在训练过程中联合优化预测区间的效率与有效性，无需批分割或预设置信水平，使得单个模型在推理时能为多个置信水平同时生成有效的预测区间。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，与标准共形预测和DOICR相比，SPACR始终能产生更窄的预测区间，获得更好的覆盖-效率权衡，同时显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: SPACR通过将共形预测目标融入训练过程，有效解决了模型训练与共形预测目标不一致的问题，在保证有效性的同时提升了区间效率并降低了计算开销。

Original Abstract: Conformal Prediction (CP) provides robust uncertainty guarantees for predictive models, but is typically applied post hoc, which misaligns model training with the conformal goal of producing efficient (i.e, narrow) intervals. We propose SPACR (Single-Pass Adaptive Conformal Regressor), a novel method for directly training uncertainty-aware regressors within a differentiable loss. SPACR jointly optimizes efficiency and validity without batch-splitting or a predefined confidence levels during training. As a result, a single SPACR model yields valid prediction intervals at multiple confidence levels during inference, avoiding the costly retraining required by methods like DOICR. Experiments on diverse datasets show that SPACR consistently gives tighter intervals and better coverage-efficiency trade-offs compared to standard CP and DOICR, while significantly reducing computational costs.

中文摘要翻译: 共形预测为预测模型提供了稳健的不确定性保证，但通常作为后处理步骤使用，这使得模型训练与共形目标（即产生高效、窄的预测区间）不一致。本文提出SPACR（单次自适应共形回归器），一种在可微损失函数内直接训练不确定性感知回归器的新方法。SPACR联合优化区间的效率与有效性，训练过程中无需批分割或预设置信水平。因此，单个SPACR模型在推理时能为多个置信水平生成有效的预测区间，避免了像DOICR等方法所需的昂贵重训练。在多个数据集上的实验表明，与标准共形预测和DOICR相比，SPACR始终能提供更窄的预测区间和更好的覆盖-效率权衡，同时显著降低计算成本。

</details>


### [233] [N-GRPO: Embedding-Level Neighbor Mixing for Enhanced Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10768)
*Xukun Zhu,Hang Yu,Peng Di,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出N-GRPO方法，通过在GRPO框架中引入语义邻居混合机制，在数学推理任务中兼顾轨迹多样性与语义一致性，显著提升了模型性能与泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有rollout技术在数学推理中面临根本性权衡：token级采样产生大量仅表述不同而实质冗余的轨迹，而embedding级随机噪声方法则频繁破坏语义一致性。亟需一种既能注入多样性又能保持语义一致性的探索策略。

Method: 提出N-GRPO方法，集成在GRPO框架中。核心机制是语义邻居混合（Semantic Neighbor Mixing）：通过混合锚点token与其最近语义邻居的嵌入来动态构建输入表示，在严格遵循局部语义流形的前提下注入多样性。

Result: 在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen系列不同规模模型上的实验表明，N-GRPO在数学推理基准上持续超越强基线，且在分布外任务上展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: N-GRPO通过语义邻居混合机制有效解决了rollout阶段的多样性-一致性权衡问题，是一种既能提升数学推理性能又具备良好泛化能力的有效探索策略。

Original Abstract: The success of Large Language Models in mathematical reasoning relies heavily on the generation of diverse and valid solution paths during the rollout phase. However, current rollout techniques face a fundamental trade-off: token-level sampling often yields redundant trajectories that differ only in rephrasing, while embedding-level methods utilizing random noise frequently disrupt semantic consistency. To resolve this, we introduce N-GRPO, a novel exploration strategy integrated into the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) framework. Rather than relying on token-level sampling or native embedding-level noise, our approach leverages Semantic Neighbor Mixing. This mechanism dynamically constructs input representations by mixing the embeddings of an anchor token and its nearest semantic neighbors, thereby injecting diversity while strictly adhering to the local semantic manifold. Experimental evaluations on the DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen models across different sizes show that N-GRPO not only achieves consistent improvements over strong baselines on math reasoning benchmarks but also exhibits robust generalization capabilities on out-of-distribution tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型在数学推理中的成功在很大程度上依赖于rollout阶段生成多样且有效的解题轨迹。然而，当前的rollout技术面临一个根本性的权衡：token级采样往往产生仅表述不同而实质冗余的轨迹，而利用随机噪声的embedding级方法则频繁破坏语义一致性。为解决这一问题，我们提出了N-GRPO，一种集成在组相对策略优化（GRPO）框架中的新型探索策略。我们的方法不依赖于token级采样或原生embedding级噪声，而是利用语义邻居混合（Semantic Neighbor Mixing）机制。该机制通过混合锚点token与其最近语义邻居的嵌入来动态构建输入表示，从而在严格遵循局部语义流形的前提下注入多样性。在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen系列不同规模模型上的实验评估表明，N-GRPO不仅在数学推理基准上持续优于强基线，而且在分布外任务上也展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

</details>


### [234] [Inverse Probability Weighting and Age-of-Information Aggregation for Decentralized Federated Learning under Partial Reception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10774)
*Chanuka A. S. Hewa Kaluannakkage,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对有损无线网络下去中心化联邦学习面临的选择偏差和更新过时问题，提出DFL-AA方法，结合逆概率加权、在线EWMA信道估计和基于信息年龄的加权，理论证明能消除链路质量偏差，实验表明在各种网络条件下均优于现有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 有损无线网络中的去中心化联邦学习面临两个关键挑战：一是选择偏差（selection bias），即来自差质量链路的更新因部分接收而被系统性低估；二是更新过时（staleness），即异步节点的更新信息陈旧。现有均匀八卦聚合与局部填充重构方法会引入持续的链路质量偏差，而基于完整性的加权进一步放大了该效应。

Method: 提出DFL-AA（Decentralized Federated Learning with Adaptive AoI-weighted Aggregation），核心包含：(1) 逆概率加权（Inverse Probability Weighting）结合在线EWMA（指数加权移动平均）信道估计，用于修正选择偏差；(2) 基于信息年龄（Age-of-Information）的加权机制，用于缓解更新过时问题，且无需全局同步。

Result: 理论上证明了DFL-AA能在期望意义上消除链路质量引入的偏差；实验上在不同丢包率、网络规模和异构无线条件下，该方法始终优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: DFL-AA通过自适应的信息年龄加权聚合，有效解决了有损无线网络中DFL的选择偏差和更新过时问题，为异构无线环境下的去中心化学习提供了鲁棒的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Decentralized Federated Learning (DFL) over lossy wireless networks faces two key challenges: selection bias, where updates from poor-quality links are systematically underrepresented due to partial model reception, and update staleness, where asynchronous nodes contribute outdated information. We show that uniform gossip aggregation with local-fill reconstruction introduces persistent link-quality-induced bias, while completeness-based weighting further amplifies this effect. To address these challenges, we propose DFL-AA (Decentralized Federated Learning with Adaptive AoI-weighted Aggregation), which combines Inverse Probability Weighting with online EWMA-based channel estimation to correct selection bias and Age-of-Information-based weighting to mitigate staleness without requiring global synchronization. We theoretically show that DFL-AA removes link-quality distortion in expectation and experimentally demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines across varying loss rates, network sizes, and heterogeneous wireless conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 有损无线网络中的去中心化联邦学习（DFL）面临两个关键挑战：选择偏差——由于部分模型接收导致来自差质量链路的更新被系统性低估；以及更新过时——异步节点贡献过时信息。我们证明，采用局部填充重构的均匀八卦聚合会引入持续的链路质量诱导偏差，而基于完整性的加权进一步放大了这一效应。为应对这些挑战，我们提出DFL-AA（自适应信息年龄加权的去中心化联邦学习），该方法将逆概率加权与基于在线EWMA的信道估计相结合以修正选择偏差，并采用基于信息年龄的加权来缓解更新过时问题，且无需全局同步。我们从理论上证明DFL-AA能在期望意义上消除链路质量失真，并通过实验表明，在不同的丢包率、网络规模和异构无线条件下，该方法始终优于当前最先进的基线方法。

</details>


### [235] [Can we trust our models? Epistemic calibration in second-order classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10777)
*Arthur Hoarau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“认知校准”（epistemic calibration）这一新准则，用于评估二阶分类模型中认知不确定性估计的可信度，并提出了期望认知校准误差（EECE）作为衡量指标，实验证明该准则比传统概率校准更强、更能揭示模型间的本质差异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统概率校准仅评估预测概率的可靠性，无法检验认知不确定性估计本身是否可信，这一缺陷在高风险应用场景中尤为突出。论文旨在弥合这一空白，提出专门针对二阶模型认知不确定性估计的校准标准。

Method: 1) 定义认知校准（epistemic calibration）准则，衡量认知不确定性是否真实反映模型预测围绕真实标签的离散程度；2) 提出期望认知校准误差（EECE），并证明其是真实认知校准误差（TECE）的一致估计量；3) 通过不可能性定理阐明认知校准与现有文献的关系。

Result: 1) 证明认知校准是比经典概率校准更强的条件，能捕获标准指标无法发现的失效模式；2) EECE是一致估计量，具有理论保证；3) 在多种不确定性量化方法上的实验表明，认知校准是一个连贯且有意义的准则，不同方法在认知校准上差异显著，尽管它们的预测性能相似。

Conclusion: 认知校准为评估二阶分类模型的不确定性估计提供了一种严格且必要的标准，能够揭示经典校准所忽略的关键缺陷，实验验证了该准则的有效性和区分度。

Original Abstract: Uncertainty estimation is critical for deploying machine learning models in high-stakes settings. However, classical calibration only assesses the reliability of predicted probabilities and does not evaluate whether epistemic uncertainty estimates are themselves trustworthy. This limitation is particularly relevant for second-order classification models. We introduce epistemic calibration, a principled criterion that measures whether reported epistemic uncertainty faithfully reflects the dispersion of model predictions around the ground truth. We show that epistemic calibration is a strictly stronger notion than classical calibration and captures failure modes invisible to standard metrics. We relate this work to the existing literature through an impossibility theorem that holds under the epistemic calibration hypothesis. To operationalize this concept, we propose the Expected Epistemic Calibration Error (EECE), which we prove to be a consistent estimator of a True Epistemic Calibration Error (TECE). Experiments across a broad range of uncertainty quantification methods show that epistemic calibration is a coherent and meaningful criterion and reveal substantial differences across methods, despite similar predictive performance.

中文摘要翻译: 不确定性估计对于在高风险场景中部署机器学习模型至关重要。然而，经典校准仅评估预测概率的可靠性，并不检验认知不确定性估计本身是否可信。这一局限在二阶分类模型中尤为突出。本文引入认知校准（epistemic calibration），这一原则性准则衡量报告的不确定性是否真实反映了模型预测围绕真实标签的离散程度。我们证明认知校准是比经典校准严格更强的概念，并能捕获标准指标无法发现的失效模式。我们通过一个在认知校准假设下成立的不可能性定理，将本研究与现有文献联系起来。为了将该概念付诸实践，我们提出期望认知校准误差（EECE），并证明它是真实认知校准误差（TECE）的一致估计量。在多种不确定性量化方法上的实验表明，认知校准是一个连贯且有意义的准则，并揭示了不同方法之间的显著差异，尽管它们的预测性能相似。

</details>


### [236] [Closing the Modality Gap in Zero-Shot HAR: Contrastive Training and Separability-Optimized Prototypes on IMU Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10789)
*Anik Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统研究了IMU人体活动识别中零样本学习的模态鸿沟问题，发现编码器目标是关键因素，通过使用判别性活动描述替代标签名作为文本原型，可将传感器嵌入与文本原型的余弦相似度从0.30提升至0.69，结合对比训练和倒置softmax校正方法，在未见类上达到73.2%准确率和0.583 macro F1，并建议使用宏平均F1作为标准指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 解决基于IMU的人体活动识别中零样本学习的核心挑战——弥合传感器嵌入与语义类表示之间的模态鸿沟。

Method: 系统评估了三种推理方法与两种训练流程组合的七种配置，在PAMAP2数据集上使用14个可见类和4个未见类（受试者108和109留出测试）。使用时间卷积网络（TCN）分别以标签名Sentence-BERT原型和判别性活动描述作为目标进行交叉熵训练和对比训练，并对比不同推理方法（包括倒置softmax校正）。

Result: 1) 模态鸿沟是训练时现象，由编码器目标决定。2) 使用判别性活动描述替代标签名原型，传感器嵌入与文本原型的余弦相似度从0.30提升至0.69。3) 最强配置（对比训练+倒置softmax校正）在未见类上达到73.2%准确率和0.583 macro F1，远超标签名基线的58.3%准确率和0.34 macro F1。4) 更丰富的文本描述会降低Sentence-BERT空间中原型间的可分离性。5) 总体准确率在类别分布不平衡时是误导性指标。

Conclusion: 编码器目标决定了传感器与文本模态间的对齐程度，使用判别性活动描述作为原型目标能显著提升零样本学习性能；建议使用宏平均F1作为ZSL-HAR基准的标准报告指标。
