### [283] [Multifractal Signatures of Ageing and Dementia Development: A Multifractal Space-Filling Curve Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10222)
*Marta Lotka,Jacek Grela,Zbigniew Drogosz,Jeremi K. Ochab,Paweł Oświęcimka*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为多分形空间填充曲线分析（MFSCA）的新方法，用于量化多维数据的相关结构。通过将多维数据映射到一维信号后使用多分形算法分析，该方法应用于阿尔茨海默症患者的MRI数据，发现大脑空间组织的异质性随年龄增长和痴呆发展而减弱，呈现出从多分形性向单分形性的转变，表明多分形性可作为脑结构变化的生物标志物。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的多分形分析方法在处理多维数据时存在局限性，需要一种高效且能保留数据局部和长程组织特性的方法，以更好地量化大脑等复杂系统的空间组织结构及其在疾病状态下的变化。

Method: 提出多分形空间填充曲线分析（MFSCA）框架：首先利用分形空间填充曲线将原始多维数据投影到一维表示，同时保留数据的局部和长程组织特性；随后使用多分形算法对得到的一维信号进行分析，从而量化多维数据的相关结构。

Result: 对阿尔茨海默症患者不同痴呆阶段的MRI数据分析表明：大脑空间组织的多分形程度随年龄增长和痴呆发展逐渐减弱，出现从多分形性向单分形性的转变；年轻对照组与老年对照组之间、以及早期痴呆与轻度认知障碍患者之间均观察到这一趋势；恶化条件下异质性特征退化，形成均质且弱相关的结构。

Conclusion: MFSCA方法有效捕捉了大脑组织的关键特征，MRI数据的多分形性可作为脑结构变化的生物标志物，为阿尔茨海默症的诊断和病情监测提供了新的定量分析工具。

Original Abstract: Multifractality is an effective formalism for quantifying the nonlinear, scale-free properties of complex data. In this study, we propose a novel and efficient methodology, termed Multifractal Space-filling Curve Analysis (MFSCA), for quantifying the correlation structure of multidimensional data. Within this framework, the original multidimensional data - while preserving both local and long-range organisational properties - are projected onto a one-dimensional representation using a fractal space-filling curve. The resulting one-dimensional signal is then analysed using multifractal algorithms. We demonstrate the utility of the method using both artificially generated multifractal structures and real data. In particular, we apply MFSCA to analyse magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) data from Alzheimer patients at different stages of dementia. Based on the results, we estimate the multifractal profiles of the brain for healthy subjects of different ages as well as for dementia patients. The analysis reveals that the spatial organization of brain structures, as measured by the degree of multifractality, progressively weakens with age and the development of dementia. A transition from multifractality to monofractality is observed both in control groups, when comparing the Young Control and Elderly Control groups, and among dementia subjects of similar age but at different stages of the disease, namely early dementia and mild cognitive impairment. Thus, from the perspective of multiscaling properties, the heterogeneous characteristics of spatial brain organization deteriorate under worsening conditions, leading to a homogeneous and weakly correlated structure. These findings not only effectively capture key aspects of brain organisation, but also demonstrate that the multifractality of MRI data can serve as a marker of structural brain changes.

中文摘要翻译: 多分形性是量化复杂数据非线性、无标度特性的有效形式体系。在本研究中，我们提出了一种新颖且高效的方法，称为多分形空间填充曲线分析（MFSCA），用于量化多维数据的相关结构。在该框架下，原始多维数据通过分形空间填充曲线投影到一维表示，同时保留局部和长程组织特性。随后，使用多分形算法对得到的一维信号进行分析。我们通过人工生成的多分形结构和真实数据证明了该方法的实用性。特别地，我们将MFSCA应用于分析不同痴呆阶段阿尔茨海默症患者的磁共振成像（MRI）数据。基于分析结果，我们估算了不同年龄健康受试者及痴呆患者大脑的多分形特征。分析揭示，大脑结构的空间组织——以多分形程度衡量——随年龄增长和痴呆发展逐渐减弱。在比较年轻对照组和老年对照组时，以及在年龄相似但处于不同疾病阶段（即早期痴呆和轻度认知障碍）的痴呆受试者中，均观察到从多分形性向单分形性的转变。因此，从多标度特性的角度来看，在恶化条件下，大脑空间组织的异质性特征退化，导致均质且弱相关的结构。这些发现不仅有效捕捉了大脑组织的关键方面，也证明了MRI数据的多分形性可作为脑结构变化的生物标志物。

</details>


### [284] [Hyperbolic Neural Population Geometry Benefits Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10238)
*Dennis Wu,Yi-Chun Hung,Braden Yuille,James E. Fitzgerald,Han Liu*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出海马体神经群体活动具有双曲几何结构的理论框架，证明其能提升记忆容量与解码精度，并构建了双曲空间中的新型联想记忆模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 神经生物学实验发现海马体群体活动存在双曲结构，但缺乏理论解释。本文旨在从理论上阐明这种双曲几何的成因及其对下游计算（记忆与解码）的优势。

Method: (1) 提出一种可统计诱导双曲几何的海马体调谐曲线构造方法；(2) 证明现代Hopfield网络的更新规则等价于最小均方误差(MMSE)估计，建立神经解码与联想记忆的联系；(3) 在双曲空间中定义一种新的联想记忆模型。

Result: 双曲空间中的联想记忆模型容量显著优于现有主流模型；双曲几何同时提升了解码精度和记忆容量。

Conclusion: 动物将空间信息编码为潜在的双曲认知地图，这种几何结构同时改善了记忆容量和解码准确性。

Original Abstract: Neural population geometry shapes downstream computation. Recent empirical findings in neurobiology suggest that a hyperbolic structure underlies population activity in the hippocampus. Here we provide a theoretical framework for this phenomenon. First, we propose a plausible construction of hippocampal tuning curves that statistically induces hyperbolic geometry. Next, we establish a connection between neural decoding and associative memory by demonstrating that the Modern Hopfield Network update rule computes the minimum mean-squared-error (MMSE) estimator. Finally, we introduce a novel associative memory model defined in hyperbolic space that yields significantly larger capacity than leading models. Our results suggest that animals encode spatial information as a latent hyperbolic cognitive map, improving both memory capacity and decoding accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 神经群体的几何结构塑造了下游计算。近期神经生物学实验发现，海马体的群体活动存在双曲结构。本文为该现象提供了理论框架。首先，我们提出了一种合理的海马体调谐曲线构造方法，该方法在统计上诱导了双曲几何。其次，我们通过证明现代Hopfield网络的更新规则计算最小均方误差（MMSE）估计，建立了神经解码与联想记忆之间的联系。最后，我们引入了一种定义在双曲空间中的新型联想记忆模型，其容量显著优于现有主流模型。我们的结果表明，动物将空间信息编码为潜在的双曲认知地图，这同时改善了记忆容量和解码精度。

</details>


### [285] [Sleep EEG Signal Criticality as a Non-Invasive Predictor of Cognitive Decline in Dementia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10889)
*Stanisław Narębski,Tomasz Komendziński,Tomasz M. Rutkowski*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究通过多分形去趋势波动分析（MFDFA）量化睡眠脑电信号的临界性，发现认知健康者的脑电信号更接近最优临界状态，而未来发展为痴呆的个体其无标度神经动力学在睡眠期间发生重构，表明MFDFA衍生指标可作为早期认知衰退的非侵入性生物标志物。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 神经退行性疾病的早期检测仍面临重大临床挑战，亟需一种非侵入性的早期生物标志物，以便在痴呆的前驱症状出现之前进行预防性干预。

Method: 采用多分形去趋势波动分析（MFDFA）量化睡眠脑电信号的临界性，分析来自NSRR的SOF队列纵向数据，比较基线期认知正常女性与后期进展为痴呆（3MS<78）女性的睡眠脑电动力学差异。

Result: 认知健康个体的脑电信号在所有电极位置均显著更接近最优临界状态（p≤0.001）；痴呆组的DFA指数向1.0偏移，表明无标度神经动力学在临床症状出现前已发生重构；监督UMAP投影显示两组在夜间睡眠结构中呈现清晰的空间分离。

Conclusion: MFDFA衍生指标有潜力被整合到自动化的睡眠筛查工具中，从而在痴呆的前驱窗口期实现更早的预防性干预。

Original Abstract: Early detection of neurodegeneration remains a critical clinical challenge. This study investigates whether sleep EEG signal criticality, quantified via Multifractal Detrended Fluctuation Analysis (MFDFA), serves as a non-invasive biomarker for future cognitive decline. We analyzed longitudinal data from the National Sleep Research Resource (NSRR) Study of Osteoporotic Fractures (SOF) cohort, comparing baseline sleep EEG dynamics between women who remained cognitively normal and those who later progressed to dementia-related impairment ($3MS < 78$).Our results reveal significant group-level differences in Hurst exponent $H(q)$ distributions, particularly during non-REM stages N2 and N3. Cognitively healthy individuals exhibited signal dynamics significantly closer to an optimally critical state across all electrode locations ($p \leqslant 0.001$), supporting the Brain Criticality Hypothesis. Supervised UMAP projections confirmed clear spatial separation between groups throughout the overnight sleep architecture.The dementia group demonstrated a shift in DFA exponents toward $1.0$, suggesting that a reconfiguration of scale-free neural dynamics during sleep precedes clinical symptoms. These findings highlight the potential for MFDFA-derived measures to be integrated into automated, sleep-based screening tools, enabling earlier preventative interventions during the prodromal window of dementia.

中文摘要翻译: 神经退行性疾病的早期检测仍然是一项关键的临床挑战。本研究探究了通过多分形去趋势波动分析（MFDFA）量化的睡眠脑电信号临界性，是否可作为未来认知能力下降的非侵入性生物标志物。我们分析了来自国家睡眠研究资源库（NSRR）骨质疏松性骨折研究（SOF）队列的纵向数据，比较了基线期保持认知正常的女性与后期进展为痴呆相关认知障碍（3MS<78）的女性之间的睡眠脑电动力学差异。结果显示，Hurst指数H(q)的分布在组间存在显著差异，尤其是在非快速眼动睡眠N2和N3阶段。认知健康个体在所有电极位置上的信号动力学均显著更接近最优临界状态（p≤0.001），这支持了脑临界性假说。监督UMAP投影证实，两组在整夜睡眠结构中呈现清晰的空间分离。痴呆组的DFA指数向1.0偏移，提示临床症状出现前睡眠期间的无标度神经动力学已发生重构。这些发现表明，MFDFA衍生指标有潜力被整合到自动化的睡眠筛查工具中，从而在痴呆的前驱窗口期实现更早的预防性干预。

</details>


### [286] [Bilinear gating of motor primitives: a principle linking dendritic computation to rapid goal-directed adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10891)
*Cristiano Capone,Luca Falorsi,Andrea Ciardiello,Luca Manneschi*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文发现猕猴运动皮层中神经元的爆发分数（high-frequency bursts的比例）比整体放电率更选择性地编码运动目标方向，并通过双房室模型解释该现象源于第5层锥体神经元的树突重合检测机制，该机制可支持强化学习智能体的零样本泛化和快速适应。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究不清楚运动皮层中的单个神经元如何同时区分"产生什么动作"和"服务于什么目标"，即神经编码中动作与目标信息的分离机制尚未被理解。

Method: 1) 分析猕猴运动皮层神经元记录数据，比较爆发分数与整体放电率对运动方向的选择性编码差异；2) 提出双房室（two-compartment）最小尖峰模型，模拟第5层锥体神经元的树突重合检测；3) 将双线性门控机制嵌入强化学习智能体，验证其学习优势。

Result: 1) 爆发分数编码运动方向的选择性显著高于整体放电率，该结论在12个记录session、3只动物、2个实验室中一致成立（p<10^{-12}）；2) 控制放电率后该效应依然存在，表明目标信息集中在爆发中；3) 双房室模型重现了该编码特征；4) 嵌入双线性门控的强化学习智能体实现了新目标的零样本泛化和快速在线适应。

Conclusion: 爆发分数是运动皮层中目标选择性编码的信号，其背后的树突重合检测机制提供了具体细胞层面的解释，并且该机制具有学习优势，为零样本泛化和快速适应提供了计算层面的理由。

Original Abstract: Movement requires the motor cortex to specify both \emph{what} action to produce and \emph{which goal} it serves, yet how individual neurons separate these factors is not understood. Here we show that in macaque motor cortex the \emph{burst fraction} of a neuron, the proportion of its spikes emitted in high-frequency bursts, encodes reach direction far more selectively than its overall firing rate. This dissociation is highly consistent: it holds in every one of 12 recording sessions spanning three animals and two laboratories (all $p<10^{-12}$) and survives controls that remove any contribution of firing rate, showing that goal information is concentrated specifically in bursts. We then show that this coding signature is the predicted consequence of dendritic coincidence detection in layer-5 pyramidal neurons: when a goal-related apical input coincides with a state-related basal drive the neuron bursts, so burst probability computes the product of goal and state, a bilinear gate $G(g)\,Y(s)$. A minimal two-compartment spiking model reproduces the effect, and the same multiplicative gate, embedded in a reinforcement-learning agent, supports zero-shot generalisation to new goals and rapid online adaptation, providing a computational rationale for segregating goal information into bursts. These results identify burst fraction as a goal-selective code in motor cortex, tie it to a concrete cellular mechanism, and show that the mechanism confers a learning advantage.

中文摘要翻译: 运动需要运动皮层既指定"产生什么动作"，又指定"服务于什么目标"，然而单个神经元如何分离这两个因素尚不清楚。本文发现，在猕猴运动皮层中，神经元的爆发分数（即其尖峰中高频爆发的比例）编码运动方向的选择性远高于其整体放电率。这种分离高度一致：在涉及三只动物和两个实验室的12个记录session中每一个都成立（所有p<10^{-12}），并且排除了放电率任何贡献的控制检验后仍然成立，表明目标信息特异性地集中在爆发中。我们随后证明，这一编码特征是第5层锥体神经元树突重合检测的预期结果：当与目标相关的顶部输入与状态相关的基底部驱动同时发生时，神经元产生爆发，因此爆发概率计算了目标与状态的乘积，即一个双线性门控函数G(g)·Y(s)。一个最小双房室尖峰模型重现了这一效应，且嵌入同一个乘法门控的强化学习智能体支持对新目标的零样本泛化和快速在线适应，为目标信息归入爆发提供了计算层面的理由。这些结果将爆发分数确认为运动皮层中目标选择性的编码，将其与具体的细胞机制相联系，并表明该机制赋予学习优势。

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [287] [Human Walking Sensing and Pose Estimation in the 6 GHz Band Using Amplitude and Phase CSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10048)
*Zhaorui Yin,Mattia Brambilla,Monica Nicoli*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究在6 GHz室内多基地无线网络环境下，利用OFDM信号的CSI幅度和相位信息进行人体姿态估计，对比四种深度学习模型，发现DT-Pose精度最优，幅度信息与联合幅度-相位处理性能相当，相位作为补充特征更有价值。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索从6 GHz OFDM无线信号中恢复人体姿态的可行性，评估不同深度学习架构在无线感知任务中的表现，并分析CSI幅度与相位信息各自及联合使用的有效性。

Method: 设计了一套处理流水线，提取6 GHz OFDM CSI的幅度和相位特征；将四种深度学习模型（DT-Pose、MetaFi++、HPE-Li、VST-Pose）适配到OFDM CSI结构上，使其能联合利用幅度和相位信息，实现对人体行走姿态的估计。

Result: 在公开数据集上，DT-Pose取得了最优的PA-MPJPE和BLL指标；幅度仅CSI的表现与联合幅度-相位处理相当；相位作为补充特征更有益，单独使用效果有限。

Conclusion: 基于6 GHz OFDM CSI实现可靠的人体姿态重建是可行的；幅度信息在姿态估计中起主导作用，相位信息可作为有效补充。

Original Abstract: This paper investigates human pose estimation from Orthogonal Frequency-Division Multiplexing (OFDM) signals in an indoor multistatic wireless network operating in the 6 GHz band. We design and validate a processing pipeline that exploits both the amplitude and phase of the Channel State Information (CSI) from multiple radio links to estimate the human body pose. Four deep learning architectures from the literature, namely DT-Pose, MetaFi++, HPE-Li, and VST-Pose, are adapted to the OFDM CSI structure and extended to jointly exploit the amplitude and phase information. The models estimate the pose of a human walking within the network coverage area. Performance evaluation is conducted on an open-access dataset using standard pose-estimation metrics such as Procrustes-aligned Mean Per-Joint Position Error (PA-MPJPE) and Bone Length Loss (BLL). Results indicate that reliable human pose reconstruction can be achieved from 6 GHz OFDM CSI measurements, with DT-Pose providing the best overall accuracy. On average, amplitude-only CSI yields performance comparable to joint amplitude-phase processing, whereas phase information is more beneficial as a complementary feature rather than as a standalone input.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了在6 GHz频段室内多基地无线网络中，利用正交频分复用（OFDM）信号进行人体姿态估计的问题。我们设计并验证了一个处理流水线，该流水线利用来自多个无线电链路的信道状态信息（CSI）的幅度和相位来估计人体姿态。文献中四种深度学习架构——DT-Pose、MetaFi++、HPE-Li和VST-Pose——被适配到OFDM CSI结构，并扩展以联合利用幅度和相位信息。这些模型估计在覆盖区域内行走的人体姿态。性能评估基于公开数据集，使用标准姿态估计指标如Procrustes对齐平均关节位置误差（PA-MPJPE）和骨骼长度损失（BLL）。结果表明，从6 GHz OFDM CSI测量中可以实现可靠的人体姿态重建，其中DT-Pose提供了最佳的整体精度。平均而言，仅使用幅度CSI的性能与联合幅度-相位处理相当，而相位信息作为补充特征比作为独立输入更有益。

</details>


### [288] [Curved Beam Enabled Wireless Communications: Modeling, Analysis and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10164)
*Jiawei Yao,Xiaoren Xu,Walid Saad,Mingzhe Chen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出利用弯曲波束解决无线通信中障碍物阻挡问题，通过连续孔径阵列生成弯曲波束服务多个接收端，并设计迭代算法优化加权和速率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统直线波束在遇到障碍物阻挡时通信性能严重下降，而现有方法难以有效利用弯曲路径传播信号来绕过障碍物，因此需要研究如何利用弯曲波束来提升存在阻挡时的无线通信性能。

Method: 首先建立弯曲波束模型，包含波束转向、聚焦和弯曲控制功能，并构建分段信道模型刻画障碍物影响下的实际信道。将连续孔径通过离散采样转换为有限求和，分析连续与离散设计的性能差距。基于离散近似，利用分数规划（FP）将原问题转化为易处理形式，再设计增强型块坐标上升（BCA）方法，通过利用前次迭代的局部下降信息构建替代函数，加入近端正则化项控制更新幅度，最终基于有效信道增益计算波束幅度。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与传统仅使用直线波束的方法相比，所提方法能够显著提升加权和速率性能。

Conclusion: 弯曲波束能够有效绕过障碍物改善通信性能，所提出的优化算法在收敛速度和更新稳定性方面具有优势，为存在阻挡场景下的无线通信提供了一种有效解决方案。

Original Abstract: In this paper, the problem of using curved beams to improve wireless communication performance in the presence of a blockage is studied. In particular, a transmitter equipped with a continuous aperture array can generate curved beams to serve multiple receivers by allowing signals to propagate along both straight and curved paths. To optimize the weighted sum-rate, a curved beam model is developed for controlling the beam steering, beam focusing, and beam curving functions, along with a segmented channel model to characterize practical channels induced by the blockage. Based on the introduced curved beam model, an optimization problem is posed with the goal of maximizing the weighted sum-rate of all users under a transmit power budget and physical constraints of curved beams. To solve this problem, the continuous aperture is first converted into finite summations via a discrete sampling of the continuous coordinate. Then, the performance gap between the ideal continuous aperture design and its practical discrete aperture approximation is analyzed. Based on the above discrete approximation, an iterative algorithm is developed to optimize curved beam control parameters. In particular, the original problem is reformulated as a trackable form via fractional programming (FP). Then, the transformed problem is solved by designing an enhanced block coordinate ascent (BCA) method which determines a surrogate-construction point leveraging the local descent from previous iterations, thereby accelerating convergence. Then, a proximal regularization term is included into the surrogate function to control the update magnitude and suppress aggressive update, thereby improving updates stability. Finally, the beam amplitudes are computed based on the effective channel gains. Simulation results show that the proposed method can improve the weighted sum-rate compared to using only straight beam.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了在存在阻挡物的情况下，利用弯曲波束提升无线通信性能的问题。具体而言，配备连续孔径阵列的发射机能够生成弯曲波束，通过允许信号同时沿直线和弯曲路径传播来服务多个接收端。为优化加权和速率，本文建立了用于控制波束转向、波束聚焦和波束弯曲功能的弯曲波束模型，并构建了分段信道模型以刻画由阻挡物引起的实际信道特性。基于所提出的弯曲波束模型，在发射功率预算和弯曲波束物理约束下，构建了以最大化所有用户加权和速率为目标的优化问题。为解决该问题，首先通过对连续坐标进行离散采样将连续孔径转换为有限求和形式。然后，分析了理想连续孔径设计与其实际离散孔径近似之间的性能差距。基于上述离散近似，开发了一种迭代算法来优化弯曲波束控制参数。具体而言，通过分数规划（FP）将原问题重新表述为可处理的形式。随后，通过设计一种增强型块坐标上升（BCA）方法求解变换后的问题，该方法利用来自前次迭代的局部下降信息确定替代函数构建点，从而加速收敛。然后，在替代函数中引入近端正则化项以控制更新幅度并抑制激进更新，从而提升更新稳定性。最后，基于有效信道增益计算波束幅度。仿真结果表明，与仅使用直线波束的方法相比，所提方法能够有效提升加权和速率。

</details>


### [289] [Optimal Illumination via Joint Movement and Phase Optimization for Movable Antenna-RIS Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10190)
*Yan Zhang,Nicola Marchetti,Indrakshi Dey*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种移动天线增强的可重构智能表面（MA-RIS）架构，通过天线单元的物理重定位实现空间信道采样与移动性分集。利用随机微分方程（SDE）建模天线运动，基于伊藤积分分析稳态分布与中断概率。提出两时间尺度框架（慢速天线轨迹控制+快速相位自适应），通过HJB方程的预测近似求解随机最优控制问题。仿真表明，该策略稳态SNR达36 dB，比位置控制提升15 dB，比无控制基线提升30 dB以上，且能效是有源RIS的16倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统可重构智能表面（RIS）在静态部署中容易遭受持续深度衰落，缺乏对抗信道时变衰落的动态适应能力。本文旨在通过引入天线移动性来突破这一瓶颈，利用空间自由度实现信道多样性，从而提升无线链路的鲁棒性和能效。

Method: 提出MA-RIS架构，天线单元可物理移动以采样独立空间信道。采用随机微分方程（SDE）框架建模天线运动，包含受控漂移和环境扩散。利用伊藤积分（Itô calculus）分析稳态天线分布、空间去相关性和中断概率。提出开销感知的两时间尺度框架，将慢速天线轨迹控制与快速相位自适应分离，通过HJB方程的预测近似求解随机最优控制问题。

Result: 两时间尺度策略实现高达36 dB的稳态SNR，稳定性显著；相比纯位置控制提升15 dB，相比无控制基线提升超过30 dB。尽管SNR低于有源RIS，但能效（EE）在不同系统规模下最高提升16倍。

Conclusion: MA-RIS架构通过移动性实现信道自适应，为高鲁棒性无线系统建立了新的范式，在能效方面展现出显著优势，是传统RIS和有源RIS之外的又一可行方案。

Original Abstract: Reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) enable programmable control of wireless propagation but remain vulnerable to persistent deep fades in static deployments. This paper introduces a Movable Antenna-enhanced RIS (MA-RIS) architecture where antenna elements physically reposition to sample independent spatial channels, enabling mobility-induced diversity. We model antenna motion using a Stochastic Differential Equation (SDE) framework capturing controlled drift and environmental diffusion. It^o calculus-based analysis characterizes steady-state antenna distributions, spatial decorrelation, and outage probability, revealing fundamental trade-offs between control strength and mobility randomness. To maximize long-term SNR while accounting for control overhead, we propose an overhead-aware Two-timescale framework separating slow antenna trajectory control from fast phase adaptation. The stochastic optimal control problem is solved via predictive approximation of the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) formulation, enabling real-time implementation. Simulations validate theoretical predictions: the Two-timescale strategy achieves up to 36 dB steady-state SNR with remarkable stability, outperforming position-only control by up to 15 dB and uncontrolled baselines by over 30 dB. Despite experiencing a lower SNR than Active RIS, the proposed approach delivers up to 16 times higher energy efficiency (EE) across varying system scales, establishing a new paradigm of mobility-enabled channel adaptation for resilient wireless systems.

中文摘要翻译: 可重构智能表面（RIS）能够实现无线传播的可编程控制，但在静态部署中仍容易遭受持续深度衰落。本文提出一种移动天线增强型RIS（MA-RIS）架构，其中天线单元可物理重定位以采样独立的空间信道，从而实现移动性诱导的分集增益。我们采用随机微分方程（SDE）框架对天线运动进行建模，该框架捕获受控漂移与环境扩散。基于伊藤积分的分析刻画了稳态天线分布、空间去相关性以及中断概率，揭示了控制强度与移动随机性之间的基本权衡。为了在考虑控制开销的同时最大化长期信噪比（SNR），我们提出一种开销感知的两时间尺度框架，将慢速天线轨迹控制与快速相位自适应相分离。该随机最优控制问题通过哈密顿-雅可比-贝尔曼（HJB）方程组的预测近似求解，从而实现实时部署。仿真结果验证了理论预测：两时间尺度策略实现了高达36 dB的稳态SNR，具有显著的稳定性，比纯位置控制高出15 dB，比无控制基线高出30 dB以上。尽管所提方法的SNR低于有源RIS，但在不同系统规模下其能效（EE）最高可提升16倍，从而为高鲁棒性无线系统建立了一种移动性驱动的信道自适应新范式。

</details>


### [290] [Fundamentals of NOMA in Low-Earth Orbit Coordinated Multi-Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10301)
*Xiangyu Li,Bodong Shang,Junchao Ma,Qingqing Wu,Jie Feng,Deshuang Huang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了CoMS-NOMA网络的下行性能，利用随机几何工具分析覆盖与频谱效率，发现增加协作卫星数并非总是有益，近完美SIC收益有限，合理功率分配可提升覆盖270%和频谱效率56%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 将协调多卫星传输与非正交多址接入集成到统一的CoMS-NOMA框架中，以实现更高效、可靠且节能的多用户接入，并从系统级角度分析其下行性能。

Method: 利用随机几何工具，推导CoMS-NOMA中的相关角度和距离作为中间结果，进而获得合并信号功率分布，并在考虑星间和星内干扰及潜在不完全SIC的情况下分析覆盖与频谱性能。

Result: 增加协作卫星数量并不总是提升覆盖和频谱效率；更高主瓣增益可改善覆盖，但近完美SIC相较于合理SIC带来的增益有限；合理选择功率分配系数后，CoMS-NOMA相比传统正交和单卫星方案，覆盖提升高达270%，总频谱效率提升56%。

Conclusion: CoMS-NOMA具有显著性能优势，且通过合理参数配置可实现绿色节能的卫星组网。

Original Abstract: Coordinated multi-satellite (CoMS) transmission and non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) are envisioned to jointly enhance coverage, capacity, and spectrum efficiency for satellite networks. Their integration into a unified CoMS-NOMA framework will allow more efficient, reliable, and energy-efficient multi-user access. This paper investigates the downlink performance of CoMS-NOMA networks from a system-level perspective, in which multiple satellites cooperatively serve multiple users via NOMA. Leveraging tools from stochastic geometry, related angles and distances in CoMS-NOMA are first derived as intermediate results. Then, we obtain the combined signal power distributions and analyze coverage and spectrum performance under both inter- and intra-satellite interference, accounting for potential imperfect successive interference cancellation (SIC). The analytical model is validated across a range of system parameters, including the number of satellites, service region angle, error-propagation factor, and power allocation coefficients. Numerical results indicate that increasing the number of cooperative satellites does not always improve coverage and spectrum efficiency. Additionally, while a higher main-lobe gain improves coverage, a near-perfect SIC provides only slightly greater benefits than a reasonably good SIC. With properly selected power allocation coefficients, CoMS-NOMA achieves up to a 270% improvement in coverage and a 56% gain in sum spectral efficiency, compared with conventional orthogonal and single-satellite schemes, indicating potential for green, energy-efficient satellite networking.

中文摘要翻译: 协调多卫星传输和非正交多址接入被寄望于协同增强卫星网络的覆盖、容量和频谱效率。将两者集成到统一的CoMS-NOMA框架中，将实现更高效、可靠且节能的多用户接入。本文从系统级角度研究了CoMS-NOMA网络的下行性能，其中多颗卫星通过NOMA协作服务多个用户。借助随机几何工具，首先推导了CoMS-NOMA中的相关角度和距离作为中间结果。然后，我们获得了合并信号功率分布，并在考虑星间和星内干扰及潜在不完全连续干扰消除的情况下，分析了覆盖和频谱性能。该分析模型在一系列系统参数下得到验证，包括卫星数量、服务区域角度、误差传播因子和功率分配系数。数值结果表明，增加协作卫星数量并不总是能提升覆盖和频谱效率。此外，虽然更高的主瓣增益改善了覆盖，但近完美SIC相比合理良好的SIC带来的增益仅略微更大。通过合理选择功率分配系数，与传统正交和单卫星方案相比，CoMS-NOMA实现了高达270%的覆盖提升和56%的总频谱效率增益，这表明了绿色节能卫星组网的潜力。

</details>


### [291] [Simplified Temporal Convolutional-Based Channel Estimation for a WiFi Vehicular Communication Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10511)
*Simbarashe Aldrin Ngorima,Albert Helberg,Marelie Davel*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于简化时间卷积网络（DPA-TCN）的数据导频辅助信道估计方法，在降低约65%模型复杂度的同时，实现了与LSTM-DPA-TA相当的误码率性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: IEEE 802.11p标准中的导频信号在高移动性场景下不足以实现准确的信道估计；现有DPA方法虽能缓解该问题，但存在解映射误差。

Method: 提出一种简化的时间卷积网络（TCN）结合数据导频辅助（DPA）的信道估计器（DPA-TCN），在混合信噪比数据集上训练。

Result: 所提DPA-TCN估计器实现了与最先进的LSTM-DPA-TA相当的误码率性能，同时模型复杂度降低了约65%。

Conclusion: DPA-TCN能够在保持高信道估计性能的前提下显著降低计算复杂度，适用于车载通信中的高移动性场景。

Original Abstract: Channel estimation in vehicular communication is a crucial element in the advancement of intelligent transportation systems. However, the use of pilot signals in the IEEE 802.11p standard is insufficient for accurate channel estimation in high-mobility scenarios. Data pilot-aided (DPA) estimation helps address this, but suffers from demapping errors. We propose a simplified Temporal Convolutional Network-based estimator (DPA-TCN) trained on a mixed signal-to-noise ratio dataset to improve estimation performance and reduce computational complexity. Our DPA-TCN estimator achieves a bit error rate comparable to a state-of-the-art long-short-term memory network with DPA and temporal averaging (LSTM-DPA-TA) while reducing the complexity of the model by approximately 65%.

中文摘要翻译: 车载通信中的信道估计是智能交通系统发展的关键要素。然而，IEEE 802.11p标准中导频信号的使用不足以在高移动性场景下实现准确的信道估计。数据导频辅助（DPA）估计方法有助于解决这一问题，但存在解映射误差。我们提出了一种基于简化时间卷积网络的估计器（DPA-TCN），在混合信噪比数据集上训练，以提升估计性能并降低计算复杂度。我们的DPA-TCN估计器实现了与采用DPA和时间平均的最先进长短期记忆网络（LSTM-DPA-TA）相当的误码率，同时将模型复杂度降低了约65%。

</details>


### [292] [Complex VAE with Heavy-Tailed Likelihood for Radar Target Detection in Sea Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10540)
*Ting Bai,Jun Tang,Yuxin Xu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种无监督复值变分自编码器（VAE）用于海杂波环境下的海事雷达目标检测。该模型仅用杂波数据训练，采用Student-t负对数似然处理重尾重建误差，并引入时域幅度误差约束。推理时以重建偏差作为检测统计量，通过经验分位数设定CFAR门限。实测数据实验表明，在CFAR约束下性能一致优于MF、AMF和实值β-VAE。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 海杂波具有重尾、尖峰特性，且标注目标数据稀缺，现有方法难以有效处理复值杂波的无监督检测问题。

Method: 1) 使用复值VAE对I/Q分量进行联合重建；2) 采用Student-t负对数似然拟合重尾重建误差，降低异常值敏感度；3) 引入时域幅度误差约束惩罚慢时间幅度失配；4) 推理时以重建偏差为检测统计量，通过杂波验证集的经验分位数设定CFAR门限。

Result: 在实测海杂波数据上，所提方法在CFAR约束下检测性能一致优于匹配滤波（MF）、自适应匹配滤波（AMF）以及实值β-VAE基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的无监督复值VAE方法有效利用了海杂波的重尾特性并缓解了标注数据稀缺问题，在恒虚警率约束下实现了更优的雷达目标检测性能。

Original Abstract: To address the heavy-tailed, spike-prone nature of sea clutter and the scarcity of labeled target data, an unsupervised complex-valued variational autoencoder (VAE) for maritime radar target detection is proposed. In implementation, each complex baseband slow-time sequence is represented by its in-phase and quadrature components, and the model learns their joint reconstruction from clutter-only data. A Student-\(t\) negative log-likelihood is adopted to capture heavy-tailed reconstruction errors while reducing sensitivity to outliers during clutter learning. In addition, a time-domain amplitude error constraint is introduced to penalize slow-time magnitude mismatch in the reconstruction. At inference, reconstruction deviation is used as the detection statistic, and the decision threshold is set via an empirical quantile estimated from a clutter-only validation set to enforce a constant false-alarm rate (CFAR). Experiments on measured sea-clutter data show that detection performance is consistently improved over MF, AMF, and a real-valued \(β\)-VAE under CFAR constraints.

中文摘要翻译: 针对海杂波的重尾、尖峰特性以及标注目标数据稀缺的问题，提出了一种用于海事雷达目标检测的无监督复值变分自编码器（VAE）。在实现中，每个复基带慢时序列由其同相和正交分量表示，模型从仅含杂波的数据中学习它们的联合重建。采用Student-t负对数似然来捕获重尾重建误差，同时降低杂波学习过程中对异常值的敏感度。此外，引入时域幅度误差约束来惩罚重建中的慢时间幅度失配。在推理阶段，将重建偏差作为检测统计量，并通过从杂波验证集估计的经验分位数设定决策阈值，以实现恒虚警率（CFAR）。在实测海杂波数据上的实验表明，在CFAR约束下，检测性能一致优于匹配滤波、自适应匹配滤波以及实值β-VAE。

</details>


### [293] [Information Bottleneck Meets Quantization: Finite Rate Analysis and Optimal Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10869)
*Francesco Binucci,Paolo Banelli*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究信息瓶颈（IB）框架下潜变量表示在有限比特约束下的量化问题，提出了面向任务的量化设计方法，并推广到非高斯场景下的变分自编码器（VAE）中。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准高斯IB解是连续值，而实际应用中潜变量需用有限比特存储/表示，因此需要研究量化对信息性的影响并设计有限速率下的最优量化方案。

Method: 理论分析标量和向量量化对GIB潜变量表示的（非）信息性影响；在有限速率约束下重新构建GIB优化问题，提出联合优化的任务导向量化设计；将方法扩展到非高斯场景，修改VAE中IB启发式向量量化器的代价函数。

Result: 在MMSE回归问题上的仿真表明，所提量化设计相比启发式或分离式量化方案有显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 面向任务的量化设计能有效弥补有限速率下GIB潜变量表示的信息损失，并能推广至非高斯场景下的VAE量化器设计。

Original Abstract: The Information Bottleneck (IB) is a well established framework that looks for a latent compact representation of a data source, by trading rate and data-size representation, for information accuracy with respect to another target data. The Gaussian IB (GIB) is its simple closed form solution, when the target is jointly Gaussian with the source. Actually, in many practical problems the latent representation has to be stored or represented by a finite number of bits, while the optimal (G)IB solution has not. First, this manuscript theoretically analyzes the effect of scalar and vector quantization of the GIB latent representation, and its impact on the (dis)informativeness with respect to the target data. Then, task-oriented quantization designs are proposed by (jointly) reformulating the GIB optimization problem under a finite-rate constraint on the latent representation. Simulation results on MMSE regression problems confirm the effectiveness of the proposed quantization designs, which show significant gains with respect to more heuristic, or separate, quantization designs of the standard GIB latent representation. Finally, the paper extends the task-oriented philosophy to non-Gaussian settings, by properly modifying the cost function used in variational auto-encoders (VAEs) of IB-inspired vector quantizers.

中文摘要翻译: 信息瓶颈（IB）是一个成熟的框架，通过权衡速率和数据表示大小，以寻找数据源的紧凑潜变量表示，从而保证关于另一目标数据的信息准确性。当目标与源服从联合高斯分布时，高斯IB（GIB）给出了简单的闭式解。然而，在许多实际问题中，潜变量表示必须用有限比特数存储或表示，而最优（G）IB解并未考虑这一约束。首先，本文从理论上分析了标量和向量量化对GIB潜变量表示的影响，及其对目标数据（非）信息性的作用。然后，通过在潜变量表示上施加有限速率约束，重新构建GIB优化问题，提出了面向任务的量化设计方案。在MMSE回归问题上的仿真结果证实了所提量化设计的有效性，相比标准GIB潜变量表示上更启发式或分离式的量化设计，所提方法显示出显著的性能提升。最后，本文将面向任务的思想扩展到非高斯场景，通过适当修改受IB启发的向量量化器在变分自编码器（VAE）中使用的代价函数来实现。

</details>


### [294] [Personalized Deep Learning for Short-Term Forecasting of Impending Atrial Fibrillation from Continuous Wearable ECG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10900)
*Jangwon Suh,Soonil Kwon,Jungmin Ko,Yun Kwan Kim,Hee Seok Song,Eue-Keun Choi,Wonjong Rhee*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过个性化微调全局深度学习模型，利用患者个体心电图数据来提升短期房颤预测性能，结果表明个性化模型显著优于全局模型，AUROC提升明显，且能捕捉患者特异性特征，有助于可穿戴监测环境中的房颤预警。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 可穿戴心电图持续监测虽广泛用于心律失常监测，但跨患者心电信号的变异性使得预测即将发生的房颤（AF）面临挑战。现有全局模型难以适应个体差异，因此需要探索基于患者个体数据微调模型的个性化方法。

Method: 基于ICENTIA11K数据集训练全局深度学习模型，随后在三个队列（ICENTIA11K、IRIDIA-AF、MobiCARE）中对模型进行个性化微调。预处理后，模型处理60秒心电片段，预测未来5分钟内的房颤发生风险。评估了适应数据量的影响，并分析了心率、RMSSD等心电特征。

Result: 个性化模型显著优于全局模型：ICENTIA11K队列AUROC为0.711 vs. 0.614，MobiCARE队列AUROC为0.686 vs. 0.585。个性化收益随患者特异性微调数据量增加而增大。全局模型在房颤临近时准确率上升，而个性化模型在两个外部队列中呈现不同时间动态，表明其捕捉了与房颤事件距离依赖性较低的患者特异性特征。房颤前阶段心率和RMSSD升高，特征归因突出频繁房性早搏（PACs）和短阵室上性心动过速（SVTs）等临床相关前兆。

Conclusion: 利用患者特异性可穿戴心电图数据对深度学习模型进行个性化微调，能够显著增强对即将发生房颤的短期预测能力。该个性化框架有助于在动态监测环境中实现及时的预防性干预和更好的房颤管理。

Original Abstract: Background and Objective: Continuous wearable electrocardiogram (ECG) monitoring is increasingly used for ambulatory arrhythmia surveillance, yet forecasting impending atrial fibrillation (AF) is challenged by inter-patient ECG variability. This study investigated whether personalizing a global model via fine-tuning on an individual's ECG signals improves short-term forecasting of impending AF. Methods: A global model trained on the ICENTIA11K dataset was compared against personalized models fine-tuned across three cohorts: ICENTIA11K, IRIDIA-AF, and MobiCARE. Following preprocessing, models processed 60-second ECG segments for a five-minute forecast horizon. We evaluated the impact of adaptation data volume and analyzed ECG features, such as heart rate and RMSSD. Results: Personalized models significantly outperformed the global model, achieving AUROCs of 0.711 vs. 0.614 in ICENTIA11K and 0.686 vs. 0.585 in MobiCARE. Personalization benefits increased with the amount of patient-specific fine-tuning data. While the global model's accuracy rose as AF onset approached, personalized models in the two external cohorts exhibited distinct temporal dynamics, which may indicate the capture of patient-specific characteristics less dependent on proximity to the AF event. Pre-AF episodes showed elevated heart rates and RMSSD. Feature attributions highlighted clinically relevant precursors, including frequent premature atrial complexes (PACs) and short supraventricular tachycardias (SVTs). Conclusions: Adapting deep learning models with patient-specific wearable ECG data significantly enhances short-term forecasting of impending AF. This personalized framework supports timely preventive interventions and improved AF management in ambulatory monitoring environments.

中文摘要翻译: 背景与目的：连续可穿戴心电图监测越来越多地用于动态心律不齐监测，然而预测即将发生的房颤（AF）面临患者间心电图变异性的挑战。本研究探究了通过个体心电信号对全局模型进行微调（fine-tuning）是否能够改善对即将发生房颤的短期预测能力。方法：将在ICENTIA11K数据集上训练的全局模型与在三个队列（ICENTIA11K、IRIDIA-AF和MobiCARE）上进行个性化微调的模型进行比较。经过预处理后，模型处理60秒心电片段，预测未来五分钟的房颤风险。我们评估了适应数据量的影响，并分析了心率、RMSSD等心电特征。结果：个性化模型显著优于全局模型，在ICENTIA11K队列中AUROC分别为0.711 vs. 0.614，在MobiCARE队列中为0.686 vs. 0.585。个性化收益随患者特异性微调数据量的增加而增大。全局模型的准确性随房颤发作临近而上升，但个性化模型在两个外部队列中表现出不同的时间动态，这可能表明其捕捉了与房颤事件距离依赖性较低的患者特异性特征。房颤前阶段显示心率和RMSSD升高。特征归因（feature attribution）突出了临床相关前兆，包括频繁房性早搏（PACs）和短阵室上性心动过速（SVTs）。结论：通过患者特异性可穿戴心电图数据对深度学习模型进行自适应微调，能够显著增强对即将发生房颤的短期预测能力。该个性化框架支持在动态监测环境中进行及时的预防性干预，并改善房颤管理。

</details>


### [295] [Multi-Channel Soil Moisture Measurement: High Accuracy and Low Crosstalk Through Optical-Semiconductor Based Differential Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11020)
*Thomas Maier,Charlotte Rohleder,Lukas Kamm,Philipp Dauner,Jan Pinski,Bjoern M. Eskofier*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种紧凑型多深度电容式土壤湿度探头，通过差分激励、光学耦合晶体管桥接和等效电路建模，显著抑制了杂散电容和温度漂移，在实验室和实地验证中展现了高精度、可重复性和可扩展性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有土壤湿度测量因土壤异质性、传感体积有限、温度漂移和寄生通道耦合而不可靠，亟需一种更精确、稳定且可扩展的测量方案。

Method: 采用紧凑型多深度电容探头，扩展平行板几何结构并引入差分激励以抑制杂散电容；建立等效电路模型量化寄生效应；利用光学耦合晶体管桥隔离各传感层；通过标定模型将原始电容转换为体积含水量和植物可用水。

Result: 实验室结果显示温度灵敏度降低四倍，传感体积高度集中，异质土壤中重复性提升；实地验证表明精度和准确性与广泛使用的商用仪器相当。

Conclusion: 该方法为农业和城市土壤湿度监测提供了一种实用、可扩展的高精度解决方案。

Original Abstract: Soil moisture measurement plays a key role in irrigation and environmental management. Yet it remains unreliable due to heterogeneous soils, limited sensing volumes, temperature drift, and parasitic inter-channel coupling. This work presents a compact multi-depth capacitive probe that extends a parallel-plate geometry from previous work with differential activation to suppress stray capacitances and improve accuracy. An equivalent-circuit model quantifies parasitic effects, and optically coupled transistor bridges isolate each sensing layer. Raw capacitance is converted to volumetric water content and plant-available water using established calibration models. Laboratory results show a fourfold reduction in temperature sensitivity, strong confinement of the sensing volume, and improved repeatability in heterogeneous soils. Field validation against reference sensors demonstrates high accuracy and precision comparable to widely used instruments, enabling a practical and scalable solution for agricultural and urban soil-moisture monitoring.

中文摘要翻译: 土壤湿度测量在灌溉和环境管理中起着关键作用。然而，由于土壤的异质性、有限的传感体积、温度漂移以及寄生通道间的耦合，其可靠性仍然不足。本工作提出了一种紧凑的多深度电容式探头，扩展了先前工作中的平行板几何结构，并引入差分激励以抑制杂散电容并提高精度。通过等效电路模型量化寄生效应，并利用光学耦合晶体管桥隔离各传感层。原始电容通过已有的标定模型转换为体积含水量和植物可用水。实验室结果显示，温度灵敏度降低了四倍，传感体积高度集中，且在异质土壤中的重复性得到改善。与参考传感器的实地验证表明，其精度和准确性与广泛使用的仪器相当，为农业和城市土壤湿度监测提供了一种实用且可扩展的解决方案。

</details>


### [296] [DMT: Demographic Conditioning, Morphology-Enhanced Transformer for Cuffless Blood Pressure Estimation from PPG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11125)
*Yidan Shen,Neville Mathew,Maham Rahimi,Deependra Dhakal,George Zouridakis,Xin Fu,Renjie Hu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Transformer的PPG无袖带血压估计方法，通过FiLM特征调制融合人口统计学信息，并引入辅助形态学头引导模型关注血压相关波形特征。在PulseDB数据集上，SBP的MAE为4.56 mmHg，DBP为2.62 mmHg，相比基线分别降低47%和50%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PPG血压估计模型仅使用回归训练，易依赖振幅主导的捷径；人口统计学协变量通常仅通过后期融合方式引入，限制了主体特异性表示学习。

Method: 基于Transformer网络，利用自注意力捕获多心动周期的长程依赖；通过FiLM风格的特征调制在注意力层和前馈子层中引入人口统计学条件；添加辅助形态学头引导模型关注与动脉硬化和波反射相关的血压波形形态。

Result: 在PulseDB数据集上，SBP的MAE为4.56 mmHg，DBP为2.62 mmHg；相比先前的人口统计学增强PPG基线，误差分别降低47%和50%。

Conclusion: 提出的轻量级单传感器模型在标定部署场景下支持可扩展且临床可靠的无袖带血压估计。

Original Abstract: Blood pressure (BP) is a key marker for cardiovascular risk assessment and therapeutic decision-making, and Photoplethysmography (PPG) enables low-cost, wearable-friendly cuffless BP estimation. However, even with recent progress, many PPG-based models are trained with BP regression alone and may rely on amplitude-dominated shortcuts. In addition, demographic covariates that systematically modulate vascular compliance are often incorporated only via late fusion, limiting subject-specific representation learning. We propose a Transformer-based network for cuffless BP estimation from PPG signal, leveraging self-attention to capture long-range dependencies across multiple cardiac cycles. To account for subject-specific vascular differences, the model is conditioned on demographics via FiLM-style feature modulation applied through the attention and feed-forward sublayers of Transformer blocks. In addition, we add an auxiliary morphology head to guide the model to attend to BP-relevant waveform morphology associated with arterial stiffness and wave reflection. Under calibration-based evaluation protocols on the large-scale PulseDB dataset, the proposed method achieves MAE of 4.56 mmHg for systolic BP and 2.62 mmHg for diastolic BP, reducing errors by 47% and 50% compared with prior demographic-enhanced PPG baselines. The resulting lightweight, single-sensor model supports scalable and clinically grounded cuffless BP estimation in calibration-enabled deployment settings.

中文摘要翻译: 血压（BP）是心血管风险评估和治疗决策的关键指标，光电容积描记法（PPG）能够实现低成本、可穿戴友好的无袖带血压估计。然而，即使近年来取得了进展，许多基于PPG的模型仅使用血压回归进行训练，可能依赖于振幅主导的捷径。此外，系统性调节血管顺应性的人口统计学协变量通常仅通过后期融合方式引入，限制了主体特异性表示学习。我们提出了一种基于Transformer的网络，用于从PPG信号进行无袖带血压估计，利用自注意力机制捕获多个心动周期的长程依赖关系。为了考虑主体特异性的血管差异，模型通过FiLM风格的特征调制在Transformer块的注意力子层和前馈子层中引入人口统计学条件。此外，我们添加了一个辅助形态学头，以引导模型关注与动脉硬化和波反射相关的血压相关波形形态。在基于标定的评估协议下，在大规模PulseDB数据集上，所提方法在收缩压上达到了4.56 mmHg的平均绝对误差（MAE），在舒张压上达到了2.62 mmHg，相比先前的人口统计学增强PPG基线，误差分别降低了47%和50%。由此产生的轻量级单传感器模型支持在标定部署场景中进行可扩展且临床可靠的无袖带血压估计。

</details>


### [297] [Pre-Fault Voltage Discrimination and Time-Domain Protection for Distribution Networks with Inverter-Based Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11135)
*Junyuan Zhao,François Bouffard,Géza Joós*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对逆变器型资源（IBR）接入配电网后传统过流保护失效的问题，提出一种基于故障前电压辨识（PVD）的时域保护策略，通过区分故障与正常开关及励磁涌流，实现快速、可靠的故障检测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 逆变器型资源（IBR）在配电网中日益普及，由于其缺乏短路电流供给能力，导致基于相量的过流保护（如ANSI 51）无法有效工作，亟需替代方案。

Method: 提出一种故障前电压辨识（PVD）策略，利用时域保护原理，通过检测故障并区分正常开关操作和变压器励磁涌流，设计简单有效的故障检测算法。

Result: 离线仿真实验和控制器硬件在环实时仿真验证了所提算法在各类故障和正常开关事件下的有效性，PVD能够在不降低安全性和可靠性的前提下实现更快的故障检测。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于PVD的时域保护算法能够有效解决IBR接入配电网后的过流保护难题，兼顾检测速度与可靠性。

Original Abstract: The increasing proliferation of inverter-based resources (IBRs) in distribution networks is presenting a major challenge for phasor-based overcurrent protection. This challenge stems from IBRs' lack of short-circuit current sourcing capacity. As a result, traditional overcurrent protection functions (e.g., ANSI 51) are inadequate in such scenarios, and warrant alternative approaches. Time-domain protection, for example, shows promise in overcoming this challenge. In this paper we propose a pre-fault voltage discrimination (PVD) strategy whose role is to detect faults and discriminate normal switching and transformer inrush disturbances from actual faults. The use of PVD allows for the design of a simple, yet effective fault detection algorithm by using time-domain protection principles for distribution networks containing IBRs. The introduction of PVD provides for faster fault detection without reducing security and dependability. Offline simulation experiments and controller hardware-in-the-loop real-time simulation validate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm against various fault and normal switching events.

中文摘要翻译: 配电网中基于逆变器的资源（IBR）日益普及，给基于相量的过流保护带来了重大挑战。这一挑战源于IBR缺乏短路电流供给能力。因此，传统的过流保护功能（如ANSI 51）在此类场景中已不适用，需要替代方案。例如，时域保护在克服这一挑战方面展现出潜力。本文提出一种故障前电压辨识（PVD）策略，其作用是检测故障，并将正常的开关操作和变压器励磁涌流与真实故障区分开来。PVD的应用使得能够利用时域保护原理，为包含IBR的配电网设计一种简单而有效的故障检测算法。PVD的引入能够在不降低安全性和可靠性的前提下实现更快的故障检测。离线仿真实验和控制器硬件在环实时仿真验证了所提算法在各类故障和正常开关事件下的有效性。

</details>
